## v0.45 — Straight-line traction extra kept on medium-corner exits: `tc_slip_slide` 8 → 14 km/h

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.45 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.45`). `tc_slip_slide` 8 → 14 km/h (line 559): the sideways speed at which traction control's straight-line extra (`tc_slip_straight` 5.0 m/s, v0.43) is gone. The limit stays `tc_slip + tc_slip_straight·clip(1 − \|steer\|/tc_slip_steer, 0, 1)·clip(1 − \|speedY\|/tc_slip_slide, 0, 1)` (line 702): at 8 km/h sideways the extra was 0, now 43% of it; at 14 km/h and above it is still 0. Comment extended by one line (lines 698–699); everything from line 699 down moved by 1. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.44 first** (`runs/run_20261001_215050.csv`). (a) **Medium corners** run 7–9.5 km/h sideways as their normal cornering slip (e.g. the long left at ~400–515 m: 7–8 km/h the whole way, 8–9 km/h on the exit while the wheel straightens). So the v0.43 guard at 8 km/h removed the straight-line extra on every medium-corner exit, not only in real slides (v0.43's runaway case was 19 km/h). (b) **The flick limit is the approach, not the full-lock phase.** With the slowest wheel logged (debug trial), the flick approach (2,395–2,432 m) is a low-grip braking zone: wheels at 0.80–0.90 of car speed at pedal ~0.5 (0.91 lap average), ~22 m/s² there against ~25 at the same pedal elsewhere. With `brake_aero` 0.006 the car stays 17–22 km/h over the plan through the whole approach (pedal 1.0 asked, ABS halves it to 0.5 every step), turns in at ~122 km/h instead of 107 and understeers off at full lock. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "an enabling flick fix first, then `brake_aero`" (v0.44 Learned, plan rank 1): no full-lock or edge mechanism rescued 0.006 (below). (c) **Replay of the sharpness plan:** in the ~400 m corner and the flick approach the allowed speed is set by the "slow to `corner_speed` within the −7° beam (20–31 m) minus the 15 m margin" term, not by grip, which is why grip raises saturate; but relaxing that term only adds slides (below). **Candidates, closed-loop full laps with `run_race.py` on v0.44 (TORCS s; baseline reproduced at 80.588; flick `\|trackPos\|` in brackets):** (1) **New: slide-aware sharpness credit** (the plan's credit above the road-ahead plan fades with sideways speed, from s0 over w km/h): 4/8 → 80.560 (0.780), 6/8 → 80.460 (0.789), 8/8 → 80.572 (0.797), 6/12 → 80.508 (0.760), 8/12 → 80.572 (0.699, flick exit slide 22.5 → 9.4 km/h), 10/10 → 80.606 (0.720), 4/16 → 80.492 (0.762). (2) **Inherited follow-up: sharpness plan faded out** over the last F below `turn_steer_max` instead of switched off: F 0.1 → 80.678 (0.685), 0.2 → 80.512 (0.669), 0.3 → 80.850, 0.4 → 81.046. (3) **New: throttle limit while sliding** (8–12 km/h onset): 80.576–80.846 (0.82–0.85). (4) **New: friction-circle braking plan** (planned deceleration × (1 − k·\|steer\|)): k 0.3 → 81.086, 0.5 → 81.590, 0.8 → 82.216, 1.0 → 82.966, 1.5 → 85.492 (it also cuts the sharpness plan in every medium corner); with `brake_aero` 0.006 → 80.820–84.166 (flick 0.73–0.99). (5) **New: edge guard** (brake and cut the throttle when `trackPos` predicted 15–40 steps ahead passes 0.7–0.9 while heading outward): alone 80.634–80.818 (0.77–0.83); with `brake_aero` 0.006 **still off at the flick** (1.63–1.79). (6) **Braking knobs:** `brake_aero` 0.005 → 80.132 (0.774) but **0.0055 → off** (1.243 at ~2,480 m) and 0.006 → off (1.926): a cliff; `brake_margin` 10 → **off** (1.330); `brake_gain` 0.08 → 80.948, + `brake_aero` 0.005/0.006 → 80.768/80.768 (0.999); ABS ×0.7 instead of ×0.5 → 81.036; `corner_speed` 72 + `brake_aero` 0.006 → off, + 0.005 → 80.688 (0.910); a cap on the planned deceleration: 0.004 capped at 30/26 m/s² → 80.792/80.850, 0.006 at 24/26/28 → 80.156 (0.925)/80.018 (**1.084, off**)/off, 0.008 at 24/26 → 80.056 (**1.113, off**)/off, 0.01 at 24/26 → off/off. (7) **Sharpness plan's beam-end term relaxed:** margin 12/10/8/5 for the sharpness beams only → 80.418 (hairpin exit 0.92)/87.026 (**off at the hairpin exit**, 2.03)/off/off; the same only above \|steer\| 0.2/0.3 → 80.832–81.720 or off; beam-end speed 80/85 → 80.644/83.190 (**off**, 1.347). (8) Others: throttle memory over brake touches (keep 0.5–1.0 of the stored throttle) → 80.504–80.772; `tc_slip_straight` 6/7/8 → 80.556/80.564/80.672; `line_offset` 0.3/0.6/0.7/0.85 → 81.246/80.630/80.578/80.658; `line_gain` 0.3/0.8/1.2 → 81.138/80.680/86.180 (**off**, 1.815); short-shift when traction-limited → 80.742–81.508; `lock_throttle` 0.15/0.25/0.3 → 80.616/80.700/81.632 (**off**, 1.028); `upshift_rpm` 18,000/18,300 → 80.748/80.920; `tc_slip` 2.0/3.0/3.5 → 80.998/80.636/80.356 (flick slide 28.8 km/h); `tc_slip_steer` 0.5/1.0 → 80.942/80.496. (9) **Chosen: `tc_slip_slide`** 5 → 81.012, **8 → 80.588**, 10 → 80.468, 11 → 80.464, 12 → 80.340, 13 → 80.452, **14 → 80.208**, 16 → 80.124, 20 → 80.050; the largest slide outside the kink and flick grows 9.5 → 10.6 (12) → 11.4 (14) → 12.1 (16) → 13.9 km/h (20). **Robustness** (the orchestrator's point that one flick reading is a weak margin): 8 perturbations added on top of each candidate (`brake_aero` 0.005, `turn_grip_aero` 2e-4 and 1.25e-4, `turn_grip` 7.5, `line_offset` 0.6, `tc_slip_straight` 6, `corner_speed` 77, `brake_decel` 14.5): v0.44 itself **1 of 8 off** (`corner_speed` 77 → 1.012), the others 0.69–0.83; guard 12 → 0 off (0.75–0.96); **guard 14 → 0 off, flick 0.75–0.87 (mean 0.81), other slides ≤ 11.8 km/h, 79.83–80.59 s**; guard 16 → 0 off but flick up to 0.95 and other slides up to 13.5 km/h; slide-aware 8/12 → 0 off (0.70–0.88); slide-aware 6/12 → 1 off (with `brake_aero` 0.005); fade 0.2 → 0 off (0.68–0.82) but the ~790 m corner slides 13–14 km/h. (9) won: the largest gain that holds up, from one value, physically grounded in (a), with every neighbour from 10 to 20 faster than 8; 14 sits mid-plateau and stayed on track in every perturbation with the flick no wider than v0.44's. (1) and (2) make the flick a little safer but gain ≤ 0.13 s and unlock nothing (no braking knob became safe); (4)–(8) lose time or leave the track. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (closed-loop trial, see Why): 80.208 s. Risks: exit slides in medium corners (11.4 km/h at ~499 m in the trial, 9.5 in v0.44), the flick (0.84 in the trial). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:20.20 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 257 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 64 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261001_221927.csv`); TORCS time **80.208 s** → on screen 1:20:20. Lap **0.38 s faster** than v0.44 (1:20.58 → 1:20.20) — **the fastest lap so far**. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.837** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.44 0.852). Gains on medium-corner exits: ~500 m −0.20 s (exit of the long left at ~400–515 m, minimum 98 → 100 km/h), ~1,500 m −0.04, ~200/700/900/1,000/1,100/2,700/3,000/3,300 m −0.02 each; losses ~0 m +0.04, ~2,300 m +0.04 (kink, minimum 176 → 173 km/h), ~800/3,400 m +0.02 each. Top speed 257.4 km/h at ~1,366 m in 5th. Slowest corner 64 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.38 s); flick and hairpin unchanged, medium-corner exit slides 9.5 → 11.4 km/h |
| **Learned** | v0.43's slide guard sat at the sideways speed medium corners run at anyway (7–9.5 km/h), so it switched the straight-line traction extra off on every medium-corner exit; at 14 km/h it still stops real slides from feeding themselves, and the exits gain 0.38 s. The flick is limited by its approach, a low-grip braking zone (2,395–2,432 m: wheels at 80–90% of car speed at only ~0.5 pedal) that the braking plan cannot see coming: any plan that arrives there faster turns in too fast and understeers off at full lock, and neither a full-lock nor an edge guard rescues it, so `brake_aero` above ~0.005 needs a way to know the zone has less grip (or plan item H). The medium corners are bound by the sharpness plan's "slow to 75 within the beam" term, but they are also at their grip limit: raising their speed only adds slides. For the next agent (informational, on v0.45 as committed): + `brake_aero` 0.005 → **79.830** (flick 0.851), 0.0055 → 79.634 but **1.116, off track**, 0.006 → off; + slide-aware credit 8/12 → 80.284 (flick 0.810); + sharpness fade 0.2 → 80.366 (flick 0.719); `tc_slip_slide` 16 → 80.124 (flick 0.833, other slides 12.1 km/h). |

## v0.46 — Straights no longer capped at 250 km/h: `target_speed` 250 → 300

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.46 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.46`). `target_speed` 250 → 300 km/h (line 536; comment updated, no lines moved). 300 is above the car's top speed (~270 km/h in 5th on this track), so the throttle aim no longer caps any straight; every value from 270 up drives the identical lap. Everything else unchanged. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.45 first** (`runs/run_20261001_221927.csv`): time by limiter: full throttle 29.7 s, braking 17.3 s, traction-control cut 16.7 s, part throttle 10.7 s, riding the allowed speed 2.9 s, full lock 3.0 s. About 4 s of the part throttle was the 250 km/h cap: on the straights at ~1,320–1,410 m and ~2,200–2,300 m (and the final straight) the throttle fell 0.94 → 0.40 (−0.01 per step) at 249–257 km/h while the braking plan allowed 300–420 km/h. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "the car tops out at ~255 km/h, so 260+ changes nothing" (v0.41). The car was still accelerating at 250 with the throttle already easing (+2.4 km/h in 9 m); without the cap it reaches 269.4 km/h. Also re-checked: the v0.28 slip target, which was measured at low speed (a slip-ratio limit is slower, see (4)), and "`downshift_rpm` no gain" (confirmed, see (7)). **New finding:** the flick approach is a **crest**. Logging `speedZ` shows the car nearly airborne at ~2,364–2,378 m (vertical acceleration down to −17 m/s²), then −2 to −4 m/s² over 2,382–2,433 m. That is the low-grip braking zone found in v0.45 (less load on the tyres). **Candidates, closed-loop full laps with `run_race.py` on v0.45 (TORCS s; baseline reproduced at 80.208; flick `\|trackPos\|` in brackets):** (1) **New: crest-aware braking plan**: the plan's `brake_decel` scaled by the load factor `1 + gain·a_z/g` (a_z from `speedZ`, smoothed, 0.5–1). Gain 1: `brake_aero` 0.004 → 80.740 (kink slide 14.8 → 6.8 km/h), 0.005 → 80.304, 0.006 → 79.842 (0.749), 0.007 → **off at the hairpin exit** (1.669 at ~3,299 m). Gain 2: 81.414/80.630/79.786/off. Gain 3: 81.948/80.932/80.188 (0.960)/off. Load held with slow recovery (0.005–0.02 per step): 79.84–80.63, 0.007 still off. Perturbation suites (10 runs each, v0.45's 8 perturbations plus `target_speed` 300 and `lookahead_gain` 1.6): v0.45 0 of 10 off, mean 80.36. `brake_aero` 0.005 alone 2 of 10 off (`corner_speed` 77 → 1.233, `brake_decel` 14.5 → 1.666). Crest ×1 + 0.006: 2 off (`turn_grip` 7.5 → 1.650; `line_offset` 0.6 → 1.029 at ~2,431 m). Crest ×1 + 0.0055: 1 off (unperturbed, 1.016). Crest ×2 + 0.006: 1 off (`turn_grip_aero` 2e-4 → 1.213). Crest ×1 + 0.005: 0 off, mean 80.21, but the flick reached 0.967 and the unperturbed lap was 80.304. The crest plan halves the kink slide, but a gain needs a second change (`brake_aero`), the margin is no better than today's, and the hairpin becomes the limit at 0.007. (2) **New: allowed-speed persistence** (the max of the last N steps, to skip short brake touches on straights at ~1,236, ~2,303 and ~2,331 m, where the sharpness plan drops out for 2–8 steps): N 2 → 80.394; 3 → 80.128 (hairpin exit 0.83); 5 → 80.658 (**off**, 1.166 at the hairpin); 8/12 → **off** at the flick. (3) ABS threshold 0.75/0.85/0.9 (0.8 now) → 80.298/80.456/80.636. 0.85 + `brake_aero` 0.005 → 80.082 (**1.128, off**); with 0.006, every threshold left the track. (4) **New: slip-ratio cap on traction control** (limit ≤ max(1, k·v)): k 0.08/0.1/0.15/0.2/0.3 → 81.410/81.050/80.642/80.284/80.460; floor 2 m/s → 80.530/80.790. More absolute slip is better even at low speed. (5) **Braking grid** `brake_decel` 13–17 × `brake_aero` 0.003–0.005 (25 runs): fastest on track bd14/0.005 79.830 (0.851), bd17/0.003 79.986 (0.889), bd16/0.0035 80.014 (0.898). The car left the track whenever the planned deceleration at ~150 km/h exceeded ~22.8 m/s² (bd15/0.005, bd16/0.004+, bd17/0.0035+), which is the flick approach's measured ~22 m/s². (6) **New: look-ahead weighting.** In the long left at ~2,660–2,750 m the −19° beam toggles 69 ↔ 23 m (it grazes the inside edge), so `aim` flips −4.9 ↔ −2.2° and the steering 0.23 ↔ 0.04 every ~6 steps. Weight d^1/d^1.5/d^3 instead of d² → 81.298/80.678/80.312; weight capped at 50/70/100 m → 80.838/80.208/80.270; `aim` smoothing 0.3/0.5/0.7 → 80.414/80.568/80.702. (7) Old knobs re-tested on the current car: `lookahead_gain` 1.6/2.4/3.0 → 80.340/80.600/80.438; `max_steer_step` 0.1 → **off** (1.882), 0.3 → 80.256; `tc_gain` 0.3/0.8 → 80.104 (flick slide 24.2, exits 13.2 km/h)/80.740; `tc_hold` 0.7/0.9 → 80.340/80.680; centring 0.05/0.20 → 80.170/80.880; heading gain 12/18 → 80.534/80.664 (flick 0.59); `downshift_rpm` 12,500/14,500/15,000/15,500/16,000/16,500/17,000 → 80.426/80.572/80.546/80.576/80.674/81.148/81.906; `upshift_rpm` 18,650 → 80.532. (8) **New: proportional throttle near the allowed speed** (to remove the throttle sawtooth in medium corners, where the car rides the allowed speed with 0.01–0.05 brake touches that zero the throttle): 80.426–80.688. (9) **Chosen: `target_speed`** 255/260/265 → 80.158/80.168/80.146; 270 and up → 80.136 (the cap never binds). Perturbation suite: 0 of 10 off, flick ≤ 0.876. Every perturbation ran 0.05–0.15 s faster than the same perturbation on v0.45 (mean −0.10 s). It is also faster on top of each braking setting tried (`brake_aero` 0.005 79.830 → 79.610, crest ×1 + 0.006 79.842 → 79.652, crest ×1 + 0.0055 80.080 → 79.712). (9) won: one value, a physical reason, a small but consistent gain, and no watch point worse. (1) is the most promising mechanism found (it explains the flick cliff and halves the kink slide), but it does not pay alone. (2)–(8) were slower or left the track. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (closed-loop trial, see Why): 80.136 s. Risks: longer braking from higher speed into the corners after the long straights (the hairpin at ~3,280 m, the ~1,400 m corner, the kink). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:20.13 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 269 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 64 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261001_224832.csv`); TORCS time **80.136 s** → on screen 1:20:13. Lap **0.07 s faster** than v0.45 (1:20.20 → 1:20.13) — **the fastest lap so far**. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.828** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.45 0.837). Fastest point on each long straight: ~1,200–1,420 m 257.4 → 269.4 km/h, ~2,150–2,310 m 253.8 → 269.3, final straight 256.9 → 262.7. Top speed 269.4 km/h at ~1,396 m in 5th (6th still unused). Section times: ~2,200 m −0.04 s, ~1,300/1,400/1,800/2,600 m −0.02 each; ~1,500/2,700/3,200 m +0.02 each (longer braking from the higher speed). Part throttle 10.7 → 6.6 s, full throttle 29.7 → 33.0 s, braking 17.3 → 17.6 s (steps 830 → 845). **Watch points:** flick slide 20.9 → 20.1 km/h; kink (~2,385 m) 14.8 → 13.2 km/h; hairpin 64 km/h, exit −0.67 (unchanged); largest slide elsewhere 11.4 km/h (~498 m, unchanged). Slowest corner 64 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.07 s); watch points unchanged or slightly better; 0 of 10 perturbations off track |
| **Learned** | The 250 km/h throttle aim still capped the long straights: the car reaches ~269 km/h, and the cap had the throttle falling to 0.4 for ~100 m before each braking point. The gain is small (−0.07 s) because the car is near its top speed there and the extra speed must be braked away again, but it is consistent across every perturbation (−0.05 to −0.15 s). The flick approach is a crest: `speedZ` shows the car nearly airborne at ~2,370 m and lightly loaded (−2 to −4 m/s² vertically) through the 2,382–2,433 m braking zone. That is why every braking plan that assumes more than ~22.8 m/s² at ~150 km/h leaves the track there, whatever `brake_decel`/`brake_aero` mix produces it. A plan that scales the assumed grip by the measured vertical load is safe at the flick up to `brake_aero` 0.006 and halves the kink slide (14.8 → 7 km/h), but the hairpin exit then fails at 0.007 and the margin across perturbations is no better, so it is left for a later version. In medium corners the car rides the allowed speed with tiny brake touches, and the steering has an aim limit cycle at ~2,700 m (a grazing −19° beam). Smoothing either one was slower: the corners are limited by the sharpness plan's speed, not by how it is tracked. |

## v0.47 — Sharpness plan fades out with steering (0.5 → 0.75) instead of switching off at 0.6

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.47 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.47`). `turn_steer_max` 0.6 → 0.75 (line 552) and new knob `turn_steer_fade=.25` (line 553). The sharpness plan's credit above the road-ahead plan used to be all-or-nothing at \|steer\| 0.6; now it is collected as `sharp` and blended in: `allowed = road_plan + (sharp − road_plan)·clip((turn_steer_max − \|steer\|)/turn_steer_fade, 0, 1)` (line 656), i.e. full credit up to \|steer\| 0.5, falling linearly to none at 0.75. Comment on lines 639–644. Everything from line 553 down moved (see `batch.md`). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.46 first** (`runs/run_20261001_224832.csv`): time by limiter: full throttle 33.0 s, braking 17.6, traction-control cut 16.9, part throttle 6.6, riding the allowed speed 3.1, full lock 3.0. Two findings: (a) **the car hardly uses the track width**: max \|trackPos\| is 0.06–0.30 in most 100 m sections, line targets are only active once the bearing passes 2° (≤ 50 m before a corner), and in the corners the car sits 0.3–0.8 short of the target (the line term is a weak proportional pull against the look-ahead term). (b) **The lap is bimodal at ~513 m**: at the exit of the long left the −19° beam opens 80 → 160 m, the bearing jumps −9 → −14°, the steering spikes; if it crosses 0.6 the sharpness plan switches off, the allowed speed drops 117 → 98 km/h and the car brakes at 0.64 (v0.46 just stayed under, at 0.56). Many trials below showed +0.17–0.24 s at ~500 m from this alone, so v0.46's 80.136 is on the lucky side and single laps mislead. **Inherited conclusions challenged:** "sharpness fade: safer flick but ≤ −0.13 s, unlocks nothing" (v0.45) — confirmed for a fade ending at 0.6, but the switch point itself was too low; and "`turn_steer_max` must stay ≤ 0.6 to protect the flick" — a hard switch higher does fail at the flick, a fade does not. **All candidates closed-loop with `run_race.py` on v0.46 (TORCS s). Suite 1 = the v0.46 suite with `target_speed` 300 now in the base: unperturbed, `turn_grip_aero` 2e-4/1.25e-4, `turn_grip` 7.5, `line_offset` 0.6, `tc_slip_straight` 6, `corner_speed` 77, `brake_decel` 14.5, `lookahead_gain` 1.6, `brake_aero` 0.0045; v0.46: 0 of 10 off, mean 80.260, max \|trackPos\| 0.876.** (1) **New: early bend side from the longest beam** (pre-positioning to the outside on straights, per the user's line guidance; side from the mean bearing of the beams within 1 m of the longest, when no bend is active): threshold 0.5/1.0/1.7/2.5° → 80.218/80.236/80.206/80.432, with a "beam < 199 m" guard 80.376/80.362; 1.7° halves the flick slide (0.676, exit 5.7 km/h) unperturbed, but suite: 1.7° 2 of 10 off (`brake_decel` 14.5 → 1.051, `brake_aero` 0.0045 → 1.702 at the flick), 1.0° 3 off. The line term cannot hold the car out (it reached −0.34 of a −0.5 target in 100 m) and the flick approach then sits on the wrong side. (2) **New: outside line while braking** (phase +1 while speed > allowed + m): m 0/5/10 → 84.878/83.034/81.618; with (1) 83.964/82.508/81.754 or off. Staying out holds the car wide into every medium corner. (3) **New: launch slip** (extra traction-control limit k·clip(1 − v/v0)): k −2/2/4/8 at v0 60 km/h → 80.154/80.078/80.362/80.322, at 120 km/h → 80.464/80.520/80.618/80.338; the start section gains only 0.04–0.07 s. (4) **New: speed-dependent steering lock cap** (lock falls from 1 above v0 over w km/h, floor m): (70, 40, 0.5) → 83.658 (**off at the hairpin exit**, 1.379), (60, 60, 0.5) → off (1.367), (80, 40, 0.5) → 80.064, (90, 60, 0.5) → 80.190, (75, 25, 0.6) → 80.020, (100, 50, 0.4) → 80.432; flick 0.835–0.863, no safer: full lock is needed there. (5) **Inherited: crest-aware braking plan + `brake_aero`** (suite without the `brake_aero` perturbation, 9 runs): ×1 + 0.006 → mean 79.567 but 1 off (`line_offset` 0.6 → 1.193 at ~2,480 m); ×1.5 + 0.006 → 2 off; ×1 + 0.0055 → 0 off, mean 79.746, but max 0.999; load also scaling the aero term, ×1 + 0.006 → 2 off; 0.007 → hairpin exit off (1.55–1.74 at ~3,298 m) in most runs. The largest gain found (braking zones everywhere −0.04 to −0.09 s each), but it is two changes and its flick margin is ~0. (6) **Hard switch raised:** `turn_steer_max` 0.65/0.7 → mean 80.081/79.985, but `corner_speed` 77 off (1.027/1.019, flick exit slide 38–42 km/h). (7) **v0.45's fade re-tested** (ending at 0.6): width 0.1/0.2 → mean 80.214/80.299, 0 off. (8) **Fade window (end, width)** suites: (0.7, 0.1), (0.75, 0.15), (0.8, 0.2), (0.9, 0.3) → 1 off each (`corner_speed` 77, 1.004–1.025); (0.65, 0.1) → 80.289; (0.7, 0.15) → 80.208 (max 1.000); (0.7, 0.2) → 80.109; (0.75, 0.2) → 80.014 (max 0.960); (0.8, 0.25) → 80.081 (0.979); (0.85, 0.3) → 80.062 (0.985); (0.8, 0.3) → 80.014 (0.952); **(0.75, 0.25) → 80.029, 0 of 10 off, max 0.901**, every perturbation 79.82–80.19 (v0.46 79.98–80.49). **Suite 2** (new, other knobs: `brake_gain` 0.06, `tc_gain` 0.6, `line_gain` 0.6, `max_steer_step` 0.25, `upshift_rpm` 18,300, `corner_speed` 73, `brake_margin` 17/13, `line_offset` 0.4, `lock_throttle` 0.25): v0.46 mean 80.391 (max 0.958), (0.75, 0.25) 80.233 (0.940), (0.8, 0.3) 80.218 (0.903), all 0 off. **(8) at (0.75, 0.25) won:** one mechanism, −0.23/−0.16 s over two 10-run suites with 0 of 20 off and no wider flick than v0.46 across them; (0.8, 0.3) is equal within noise (kept in reserve). (5) gains more but needs two changes and has no flick margin; (1)–(4) and (6) are slower or leave the track. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (closed-loop trial, see Why): 80.088 s. Risks: the flick (more plan credit at \|steer\| 0.6–0.75; suite max 0.901–0.940) and medium-corner slides. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:20.08 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 269 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 64 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261001_234606.csv`); TORCS time **80.088 s** → on screen 1:20:08. Lap **0.05 s faster** than v0.46 (1:20.13 → 1:20.08) — **the fastest lap so far**; over the two perturbation suites −0.23 and −0.16 s. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.770** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.46 0.828). Section times: ~1,900 m −0.08 s (minimum 96.3 → 98.6 km/h in the corner after the ~1,800 m braking zone), all others within ±0.02. The ~513 m exit stays on the good branch (no brake). Traction-control cut 16.9 → 15.9 s, braking steps 845 → 846. **Watch points:** flick slide 20.1 → 18.3 km/h, exit 17.7 → 14.2; kink (~2,385 m) 13.2 → 13.3 km/h; hairpin 64.5 km/h, exit −0.69 (−0.67); largest slide elsewhere 11.5 km/h (~500 m; 11.4). | **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.05 s; −0.16 to −0.23 s over two perturbation suites, 0 of 20 off); flick narrower (0.828 → 0.770) |
| **Learned** | The sharpness plan's on/off switch at \|steer\| 0.6 was a trap both ways: below it a one-step steering spike at a corner exit (e.g. ~513 m, when a side beam suddenly opens) dropped the allowed speed by ~20 km/h and braked the car, which makes lap times bimodal by ~0.2 s; moving the switch up runs the flick wide. A linear fade from 0.5 to 0.75 keeps the credit in medium corners whose steering briefly passes 0.6 and still removes it near full lock; the gain is modest but holds over 20 perturbed runs. Single laps can mislead by ~0.2 s from that one exit, so compare suites. The racing line barely uses the track (\|trackPos\| ≤ 0.3 in most sections) because the line term is a weak pull that only acts within ~50 m of a corner; pre-positioning by the longest beam is detectable 100+ m out, but the current steering law can't hold the car out and it made the flick less safe. The crest-aware plan + `brake_aero` 0.0055–0.006 remains the biggest open gain (−0.5 to −0.7 s over suites) but its flick margin is ~0 at ~2,480 m. |

## v0.48 — Braking plan refitted to the measured deceleration: tyre load from crests, `brake_aero` 0.004 → 0.006, capped at 28 m/s²

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.48 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.48`). One mechanism, the deceleration curve the braking plan assumes, refitted to measured data: `brake_aero` 0.004 → 0.006 (line 539); new `brake_max=28` m/s² (line 540), the most deceleration ever planned; new `brake_load_min=.5` (line 541): the mechanical part `brake_decel` is scaled by the tyre load `clip(1 + a_z/g, 0.5, 1)`, with a_z from the change of `speedZ` per step, smoothed 0.7/0.3 (lines 629–632). `brake_speed(d)` (lines 633–639) uses the exp formula up to the distance where the curve meets `brake_max`, then a constant `brake_max` beyond it. Comment lines 619–627. Lines from 540 down moved +2, from the old 624 down +16 (see `batch.md`). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.47 first** (`runs/run_20261001_234606.csv`): time by limiter: full throttle 33.2 s, braking 17.6, traction-control cut 15.9, part throttle 6.8, riding the allowed speed 3.4, full lock 3.1. In every braking zone the car runs ~10–12 km/h over the allowed speed with the pedal at 0.5–0.65, and **the achieved deceleration (straight-line braking, pedal ≥ 0.3, flick excluded) is ~19 m/s² at 100 km/h, 24 at 140, 26 at 160, 27 at 200, 30 at 240** — the plan (14 + 0.004·v²) under-claims at 100–160 km/h (17.7–22.9) and over-claims above 220 (33–36.5). In both slow corners (the ~3,250 m hairpin and the Corkscrew left at ~2,440 m) the car turns in from the centre or the inside (+0.05, +0.28) and runs wide at full lock (to −0.69/−0.77). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "the car leaves the track whenever the plan at ~150 km/h exceeds ~22.8 m/s²" (v0.46). In a failing crest run (crest ×1 + `brake_aero` 0.006, with candidate (2) below) the car took the kink at 244 km/h (v0.47: 206) because the plan claimed ~42 m/s² there, then reached ~2,412 m 25–30 km/h above the allowed speed braking at full ABS (pedal 0.50) and ran off at 2,459 m; the mid-speed claim is not the only cause, the high-speed over-claim is. **Candidates, closed loop with `run_race.py` on v0.47 (TORCS s; v0.47 reproduced at 80.088). Suite 1 = v0.47's (unperturbed, `turn_grip_aero` 2e-4/1.25e-4, `turn_grip` 7.5, `line_offset` 0.6, `tc_slip_straight` 6, `corner_speed` 77, `brake_decel` 14.5, `lookahead_gain` 1.6, `brake_aero` +0.0005); suite 2 = v0.47's second (`brake_gain` 0.06, `tc_gain` 0.6, `line_gain` 0.6, `max_steer_step` 0.25, `upshift_rpm` 18,300, `corner_speed` 73, `brake_margin` 17/13, `line_offset` 0.4, `lock_throttle` 0.25); suite 3 (new) = `turn_steer_fade` 0.2/0.3, `turn_steer_max` 0.7, `line_aim_off` 0.8/1.2, `tc_slip` 2.3/2.7, `downshift_rpm` 13,000/14,000, `tc_hold` 0.85.** v0.47: suite 1 mean 80.029 (max 0.901), suite 2 80.233 (0.940), suite 3 80.088 (0.839). (1) **New: pre-positioning to the outside from the far wall** (when no bend is active and the longest beam is < 199 m, side from the bearing of the longest beams relative to the track direction, target 0.5/0.8 on the outside with its own pull): pull 0.5/1/2 → 80.384–89.156 or **off** (1.56–1.76 at the flick); turning the nose shortens the nose beams, so `ahead` collapses (false full brakes at ~1,330/1,348 m) and `aim` passes 2° (false bends), slides 33–52 km/h. With the pull capped at ±0.04/0.08/0.15 steer: 80.278/80.070/80.216, but the car only reached −0.36 of a −0.8 target by the hairpin turn-in (hairpin minimum 64.5 → 64.8 km/h). A useful line needs side/phase measured in the track frame, not the nose frame. (2) **New: no outside target on bend exits** (phase capped at 0/0.25/0.5, to stop the kink exit pulling the car to the inside of the Corkscrew): 80.178/80.106/80.300; with crest + 0.006 all three **off** at the Corkscrew. (3) **Inherited: crest ×1 + `brake_aero` 0.006** (no cap): suite 1 0/9 off, mean 79.297 (max 0.916); suite 2 **1 off** (`line_gain` 0.6 → 1.159 at ~2,480 m), 79.439. 0.0055: 1 off in each suite; 0.0065: 3 and 2 off; crest ×1.5 + 0.006: 0/9 (79.447) and 1 off (79.529). (4) **New: cap alone, `brake_aero` 0.006, no crest:** cap 26 → 79.616 (0.985), 28 → 79.488 (**off**, 1.125), 30/33 → **off** at the Corkscrew: the crest is still needed there. (5) **Chosen: crest + cap.** Single laps (0.006): cap 28 → 79.558 (0.758), 30 → 79.570 (0.932), 33 → 79.320 (1.069, off); load-scaled cap 30 → 79.648; cap 30 + 0.007 → 79.390. Suites (1/2/3, mean, max \|trackPos\|): **cap 28: 0/0/0 off, 79.520/79.656/79.582, 0.948/0.963/0.869**; cap 26: 0/0/0 off (two suite-2 runs only flagged because the log stopped at 3,608 m; real laps 80.027/80.028), 79.609/79.854/79.622, 0.856/0.977/0.815; cap 30: 0/1/1 off (`brake_margin` 13 → 1.039; `downshift_rpm` 13,000 → 1.022); cap 28 + 0.007: 3 and 2 off; cap 30 + 0.007: 2 and 3 off. **Cap 28 won:** −0.51/−0.58/−0.51 s against v0.47 over three 10-run suites with 0 of 30 off, it matches the measured ~27–30 m/s² at 200–240 km/h, and the flick is narrower unperturbed. Cap 26 is ~0.07 s slower with similar margins (kept in reserve); 30 fails 2 of 30. (1)–(2) were slower or left the track; (3) gains ~0.2 s more but leaves the track; (4) leaves the track. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (closed-loop trial, see Why): 79.558 s. Risks: the Corkscrew/flick (suite max 0.948–0.963 vs v0.47's 0.901–0.940), the hairpin exit (\|trackPos\| 0.69 → 0.76 unperturbed) and the kink. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |

Lap **0.53 s faster** than v0.47 (1:20.08 → 1:19.55) — **the fastest lap so far, the first under 1:20**. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.758** at ~3,281 m, the hairpin exit; flick 0.770 → 0.722). Section times: ~1,400 m −0.09 s, ~200/2,600 m −0.08 each, ~2,900 m −0.07, ~300/900/1,200/1,500/1,800 m −0.03 to −0.04 each; ~500 m +0.05, ~2,400 m +0.06 (the Corkscrew braking, now load-scaled). Achieved deceleration in straight-line braking: 26.5–29.2 m/s² at 140–200 km/h (v0.47: 23.8–27.1), pedal 0.56–0.61 (0.50–0.53). Top speed 273.8 km/h at ~1,396 m (v0.47 269.6). **Watch points:** flick slide 18.3 → 15.6 km/h, exit 14.2 → 10.3; kink (~2,385 m) 13.3 → 11.5 km/h; hairpin 64.3 km/h, exit −0.76 (−0.69); largest slide elsewhere 11.3 km/h (~499 m; 11.5). Slowest corner 64 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.53 s; −0.51 to −0.58 s over three perturbation suites, 0 of 30 off); flick narrower unperturbed (0.770 → 0.722) |
| **Learned** | The braking plan's deceleration curve was the wrong shape: the car brakes harder than planned at 100–160 km/h and less than planned above 220. Raising `brake_aero` alone fits the middle but over-claims 40+ m/s² at high speed, so the car reaches the Corkscrew braking zone too fast with no reserve; capping the plan at the measured ~28 m/s² and scaling the mechanical part by the crest's tyre load makes 0.006 safe over 30 perturbed runs. The cap sits close to the edge (30 left the track in 2 of 30 runs), so further braking gains need more grip information (load, slip), not a higher cap. Pre-positioning to the outside cannot be done with the current nose-frame beams: any heading big enough to move the car shortens `ahead` and fakes a bend. A line that uses the track width needs its side and phase measured along the track direction (e.g. `beam_at(track_dir ± x)`), so turning the nose does not change them. |

## v0.49 — Traction slide guard widened again: `tc_slip_slide` 14 → 20 km/h

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.49 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.49`). `tc_slip_slide` 14 → 20 km/h (line 562): the straight-line traction-control extra (`tc_slip_straight`) now fades out over 0–20 km/h of sideways speed instead of 0–14. Comment lines 723–725 updated (one line added; everything from the old line 725 down moved +1). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.48 first** (`runs/run_20261002_002155.csv`): time by limiter: full throttle 34.2 s, traction-control cut 17.0, braking 16.7, part throttle 6.2, full lock 3.5, riding the allowed speed 1.8. Findings: (a) in steady medium corners (~1,005–1,050 m, ~2,665–2,745 m) the allowed speed (~145 km/h) is set by the sharpness plan's 7° beam (~45 m), where the grip term and the "slow to `corner_speed` within the beam" term coincide; the car slides only 3–5 km/h there and rides the limit in a sawtooth (brake touch → stored throttle reset to 0 → +0.05/step climb). (b) The hairpin speed (64 km/h) is set by full lock + `lock_throttle` with the plan at its 75 km/h floor, not by the line. (c) The Corkscrew left is entered from the inside (+0.5, left over from the kink exit) and run out to −0.72 at full lock. (d) **Almost every speed-raising change fails at the flick (~2,477 m) or the hairpin exit (~3,298 m).** **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "brake dead band: no gain" (v0.42 trials) — re-tested because the car now rides the plan in steady corners; it gains on average but is not robust (below). **Candidates, closed loop with `run_race.py` on v0.48 (TORCS s; v0.48 reproduced at 79.558). Suites = the three 10-run suites of the v0.48 entry, v0.48: 79.520/79.656/79.582, max 0.948/0.963/0.869, 0 of 30 off.** (1) **New: sight-closing-rate plan** (the braking distance of every plan beam is divided by how fast the longest beam shrinks per metre travelled, smoothed 0.8, raw value taken when larger, floor m: in a constant-radius corner the end of the view recedes with the car, approaching a hairpin it does not): single laps m 0.8/0.75/0.7/0.65/0.6/0.5/0.33 → 79.148/79.718/79.296/79.152/79.984/90.454 (off at ~1,604 m)/104.892 (off); suites m 0.8: 2/1/1 off (all at the flick, 1.03–1.14), means 79.270/79.459/79.264; 0.7: 3/2/1 off; 0.65: 3/0/2 off. Fast, but the flick approach gets the relaxed distance too. (2) **New: track-frame pre-positioning** (bend side from beam pairs at track direction ±1.7–12°, corrected for the car's offset, outside target 0.5/0.8 with a capped pull 0.05–0.4, `ahead_angle_max` 3/6/10): 79.546–80.222 single laps, hairpin minimum unchanged (64.3–64.7 km/h): the room gained at the hairpin exit (0.76 → 0.67) is not turned into speed, because full lock and `lock_throttle` set the hairpin speed; the detection fires only ~25–80 m before a turn-in. (3) **Sharpness grip:** `turn_grip` 7.5/8/8.5/9/10 → 79.508/79.414/79.366/79.314/79.422 (10: flick 0.919); `turn_grip_aero` 2e-4/2.5e-4/3e-4 → 79.482/79.352/79.526 (3e-4 off at the flick). Suites: `turn_grip` 8.5: 0/1/0 off, 79.370/79.493/79.389; 9: 1/1/0 off; `turn_grip_aero` 2.5e-4: 1/1/0 off; all suite-2 offs at the hairpin exit (`brake_margin` 13, 1.48–1.60 at ~3,298 m). (4) **End speed:** `corner_speed` 78 → 79.618, 81/85/90 off at the flick; sharpness-plan-only end speed 80 → 79.634 (0.992), 85 → off at the hairpin, 90/100 off at the flick. (5) **Brake dead band** (no brake and no throttle reset until db km/h over the allowed speed): single laps 1.5 → 79.422, 3 → flick 1.007 off; suites db 1.0: 0/2/1 off; 1.5: 0/0/0 off, 79.365/79.570/79.307 but max 0.983/0.984/0.943; 2.0: 2/1/0 off — the 0 of 30 at 1.5 is luck between two failing neighbours. (6) Throttle ramp +0.08/0.1/0.15 per step → 79.536/79.514/79.512; `brake_gain` 0.04/0.07/0.09 → 79.578/79.966/80.160 (higher gain narrows the flick, 0.60–0.65, but the car loses the ~11 km/h it carries over the plan); ABS release ×0.3/×0.7 → 79.522/86.196 (off). (7) **Chosen: `tc_slip_slide`** (rank 4 of the open ideas, last measured on v0.45): single laps 16/18/20 → 79.436/79.550/79.444; suites (mean, max): 16: 0/0/0 off, 79.490/79.554/79.495 (0.890/0.961/0.841); 18: 0/0/0, 79.478/79.610/79.520; **20: 0/0/0, 79.471/79.505/79.450 (0.915/0.952/0.925)**; 25: 0/0/0, 79.459/79.570/79.446 (0.954/0.931/0.875); 30: 0/2/0 off (flick, 1.00–1.02). **20 won:** the best mean of the robust settings (−0.05/−0.15/−0.13 s, 0 of 30 off) with flick margins no worse than v0.48 in suites 1–2. (1), (3), (4) and (5) gain more on average but leave the track in 1–6 of 30 runs; (2) and (6) are slower. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (closed-loop trial, see Why): 79.444 s. Risks: exit slides in medium corners (the extra now stays on up to 20 km/h sideways) and the flick exit. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:19.44 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 270 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 64 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_010817.csv`); TORCS time **79.444 s** → on screen 1:19:44. Lap **0.11 s faster** than v0.48 (1:19.55 → 1:19.44) — **the fastest lap so far**; over the three perturbation suites −0.05/−0.15/−0.13 s, 0 of 30 off. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.781** at ~3,281 m, the hairpin exit; v0.48 0.758). Section times: start (0–100 m) −0.11 s, ~500 m −0.05; ~200 m +0.03, ~1,200 m +0.04; all others within ±0.02. Traction-control cut 17.0 → 15.5 s (steps 857 → 785); braking 16.7 s (steps 804 → 806). | **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.11 s; −0.05 to −0.15 s over three perturbation suites, 0 of 30 off) |
| **Learned** | The straight-line traction extra still paid when allowed through larger exit slides (14 → 20 km/h): medium-corner exits run at 9–14 km/h sideways, and the cut time fell 1.5 s; 30 starts to fail at the flick (2 of 30, 1.00–1.02). The steady medium corners are held at ~145 km/h by the "slow to `corner_speed` within the beam" rule, not by grip (3–5 km/h sideways), but every way found to relax it (closing rate, higher end speed, more grip, dead band) runs the flick or the hairpin exit wide in some perturbations. The flick (entered from the inside after the kink) and the hairpin exit (full lock, `lock_throttle`) are now the shared limit of nearly all speed gains: a safety margin there is worth more than any single knob. Pre-positioning alone does not help the hairpin: the room it frees must also be turned into speed (lock throttle / exit plan), which is a second mechanism. |

## v0.50 — Full-lock throttle falls as the outside edge comes closer (enabling change: flick and hairpin margin)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.50 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.50`). One mechanism, the full-lock throttle limit now depends on the room to the outside edge: new knobs `lock_throttle_edge=.1` (line 565), `lock_room_near=.3` (line 566), `lock_room_far=.8` (line 567); `lock_throttle` (0.2, line 564) is now the limit with ≥ 0.8 trackPos units of room. room = 1 + sign(steer)·trackPos (outside = right in a left turn); the full-lock limit is lock_throttle_edge + (lock_throttle − lock_throttle_edge)·clip((room − 0.3)/0.5, 0, 1) (lines 705–711, comment 705–708). Lines from 565 down moved +3, from the old 703 down +9 (see `batch.md`). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.49 first** (`runs/run_20261002_010817.csv`): in both slow corners the car sits at full lock with the throttle at the 0.2 limit while the arc drifts outward (hairpin ~3,254–3,283 m: trackPos +0.10 → −0.78, speed 86 → 64 km/h; Corkscrew left ~2,443–2,472 m: +0.21 → −0.66, then the flick at ~2,476 m with 16 km/h sideways); the steady medium corners ride the plan in a brake/throttle sawtooth (each brake touch zeroes the stored throttle, which climbs back at +0.05/step). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "the room freed at the hairpin exit must be turned into speed via `lock_throttle`" (v0.49 Learned). Measured the other way round: the full-lock phase is grip-limited, more throttle there only runs wide, and the useful lever is less throttle near the edge, which buys margin for speed elsewhere. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py --variant`; v0.49: 79.471/79.505/79.450, max 0.915/0.952/0.925, 0 of 30 off).** (1) **New: room-scaled lock throttle raised** (full-lock limit 0.3/0.4/0.5 with room ≥ 0.8, 0.2 at ≤ 0.3): suite 1 5/10/10 of 10 **off** (flick and hairpin exit, max 1.25–1.53); 0.4 with room / 0.1 near the edge: 5 of 10 off. (2) **New: lift band** (up to b km/h over the allowed speed: throttle 0 but the stored throttle kept and no brake; brake beyond b): suite 1 b 1/2/3/5 → mean **79.223/79.113/79.147/79.168** but 1/1/3/9 of 10 off (`corner_speed` +2 at the flick, 1.02–1.58); band faded in below 100 km/h (full from 130): 0 of 30 off, **79.220/79.377/79.172** but max **0.996/1.000**/0.927 — no margin; faded at 120 km/h: 2 of 10 off; faded with \|steer\| (0.3/0.5): 1–2 of 10 off; b 3 faded at 100 km/h: 4 of 10 off. (3) **Chosen: edge-reduced lock throttle** (0.2 → 0.1 as the room falls from 0.8 to 0.3): full suites 0 of 30 off, **79.535/79.524/79.523, max 0.843/0.849/0.812**; variants (all 0 of 30 off, mean / max): edge 0.05 → 79.549/0.808; 0.15 → 79.489/0.961; room 0.2–0.6 → 79.505/0.893; 0.4–1.0 → 79.523/0.820; 0.1–1.2 → 79.536/0.819. 0.1 with 0.3–0.8 is the best margin-for-time trade. **Follow-up measured (not in this version): (3) + lift band 2 km/h faded in below 100 km/h → 0 of 30 off, 79.244/79.398/79.250 (−0.23/−0.11/−0.20 s against v0.49), max 0.871/0.854/0.822**; (3) + lift band 2 without fade → 0 of 30 off, 79.123/79.245/79.171, max 0.935/0.979/0.859. Cited, not re-run: `brake_aero` 0.0064 / `brake_max` 27 (tools demo) mean 79.470, max 0.962, no gain. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed 79.454 s (+0.01 s), suite means +0.02 to +0.07 s, suite max \|trackPos\| −0.07 to −0.11. Kept as an enabling change if the margin holds. |
| **Lap time** | 1:19.45 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 270 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_020537.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **79.454 s** → on screen 1:19:45. Lap **0.01 s slower** than v0.49 (1:19.44 → 1:19.45); suites **79.535/79.524/79.523** (v0.49 79.471/79.505/79.450), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.843/0.849/0.812** (v0.49 0.915/0.952/0.925). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.705** at ~3,279 m, the hairpin exit; v0.49 0.781). Section times: ~2,500 m +0.04 s, ~3,000/3,200/3,600 m +0.02 each, ~3,100/3,300 m −0.02 each, others within ±0.02. **Watch points:** flick 0.707 → **0.670**, slide 16.4 → 12.8 km/h, exit 11.7 → **6.3**; kink 11.7 km/h (unchanged); hairpin minimum 64.4 → 60.1 km/h, exit −0.78 → **−0.70**, slide 3.6 → 2.2; largest slide elsewhere 13.9 km/h (~501 m, unchanged). Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,282 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — enabling change: +0.01 s unperturbed (+0.02 to +0.07 s over three suites), but the flick and hairpin-exit margin grows (suite max 0.915/0.952/0.925 → 0.843/0.849/0.812, flick exit slide 11.7 → 6.3 km/h); on top of it the lift band (2 km/h, faded in below 100 km/h) measured 0 of 30 off at −0.11 to −0.23 s against v0.49, while alone it reaches max \|trackPos\| 1.000 |
| **Learned** | The full-lock phases (hairpin, Corkscrew left before the flick) are grip-limited: the car scrubs speed at 0.2 throttle and drifts outward, and any extra throttle there (0.3–0.5 with room) leaves the track in 5–10 of 10 runs, so turning the hairpin room into speed via `lock_throttle` does not work. Cutting the full-lock throttle to 0.1 as the outside edge nears costs ~0.05 s but buys ~0.1 of trackPos at the flick and the hairpin exit in every suite — the shared limit of the speed gains in v0.48–v0.49. The brake/throttle sawtooth in steady corners is worth ~0.2–0.35 s: lifting (stored throttle kept) instead of braking for the first 1–2 km/h over the plan is the next step, now with margin. |

## v0.51 — Lift band: up to 1% over the allowed speed the car lifts instead of braking

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.51 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.51`). One mechanism, the lift band: new knobs `lift_pct=1.0` (line 568), `lift_v0=86` (line 569), `lift_vw=57` (line 570). band = `lift_pct`/100 · speed · clip((speed − `lift_v0`)/`lift_vw`, 0, 1) km/h (line 698). Up to the band over `allowed_speed` the car only lifts: brake 0, throttle sent 0, stored throttle (`c.throttle`) kept (the −0.01 step is undone, lines 704–706; throttle zeroed after traction control, line 757). Beyond the band it brakes for the excess over the band, `(speed − allowed − band)·brake_gain` (line 702). Full band (~1.4 km/h at 143 km/h, ~2.5 at 250) from 143 km/h, none below 86 km/h (hairpin and Corkscrew brake at once). Comment lines 690–697. Old lines 568–686 moved +3, old 691–740 +16, old 741 onward +17 (see `batch.md`). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.50 first** (`runs/run_20261002_020537.csv`): time by limiter: full throttle 31.6 s, braking 17.1 (45 separate brake applications), traction-control cut 13.4, part throttle 12.1, full lock 3.8, riding the allowed speed 2.4. Most part-throttle time is the climb back after a brake touch: in the steady medium corner at ~2,670–2,750 m the car brakes 0.01–0.09 for one step whenever it is 0.5–1 km/h over the plan (which itself swings 142–148 km/h with a steering wiggle of 0.03 ↔ 0.25 every ~6 steps), the stored throttle goes to 0 and climbs back at +0.05/step, so speed saws 141–144 km/h. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "brake dead band: no gain / not robust" (v0.42 trials, v0.49: 1.5 km/h gave 0 of 30 but max 0.98, 1.0 and 2.0 left the track). With v0.50's lock margin and the band faded out at low speed it holds (below). Also re-checked the "untested hypotheses" of the outside review on v0.50 rather than taking them as gains. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py --variant`, full 3×10 suites; v0.50: 79.535/79.524/79.523, all-30 mean 79.527, max 0.849, 0 of 30 off).** (1) **New: allowed-speed decay** (the allowed speed may fall at most f km/h per step while it stays within h of the raw plan, so short plan dips do not brake): f 0.5/h 3 → mean 79.316, max **0.996**; f 1/h 3 → 79.386, max 0.950; f 1/h 2 → 79.429, 0.930; f 0.5/h 5 → **1 of 30 off** (1.179). Slower than the lift band at a worse margin, because it also lets the throttle climb above the plan. (2) **New: steering low-pass** (against the 0.03 ↔ 0.25 wiggle; share of the new value 0.8/0.6): 79.601 (0.877) / 79.692 with **1 of 30 off**; 0.8 on top of the lift band: 79.404 (0.871), slower than the band alone. (3) **Inherited untested hypotheses, alone on v0.50:** `corner_speed` 77 → 79.426 but max **0.995** (flick exit slide 21 km/h); `tc_slip_straight` 6 → 79.513 (0.909); `brake_gain` 0.055 → 79.567 (0.874); `lock_steer` 0.55 → 79.520 (0.915): none gains enough. (4) **Lift band, the planned candidate** (fixed width b km/h, faded in from v0 over 30 km/h; all 0 of 30 off): b 2/v0 100 (v0.50 agent's) → **79.299** (79.244/79.402/79.250, max 0.871), reproduced; b 1.5 → 79.262 (0.906); 2.5 → 79.343 (0.971); 3 → 79.321 (0.984); v0 80/90/110/120 → 79.275 (0.965)/79.316 (0.980)/79.236 (0.878)/79.312 (0.902). **New form: width as a share of speed** (1% → 1.4 km/h at 140, 2.5 at 250, so the medium corners get a small band and the fast braking zones a larger one): 1%/v0 100 → 79.232 (0.897); 1.5% → 79.239 (0.982); 1 km/h + 1% → 79.262 (0.983). (5) **Chosen, co-tuned with `tools/opt.py`** (`lift_band` 0–3 km/h, `lift_pct` 0–2, `lift_v0` 80–130, `lift_vw` 10–60; 45 trials on suite 1, top 5 confirmed on all 30): all top 5 use the speed share alone (fixed part 0, 1%); best **1%, v0 86, vw 57 → 79.197/79.256/79.212 (all 79.222), max 0.848**; 1%/89/48 → 79.222 (0.893); 1%/87/58 → 79.237 (0.886); 2 km/h/108/24 → 79.254 (0.945). Neighbours (all 0 of 30 off): 1%/85/55 → 79.255 (0.927); 1%/85/60 → 79.281 (0.951); 1%/90/50 → 79.254 (0.940); 1.2%/85/55 → 79.212 (0.957); 0.8%/85/55 → 79.251 (0.886). 1%/86/57 won: best mean and the best margin of all lift settings; the neighbourhood spread (79.21–79.28, max 0.85–0.96) is the chaos of the lap, so the gain (~−0.25 to −0.3 s) is real but the exact point is not special. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`/`opt.py`, see Why): unperturbed 79.04 s, suites 79.197/79.256/79.212, max \|trackPos\| 0.848. Risks: braking zones start ~1–2.5 km/h later (flick approach, hairpin entry above 86 km/h). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:19.04 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 274 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_024553.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **79.044 s** → on screen 1:19:04. Lap **0.41 s faster** than v0.50 (1:19.45 → 1:19.04) and **0.40 s faster than v0.49** (1:19.44) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **79.197/79.256/79.212** (v0.50 79.535/79.524/79.523; v0.49 79.471/79.505/79.450), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.845/0.848/0.816** (v0.50 0.843/0.849/0.812). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.766** at ~3,278 m, the hairpin exit; v0.50 0.705). Brake applications 45 → 34, braking time 17.1 → 15.8 s, part throttle 12.1 → 9.6 s. Section times: ~2,700 m −0.08 s; ~1,000/1,200/1,300/3,000 m −0.044 each; ~2,300/2,500 m −0.04 each; ~2,900 m −0.036; ~400/1,700/2,200/2,400/3,100/3,400 m +0.02 each; others within ±0.02. Top speed 274.3 km/h (v0.50 270). **Watch points:** flick 0.670 → 0.706, slide 12.8 → 14.4 km/h, exit 6.3 → 7.9; kink 11.7 → 11.6 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.1 → 59.6 km/h, exit −0.70 → −0.77, slide 2.2 → 1.9; largest slide elsewhere 13.9 → 14.2 km/h (~501 m). Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,281 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.41 s vs v0.50, −0.40 s vs v0.49; −0.27/−0.25/−0.24 s over three perturbation suites vs v0.49, 0 of 30 off, suite margin unchanged from v0.50) |
| **Learned** | **The follow-up recovered v0.50's enabling cost and more:** v0.49 79.444 single lap / 79.471/79.505/79.450 suites → v0.51 79.044 / 79.197/79.256/79.212, with v0.50's margin kept (suite max 0.848 vs v0.49's 0.952); without v0.50's lock margin the same band reached max 1.000 (v0.50 trials), so v0.50 + v0.51 together are the gain. The brake/throttle sawtooth in steady corners cost ~0.3 s: one-step brake touches 0.5–1 km/h over the plan reset the stored throttle. A band proportional to speed (1%) beats a fixed width: the medium corners need only ~1.4 km/h, the fast braking zones tolerate ~2.5. The band must fade out below ~86–143 km/h (slow corners brake at once) or the flick/hairpin margin goes. Easing the plan itself (allowed-speed decay) or smoothing the steering does not do the same: both let the car run over the plan with throttle and lose margin. The allowed speed swings ±3 km/h with a ~6-step steering wiggle in medium corners (~2,670–2,750 m); that wiggle is a remaining open question. |

## v0.52 — Apex line: a tighter inside line in medium bends, faded out near full lock

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.52 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.52`). One mechanism, the inside (apex) part of the racing line: new knobs `line_apex=.34` (line 548), `apex_steer=.3` (line 549), `apex_steer_fade=.25` (line 550); `line_offset` 0.5 → **0.47** (line 545, co-tuned). Near the apex (phase < 0) the line target is (`line_offset` + `line_apex`·clip((`apex_steer` + `apex_steer_fade` − \|prev steer\|)/`apex_steer_fade`, 0, 1))·phase·side, so the inside target is 0.81 while \|steer\| ≤ 0.3, falling to 0.47 at \|steer\| ≥ 0.55 (hairpin, Corkscrew/flick); the outside part (phase > 0) stays `line_offset` (lines 614–623, comment 614–619). Old lines 548–610 moved +3, old 612 onward +12 (see `batch.md`). |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.51 first** (`runs/run_20261002_024553.csv`): the car sits within \|trackPos\| ≤ 0.3 in almost every section and 0.3 short of an inside target of 0.5 in the medium bends (e.g. ~2,670–2,730 m: target +0.50, car +0.15–0.20); the medium bends ride a plan that is limited by braking-to-`corner_speed` within the beam lengths, not by grip (steer ~0.2, sideways 3–6 km/h). **The steering wiggle (open question from v0.51) is a beam flip:** the −19° beam (`track1`) jumps 68.9 ↔ 25.4 m as the nose rotates ~0.1° across the inside edge's apex, the d²-weighted `aim` jumps −4.9° ↔ −2.2° and the steering 0.15 ↔ 0.04 (~0.11), which rotates the nose back: a limit cycle every ~10–20 steps. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "`line_offset` 0.55–0.6 is only useful after the stronger tracking term" (outside review) and "outside line / wider line costs time at the flick": on v0.51, `line_offset` 0.6 alone was **79.054** all-30 mean (−0.17 s) but 1 of 30 off (0.7 at the flick approach, 1.054 at ~2,432 m: the outside pre-position for the Corkscrew left fights the exit of the right kink at full lock under braking); splitting the offset showed the gain is all on the inside. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, full 3×10 suites; v0.51: 79.222 all-30 mean, max 0.848, 0 of 30 off).** (1) **Inherited knobs re-tested on v0.51:** `corner_speed` 77 → 79.185, max **0.992**; `tc_slip_straight` 6 → 79.247 (0.895); `brake_gain` 0.055 → 79.347; `lock_steer` 0.55 → 79.248; `turn_grip` 7.5 → 1 off (1.597); `turn_steer_max` 0.8 → 79.178 (0.883); `line_gain` 0.7 → **11 of 30 off** (oscillation). (2) **New: line-pull guards for a wider line** (line_offset 0.6/0.7): line term faded with lock → 2/27 of 30 off; capped at ±0.1 steer → 80.126/80.144 (0 off, slow); faded while braking → 80.51/80.63, 1/4 off. (3) **New: separate inside offset** (`line_offset` 0.5 outside): inside 0.6 → 79.008 (0.933); **0.7 → 78.857** (0.947); 0.8 → 78.729 but 1 off; outside 0.6/0.7 with inside 0.5 → 5/7 of 30 off (the outside part is what breaks the flick). As an extra on top of `line_offset` (so the suite's `line_offset` ±0.1 moves both): +0.15 → 78.924 (0.948), +0.2/+0.25 → 1 off (flick approach, `line_offset` +0.1). (4) **Chosen: the extra faded out with \|steer\|** (at full lock a tighter target only asks for more lock): +0.2 fade 0.4–0.7 → 78.791 (0.944); +0.3 0.4–0.7 → 78.569 (0.987); +0.3 0.3–0.6 → 1 off; +0.4 0.3–0.6 → 78.455 (0.995). Rounded neighbourhood (all 30, all 0 off unless noted): **0.34/0.30/0.25/0.47 → 78.596 (0.944)**; apex 0.30 → 78.617 (0.970); 0.39 → 78.454 (0.992); 0.40 → 78.454 (0.995); `line_offset` 0.45 → 78.614 (0.994); 0.50 → 1 off (`line_offset` +0.1 → 0.6 at the flick approach); `apex_steer` 0.25/0.35 → 78.622 (0.946)/78.605 (0.948); 0.34/0.29/0.26 → 78.596 (0.961); 0.39/0.29/0.26 → 78.428 (0.947). 0.34/0.30/0.25/0.47 has the best margin of the neighbourhood; the spread (78.43–78.62) is the lap's chaos. (5) **New, measured for a later version (separate mechanism): rise-limited beams for `aim`** (each beam may grow at most r m per step for the look-ahead bearing; falls at once) against the wiggle, on top of the chosen line: r 1/3/8 → 78.641 (**0.781**)/78.556 (0.862)/78.552 (0.810), 0 of 30 off. |

Lap **0.49 s faster** than v0.51 (1:19.04 → 1:18.55) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **78.598/78.624/78.565** (v0.51 79.197/79.256/79.212: −0.60/−0.63/−0.65 s), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.915/0.944/0.852** (v0.51 0.845/0.848/0.816; all suite maxima at the flick, ~2,472–2,475 m; worst `brake_margin` −2, 0.944). Top speed 271.9 km/h (v0.51 274). **Watch points:** flick 0.706 → **0.816**, slide 14.4 → 13.5 km/h, exit 7.9 → 7.6; kink 11.6 → 9.6 km/h; hairpin minimum 59.6 → 60.3 km/h, exit −0.77 → −0.71, slide 1.9 → 1.9; largest slide elsewhere 14.2 (~501 m) → 12.6 km/h (~1,540 m). | **Learned** | The racing line's gain is on the **inside**: a tighter apex target in medium bends (0.81 instead of 0.5) is worth ~0.6 s, while a wider **outside** target (0.6–0.7) breaks the flick (its pre-position for the Corkscrew left fights the right-kink exit at full lock under braking on the crest). Near full lock a tighter target only asks for more lock, so the apex extra must fade out with steering (from 0.3 to 0.55). The car still reaches only ~0.3 of the target, so tracking remains the limit; a stronger pull still oscillates (`line_gain` 0.7: 11 of 30 off). The steering wiggle in medium bends is a beam flip, not noise: the −19° beam crosses the inside edge's apex and the d²-weighted `aim` jumps ~2.7°; rate-limiting how fast a beam may grow for `aim` (1–8 m/step) gave max \|trackPos\| 0.78–0.86 at equal speed on top of this line — the next margin source, and with it a larger apex or `corner_speed`. |

## v0.53 — Inside line integral: the car now reaches the inside line in medium bends

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.53 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.53`). One mechanism, an integral term for the racing line's inside half: new knobs `line_ki=1.5` (line 551), `line_imax=.4` (552), `line_isteer=.4` (553), `line_ifade=.25` (554), `line_idecay=.85` (555); code at lines 630–645. On the inside half of a bend (phase < 0) the line error (`line_target` − `trackPos`) is integrated, `c.line_i += error·0.021·line_ki·f`, with f = clip((`line_isteer` + `line_ifade` − \|prev steer\|)/`line_ifade`, 0, 1) (full below \|steer\| 0.4, none from 0.65: hairpin, flick), clipped to ±`line_imax` steer and added to the steering before the rate limit. Wherever f < 1 (outside half, no bend, near full lock) the integral fades by `line_idecay` per step (at f = 0: ×0.85). No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.52 first** (`runs/run_20261002_031223.csv`): in the long left bend at ~2,655–2,760 m the car held trackPos **0.23 for 100 m against an inside target of 0.81** at 146–150 km/h; the same at ~400–520 m (−0.2…−0.36 vs 0.5–0.8) and ~1,480–1,590 m (0.15–0.21 vs 0.81). The steady error has a cause: the heading term `angle·15/PI` acts on the **body** angle, which in a steady bend equals the slip angle (angle −0.035 rad ≈ vy/vx = −5/147), so it pushes back with −0.17 steer against the line term's +0.29. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`; v0.52: all-30 78.596, max 0.944, 0 of 30 off).** (1) **Own, new: slip-compensated heading term** (heading from the velocity, `angle − k·clip(vy/vx, ±cap)`): suite 1 k 1/cap 0.05 → 78.403 (max 0.842); all-30 k 0.75–1.5 × cap 0.03–0.07 → 78.43–79.71, 0–2 of 30 off (off at the flick approach ~2,433 m with `line_gain` +0.1); best k 1.5/cap 0.03 78.427 (0.859), k 1/cap 0.05 78.497 (0.842). Lost: ~−0.1–0.17 s and fragile. (2) **Own, new: speed-scaled traction limit** (straight extra × clip(v/v0, 0, 1), i.e. less over-speed at slow exits; the hairpin exit runs ~9 m/s over-speed at 60–130 km/h): suite 1 v0 80/120/160/220 km/h → 78.514/78.550/78.653/78.912: less slip at low speed is not faster. **Inherited conclusion re-checked:** v0.46's "slip-ratio traction control gives nothing" still holds; `tc_slip_straight` 6/7/9 → 78.590/78.588/78.472 (suite 1, max 0.95–0.97), noise. (3) **Plan rank 1: beam rise-rate limit for `aim`**, re-measured on v0.52 with the plan's spend-ups (all 30, all 0 off): r 3/5/8 alone 78.556/78.617/78.552 (max 0.862/0.813/0.810); + `line_apex` 0.40 78.427–78.483 (0.83–0.87); + `corner_speed` 77 78.49–78.56; both 78.38–78.43 (0.91–0.94). Lost: at best −0.2 s, and it needs a second mechanism to spend its margin. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "a stronger tracking term oscillates" (v0.52: `line_gain` 0.7 → 11 of 30 off) is true for more **proportional** gain, but the error is a steady offset, which an integral removes without raising the loop gain. (4) **Chosen, own, new: line integral.** Plain integral over the whole bend (suite 1): k 0.5/max 0.1 → 78.334 (0 off), k 1/max 0.2 → 78.052 (1 off), others 3–8 of 10 off, all at the flick approach (~2,432 m, left edge) or exit (~2,482 m). Faded with \|steer\| (all 30, k 0.75–1.5, max 0.3–0.4, fade from 0.45/0.55): **77.62–78.35 but 1–10 of 30 off, every one at the flick** (2,431–2,433 or 2,477–2,482 m): the integral built up in the right kink's outside phase was carried into the flick approach. Faster decay alone (0.7/0.5/0): 77.41–77.49 but 2–9 of 30 off. **Integrating only on the inside half (phase < 0) fixed it:** k 1 → 77.465 (max **0.851**, 0 off), + decay 0.7 → 77.381 (0.916), k 2 → 77.222 (0.872). Co-tuned with `tools/opt.py` (study `li53`: `line_ki` 1–5 (integers only, by the range syntax), `line_imax` 0.2–0.7, `line_isteer` 0.3–0.7, `line_ifade` 0.1–0.5, `line_idecay` 0.5–0.97; 60 trials on suite 1 with soft limit 0.9, top 6 confirmed on all 30: 77.170–77.355, max 0.840–0.889, all 0 off). Rounded neighbourhood (all 30, `line_isteer` 0.4, `line_idecay` 0.85, all 0 off): k 2/max 0.4/fade 0.25 → 77.312 (0.841); 2/0.45/0.2 → 77.232 (0.897); **1.5/0.4/0.25 → 77.263 (0.862)**; 2.5/0.4/0.25 → 77.536 (0.870); 3/0.4/0.25 → 77.344 (0.828); 2/0.4/0.2 → 77.248 (0.894). 1.5/0.4/0.4/0.25/0.85 chosen: among the fastest, a gentler gain, and the lowest flick maximum of the fast group in the unperturbed lap (0.651). |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed ~77.18 s (−1.37 s), suites ~77.21/77.32/77.27, max \|trackPos\| ~0.86. Risk: none new at the flick (suite margin 0.944 → 0.862). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:17.18 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 62 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_124759.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **77.184 s** → on screen 1:17:18. Lap **1.37 s faster** than v0.52 (1:18.55 → 1:17.18) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **77.206/77.316/77.267** (v0.52 78.598/78.624/78.565: −1.39/−1.31/−1.30 s), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.833/0.862/0.779** (v0.52 0.915/0.944/0.852). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.651** at ~2,476 m, the flick; v0.52 0.816). The line now holds better: ~2,660–2,760 m mean trackPos 0.23 → 0.38 (max 0.50), mean speed 148 → 157 km/h; ~1,480–1,590 m 0.11 → 0.20, 139 → 142 km/h; ~400–520 m −0.21 → −0.07, 104 → 107 km/h; mean distance to the inside target (\|target\| > 0.5) 0.69 → 0.53. Section times: ~2,700 m −0.20 s, ~200 m −0.20, ~1,000 m −0.20, ~400 m −0.18, ~1,900 m −0.16, ~800 m +0.15. Top speed 275.2 km/h (v0.52 271.9). **Watch points:** flick 0.816 → **0.651**, slide 13.5 → 10.0 km/h, exit 7.6 → 8.0; kink 9.6 → 10.5 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.3 → 61.5 km/h, exit −0.71 → −0.65, slide 1.9 → 1.8; largest slide elsewhere 12.6 (~1,540 m) → 13.6 km/h (~227 m). Slowest corner 62 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−1.37 s vs v0.52; −1.39/−1.31/−1.30 s over three perturbation suites, 0 of 30 off), with more flick margin (suite max 0.944 → 0.862) |
| **Learned** | The car missed the inside line because of a **steady offset, not a weak gain**: in a steady bend the body is yawed by the slip angle, and the heading term (on the body angle) cancels about half of the line pull. An integral removes the offset without the oscillation that more proportional gain causes, and is worth ~1.3 s. It must be confined to the inside half of a bend: an integral carried from the right kink's outside phase into the flick approach left the track in up to 10 of 30 runs; inside-only it made the flick **safer** (0.94 → 0.86). The speed-scaled traction limit and the slip-compensated heading were slower or fragile. The car still sits ~0.5 short of the inside target on average. Open: the outside half still uses only the plain line term (a wider outside line broke the flick in v0.52); a guarded outside integral, a larger `line_ki` range (only integers 1–5 were searched; 1.5–2 is the plateau), and spending the regained flick margin (larger apex, `corner_speed` 77, the `aim` rise limit) are the next candidates. |

## v0.54 — Gear shift points: downshift earlier, upshift a little later

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.54 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.54`). One mechanism, the gearbox shift points (co-tuned): `downshift_rpm` 13,500 → **15,000** (line 558, comment extended), `upshift_rpm` 18,500 → **18,600** (line 557). No line moved; no other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.53 first** (`runs/run_20261002_124759.csv`): 18.9 s of the lap run with the traction-control cut active and 16.7 s under power (throttle > 0.3, no brake) below 15,000 rpm, i.e. below the 16–18k torque plateau; in the ~750–900 m section the engine fell to 10,665 rpm. With `downshift_rpm` 13,500 the car only changes down once the lower gear would land below 13,500, so corner exits start low in the rev range. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** v0.46 found no gain in `downshift_rpm` (on v0.45: 14,500/15,000/15,500 → +0.36/+0.34/+0.37 s); the car has changed since (braking plan v0.48, lift band v0.51, apex line + inside integral v0.52–v0.53), so it was re-measured. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10 suites; v0.53: 77.263, max 0.862, 0 of 30 off; all 0 of 30 off unless noted).** (1) **Own: traction-control limit/shape mid-bend** (the bends run at the TC limit): `tc_slip_steer` 0.85/1.0 → 77.451/77.393; `tc_slip` 2.8/3.2 → 77.453/77.462; `tc_hold` 0.7 → 77.251 (max 0.889, noise); `tc_gain` 0.35 → 77.401. More slip mid-bend is slower. (2) **Own: braking gain** `brake_gain` 0.04/0.065 → 77.293 (max **0.964**)/77.464. (3) **Chosen, own: shift points.** `downshift_rpm` alone 12,000/14,500/15,000/15,500/16,000/16,500/17,000/17,500 → 77.653/**77.101/77.152/77.097**/77.158/77.451/78.596/86.215 (max 0.942/0.795/0.804/0.792/0.795/0.876/0.807/0.791; from 16,000 the unperturbed lap's flick slide rises 8 → 17 km/h and from 17,000 the gearbox hunts). Co-tuned `upshift_rpm` with 15,000: 18,000/18,300/18,500/**18,600**/18,650/18,700 → 77.595/77.361/77.152/**77.081**/77.028/77.067 (max 0.786/0.802/0.804/**0.759**/0.770/0.781); 14,500/18,600 → 77.140; 15,500/18,600 → 77.060 and 15,500/18,650 → 77.044 (but the unperturbed lap's flick slide 16 km/h). 15,000/18,600 chosen: on the 77.03–77.10 plateau, the lowest suite maximum, and clear of both the hunting (≥ 16,500) and the flick-slide edge (15,500). **First v0.54 attempt (another agent, on v0.53, all-30 means):** medium-bend grip +0.1/0.2/0.3 → 77.337/77.304/77.331; grip + `corner_speed` → 29–30 of 30 off (mildest 77.470, 2 off); wider outside exit +0.1/0.2/0.3 → 77.387 / 2 off / 20 off; stronger centring 0.1/0.2/0.4 → 77.361/77.404/77.530; `aim` rise limit 2/3/5 m/step → 77.433/77.448/77.345; `corner_speed` 77 → 77.152 but max 0.996; `line_apex` 0.40 → 77.244; `line_ki` 2 → 77.312; `corner_speed` 77 + `line_apex` 0.4 → 77.181 (0.961); `turn_grip` 7.5/8 → 77.362/77.324; `turn_grip_aero` 2e-4 → 77.344; Optuna `li54` (line knobs, 6 trials on suite 1) none better. Those lost on margin or noise; the shift points gain with **more** margin. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed ~76.92 s (−0.27 s), suites ~77.01/77.17/77.06, max \|trackPos\| ~0.76. Risk: low (gear hunting starts only from ~16,500). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.91 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 61 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_132524.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.916 s** → on screen 1:16:91. Lap **0.27 s faster** than v0.53 (1:17.18 → 1:16.91) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **77.006/77.173/77.063** (v0.53 77.206/77.316/77.267: −0.20/−0.14/−0.20 s; all-30 77.263 → 77.081), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.742/0.759/0.748** (v0.53 0.833/0.862/0.779). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.681** at ~3,284 m, the hairpin exit; flick 0.651 → 0.586). Same number of shifts (41, 18 down) but earlier downshifts: ~750–900 m minimum rpm 10,665 → 13,134; time under power below 15,000 rpm 16.7 → 15.5 s; traction-control cut time 18.9 → 19.8 s (more torque at the exits); braking 15.1 → 14.9 s. Section times: ~800 m −0.13 s, ~2,900 m −0.05, ~400 m −0.04, ~1,100 m −0.02, ~2,500 m +0.04, ~3,200 m +0.02. Slowest corner 61 km/h (~3,285 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.27 s vs v0.53; −0.20/−0.14/−0.20 s over three perturbation suites, 0 of 30 off), with more margin (suite max 0.862 → 0.759) |
| **Learned** | With the faster line the exits started too low in the rev range: changing down once the lower gear lands below 15,000 rpm (was 13,500) keeps the engine nearer its 16–18k torque plateau out of the corners and gives a little more engine braking on entry, and also calms the flick (0.65 → 0.59, slide 10 → 8 km/h). v0.46's "no gain from `downshift_rpm`" no longer holds on this car. The plateau is 14,500–15,500; from 16,000 the flick slide rises and from ~16,500 the box hunts. The traction limit mid-bend is not the bottleneck: allowing more slip (`tc_slip`, `tc_slip_steer`, `tc_gain`) was 0.13–0.20 s slower. |

## v0.55 — ABS refit: the brake is cut earlier when a wheel starts to lock

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.55 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.55`). One mechanism, the ABS, its two fixed constants made knobs: new `abs_ratio=.85` (line 579; was a hard-coded 0.8) and `abs_cut=.5` (580; the hard-coded halving, value unchanged); ABS code at lines 743–750 (comment 743–746). The brake is multiplied by `abs_cut` once the slowest wheel turns below `abs_ratio` of the car speed (above 20 km/h), i.e. the cut now starts at 15 % wheel slip instead of 20 %. No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.54 first** (`runs/run_20261002_132524.csv`): 14.7 s of the lap is braking, 17.5 s under the traction-control cut, 33.8 s at full throttle. In the big braking zones the car runs 10–12 km/h over the plan at pedal ~0.4–0.46 and slows at only 21–26 m/s² (capacity ~27–30 at speed); in the flick approach (~2,368–2,400 m, the crest) the sent pedal sits at exactly 0.50 = ABS-halved full brake while the car is up to 43 km/h over the plan, and the approach brakes, re-accelerates to full throttle (2,315–2,362 m) and brakes again as the sharpness plan flickers (allowed 237 → 302 → 228 → 267 → 190 km/h). **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10 suites; v0.54 base 77.081, max 0.759, 0 of 30 off; all 0 of 30 off unless noted).** (1) **Own, new: brake feedforward** (a base pedal `brake_ff`, scaled by how fast the allowed speed is falling, smoothed, full at 2 km/h per step, added while braking, to remove the P-controller's ~10 km/h lag): 0.1/0.2/0.3 → 77.204/77.341/77.541 (max 0.84/0.80/0.78): tracking the plan tighter brakes earlier; spending it on a shorter `brake_margin`: 0.2 + margin 10 → **27 of 30 off** (flick), 0.1 + margin 13 → 76.892 but 1 off (max 1.056, margin-11 run). (2) **Own, new: crest lift** (no throttle while the smoothed vertical acceleration is below −2/−4 m/s² above 150 km/h, for the flick approach's re-acceleration): 77.496/77.223 (max 0.78): slower. (3) **Inherited conclusion re-checked: `corner_speed` 77** (v0.53: max 0.996, rejected on margin): on v0.54 76.937 (max **0.937**); 76 → 77.005 (0.825); 78 → 76.878 but 1 off (`brake_margin` −2); 77 + `brake_load_min` 0.4 → 76.953 (0.892). Viable now, but −0.14 s for the whole margin. (4) **Chosen, own, new: ABS refit** (challenges v0.46's "no gain from the ABS threshold"; the braking plan, cap and line have changed since). Step ABS: cut 0.7 → **30 of 30 off**; cut 0/0.2/0.3/0.4 at ratio 0.8 → 76.871/76.900/76.847/76.898 (max 0.94/0.96/0.91/0.90); ratio 0.75/0.83/**0.85**/0.87/0.9 at cut 0.5 → 77.167/76.986/**76.856**/76.923/77.234 (7 off) (max 0.80/0.81/**0.899**/0.865/1.20); ratio 0.85 with cut 0.3/0.4/0.45 → 76.827/76.852/76.826 (max 0.93/0.90/0.91); ratio 0.9 with cut 0.3 → 24 of 30 off; 0.85/0.2 → 11 off. **Proportional slip ABS** (pedal × clip((wheel ratio − lo)/(hi − lo), min, 1)), hi/lo/min: 0.85/0.75/0.3 → 76.800 (max 0.962), 0.9/0.7/0.3 → 76.851 (0.984), 0.85/0.8/0.3 → 1 off, 0.9/0.8/0.2 → 21 off, 0.82/0.72/0.4 → 76.893 (0.916), 0.95/0.75/0.3 → 2 off: no better than the step ABS and closer to the edge. Ratio 0.85 with the halving kept: on the 76.83–76.99 plateau (ratio 0.83–0.87), the lowest suite maximum of the fast group, one value changed. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed ~76.80 s (−0.11 s), suites ~76.78/76.93/76.85, max \|trackPos\| ~0.90. Risk: the flick uses part of its margin (suite max 0.759 → 0.899; flick slide 7.9 → 16 km/h); a ratio of 0.9 or a weaker cut leaves the track there. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.80 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 61 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_134714.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.802 s** → on screen 1:16:80. Lap **0.11 s faster** than v0.54 (1:16.91 → 1:16.80) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **76.780/76.934/76.853** (v0.54 77.006/77.173/77.063: −0.23/−0.24/−0.21 s; all-30 77.081 → 76.856), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.820/0.899/0.810** (v0.54 0.742/0.759/0.748). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.729** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.54 0.681 at the hairpin exit). Braking time 14.73 → 14.65 s, mean pedal while braking 0.389 → 0.375. Section times: ~2,500 m −0.14 s, ~2,400 m −0.08, ~1,900 m −0.08, ~500 m −0.04, ~2,700 m +0.14 and ~2,800 m +0.08 (new brake touches at ~2,599 and ~2,737 m). Top speed 275.3 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.586 → **0.729**, slide 7.9 → 16.1 km/h, exit 7.9 → 9.4; kink 8.7 → 10.8 km/h; hairpin minimum 61.1 → 60.7 km/h, exit −0.68 → −0.67, slide 1.7 → 1.6; largest slide elsewhere 13.6 (~227 m) → 14.4 km/h (~2,625 m). Slowest corner 61 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (−0.11 s vs v0.54; −0.23/−0.24/−0.21 s over three perturbation suites, 0 of 30 off), at the cost of flick margin (suite max 0.759 → 0.899) |
| **Learned** | The braking zones are partly lock-limited: cutting the pedal at 15 % wheel slip instead of 20 % is ~0.2 s faster over 30 perturbed laps. The ABS is sharp-edged: a weaker cut (0.7) or a ratio of 0.9 leaves the track at the flick in 7–30 of 30 runs, and a proportional slip ABS gave no more. Every braking gain so far (ABS, `corner_speed` 77, a shorter margin with feedforward) lands on the flick: its slide and \|trackPos\| rise first. Tighter brake tracking (feedforward) only brakes earlier. The flick approach re-accelerates to full throttle between two brakings (2,315–2,362 m) as the sharpness plan flickers on the crest; a crest lift did not help. Open: a fix for that plan flicker would free margin for `corner_speed` 77 (−0.14 s on v0.54 at max 0.937). |
