## v0.56 — Plan hold: the allowed speed may only rise slowly while braking

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.56 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.56`). One mechanism, a rise limit on the braking plan: new `plan_rise=3` km/h per step (line 581) and `plan_hold=10` steps (582). While the car brakes and for `plan_hold` steps after the last brake touch, the allowed speed may rise by at most `plan_rise` per step (code before `c.allowed_speed`, lines 716–724; the hold counter at line 757, just before the ABS). No other knob changed. **Rejected: code restored to v0.55.** |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.55 first** (`runs/run_20261002_134714.csv`): slowest sections are the flick approach and flick (~2,400 m 3.96 s, ~2,500 m 3.00 s) and the hairpin (~3,200 m 3.83 s). Flick approach: brakes 270 → 244 km/h (2,290–2,310 m), re-accelerates to throttle 0.7 and 1.0 (2,313–2,363 m) while the sharpness plan flickers (304 → 237 → 271 → 240 → 189 km/h), then brakes from 233 km/h at a flat 0.50 pedal (ABS-halved full brake, the crest unloads the tyres; ~21 m/s² against ~27 m/s² before the hairpin) and is 20–40 km/h over the plan through the kink (vy 10.8 km/h). The flick itself runs at the `corner_speed` floor (allowed 75 km/h, road ahead 10–19 m) at full lock with the full-lock throttle cap 0.1–0.17, drifting from trackPos −0.17 to −0.73 before the steering flips at ~2,477 m (the lap's max). Hairpin: no outside set-up is possible, the beams are symmetric (road ends square) until ~40 m before the turn-in; full lock from ~3,259 m, 75 → 61 km/h scrub. Launch: traction-limited from the first 0.5 s (~11–12 m/s²), no clutch gain. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10 suites; v0.55 base all-30 76.856, max 0.899, 0 of 30 off; all 0 of 30 off unless noted).** (1) **Inherited lead, chosen to record: plan hold** (steady the crest plan flicker, from the v0.55 agent): `plan_rise` 1/3/6 (hold 10) → 77.073/**77.050**/77.038 (max 0.886/**0.787**/0.890); 1 with hold 25 → 77.073; 3 with hold 5 → 77.050. `plan_rise` 3 gives the flick margin back (unperturbed flick 0.729 → 0.640, slide 16.1 → 9.5 km/h) but costs ~0.19 s, and spending that margin fails: + `corner_speed` 77/78 → 2 of 30 off each (`corner_speed` +2 and `brake_margin` −2 runs; max 1.06/1.15), + `abs_ratio` 0.87 → 77.062. (2) **Own, new: edge brake at full lock** (a small brake, `edge_brake`·lock, once the room to the outside edge falls below `edge_room`, for the flick's drift to the right edge): 0.05/0.1 at room 0.3 → 76.901/76.905 (max 0.890), 0.1 at room 0.4 → 77.028 (0.867): the flick maximum is at the steering flip (2,477 m), not on the full-lock arc, so it barely moves. (3) **Own, new: outside line integrals** (the v0.53 inside integral extended to the exit or the entry half, own gain): exit 0.5/1.5 → 77.162/77.962 (2 off); entry 0.5/1.5 → 77.169/77.168 (1 off): confirms v0.52's "the line gain is all on the inside". (4) **Inherited conclusion re-checked: no 1st gear while moving** (v0.23, before traction control; the flick and hairpin run in 2nd at ~8,000 rpm, far below the 16–18k torque peak): `lowest_running_gear` 1 → 77.056 (max 0.874; hairpin exit slide 1.6 → 6.2 km/h, slide 22.5 km/h at ~3,266 m): still slower, v0.23 holds. (5) Knob nudges: `corner_speed` 77 → 76.832 but 1 off (`brake_margin` −2, max 1.055; unperturbed 76.494); `lock_throttle` 0.25 → 76.876 (0.904); `lock_throttle_edge` 0.15 → 76.834 (0.918); `downshift_rpm` 15,500 → 76.934 (0.918): all within noise. None beat v0.55 on the suites. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed ~76.99 s (+0.19 s), suites ~77.03/77.13/76.99, max \|trackPos\| ~0.79. Expected to be rejected unless the margin it frees can be spent; it could not. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.98 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 61 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_135925.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.988 s** → on screen 1:16:98. Lap **0.19 s slower** than v0.55 (1:16.80 → 1:16.98); suites **77.029/77.131/76.990** (v0.55 76.780/76.934/76.853: +0.25/+0.20/+0.14 s; all-30 76.856 → 77.050), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.753/0.787/0.774** (v0.55 0.820/0.899/0.810). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.677** at ~3,283 m, the hairpin exit; flick 0.729 → 0.640). Braking time 14.81 → 14.99 s (steps with brake > 0). The flick approach still reaches full throttle between the two brakings (the hold lets the plan climb 30 km/h in its 10 steps) and arrives at ~2,366 m at 231 km/h (v0.55 233). Section times: ~2,500 m +0.12 s, ~2,400 m +0.06, ~2,700 m −0.04, ~3,100/3,400/900 m −0.02 each. Top speed 275.8 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.729 → **0.640**, slide 16.1 → 9.5 km/h, exit 9.4 → 8.1; kink 10.8 → 10.4 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.7 km/h, exit −0.67 → −0.68, slide 1.6; largest slide elsewhere 14.4 (~2,625 m) → 14.3 km/h (~788 m). Slowest corner 61 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 0.19 s slower (+0.14 to +0.25 s over three perturbation suites); the flick margin it frees (suite max 0.899 → 0.787) could not be spent (`corner_speed` 77/78 on top: 2 of 30 off). Driver code reverted to v0.55 (`git checkout v0.55 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`). |
| **Learned** | Slowing the plan's rise while braking calms the flick (slide 16 → 9.5 km/h, max 0.73 → 0.64) but only by arriving slower: the margin costs as much time as `corner_speed` 77 gives, and `corner_speed` 77–78 on top still leaves the track in the `brake_margin` −2 run. The flick's maximum \|trackPos\| sits at the left-to-right steering flip (~2,477 m), not on the full-lock arc, so an edge brake on the arc does nothing. An integral on the outside half of a bend (entry or exit) is 0.3–1.1 s slower: the line gain stays on the inside. No 1st gear while moving (v0.23) still holds with traction control (1st: +0.20 s, hairpin exit slide 6 km/h). The hairpin gives no beam cue for an outside set-up until ~40 m before turn-in (square road end), so pre-positioning there needs track memory (on hold). Single-knob nudges around v0.55 are all within ±0.05 s on the suites. |

## v0.57 — Smoothed steering in the sharpness fade

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.57 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.57`). One mechanism, the steering input of the sharpness plan's fade (co-tuned): new `fade_lp=.9` (line 564): the \|steer\| that gates and fades the sharpness credit is now a smoothed value `c.steer_f` = `fade_lp`·previous + (1 − `fade_lp`)·\|steer\| (~0.2 s time constant; comment 703–708, code 711–712 and 721); the window co-tuned: `turn_steer_max` 0.75 → **0.78** (line 562), `turn_steer_fade` 0.25 → **0.17** (563; full credit up to 0.61, none from 0.78). No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.55 first** (`runs/run_20261002_134714.csv`), time by state: full throttle 33.5 s, braking 14.8, traction-control cut 14.6, below the allowed speed with the stored throttle still climbing ("ramp") 9.3, full-lock cap 4.0, riding/lifting 2.3. In steady medium corners (e.g. ~400–520 m, 95–115 km/h, \|steer\| 0.5–0.62) the car saw-tooths: brake 0.1–0.3, stored throttle back to 0, climb at +0.05 per step, over the plan, brake again; the allowed speed swings 97–115 km/h within 50 m because the raw \|steer\| (jittering in the fade window 0.5–0.75) scales the sharpness credit step by step. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10 suites; v0.55 base all-30 76.856, max 0.899, 0 of 30 off; all 0 of 30 off unless noted).** (1) **Inherited conclusion re-checked: throttle ramp +0.05 per step** (v0.5, before traction control and the braking plan): 0.03/0.04/0.08/0.10/0.15 → 77.250/77.090/76.996/77.108 (2 off)/77.134 (2 off): v0.5's value is still the optimum; a faster ramp runs wide at the flick. (2) **Own, new: deficit-scaled ramp** (extra `ramp_k`·clip((allowed − speed − 10)/20, 0, 1) per step, fast only far below the plan, for the exits): 0.05/0.15 → 77.151 (1 off)/77.173 (3 off). (3) **Chosen, own, new: smoothed \|steer\| in the sharpness fade:** `fade_lp` alone 0.5/0.8/0.9 → 76.927/76.848/76.804 (max 0.819/0.837/0.922); `fade_lp` 0.8 + `corner_speed` 77 → 76.805 but 1 off (`brake_margin` −2). Optuna study `lp57` (20 trials on suites 1+3, `fade_lp` 0.5–0.95, `turn_steer_max` 0.7–0.85, `turn_steer_fade` 0.15–0.35; top 4 confirmed on all 30): 76.712/76.713/76.720/76.731 (max 0.925/0.940/0.912/0.933), all near `fade_lp` 0.9, max ~0.8, fade ~0.16. Rounded: (0.9, 0.8, 0.16) 76.738 (max 0.892, unperturbed 76.832); **(0.9, 0.78, 0.17) 76.706** (max 0.918, unperturbed 76.572); (0.9, 0.8, 0.2) 76.744 (0.927); (0.85, 0.8, 0.16) 76.738 (0.910). Control without smoothing, (0, 0.8, 0.16): 76.814 (0.860), so ~0.09 s of the gain is the smoothing and ~0.04 the window (on v0.51 `turn_steer_max` 0.8 alone was −0.04). (0.9, 0.78, 0.17) chosen: the best all-30 mean of the plateau (76.71–76.74) and the best unperturbed lap. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): unperturbed ~76.57 s (−0.23 s), suites ~76.67/76.78/76.67, max \|trackPos\| ~0.92. Risk: a little flick margin is used (suite max 0.899 → 0.918, flick slide 16.1 → 18.6 km/h). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.57 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_141255.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.572 s** → on screen 1:16:57. Lap **0.23 s faster** than v0.55 (1:16.80 → 1:16.57) — **the fastest lap so far**; suites **76.672/76.776/76.671** (v0.55 76.780/76.934/76.853: −0.11/−0.16/−0.18 s; all-30 76.856 → 76.706), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.918/0.832/0.811** (v0.55 0.820/0.899/0.810). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.803** at ~2,477 m, the flick steering flip; v0.55 0.729). Braking time 14.81 s and 32 brake applications, both unchanged (mean pedal 0.375 → 0.377): the gain is not fewer brake touches but more speed through the medium bends. Time by state: full throttle 33.5 → 34.1 s, traction-control cut 14.6 → 14.0. Section times: ~2,700 m −0.10 s, ~2,800 m −0.08, ~500 m −0.06, ~2,600 m −0.04, ~2,900 m and ~3,000 m +0.04 each. Top speed 275.1 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.729 → **0.803**, slide 16.1 → 18.6 km/h, exit 9.4 → 14.4; kink 10.8 → 10.6 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.7 → 59.5 km/h, exit −0.67 → −0.62, slide 1.6 → 1.3; largest slide elsewhere 14.4 (~2,625 m) → 13.6 km/h (~227 m). Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |

| **Learned** | The sharpness plan was reading steering noise: in medium bends the raw \|steer\| jitters inside the fade window and moved the allowed speed by 10–18 km/h step to step. Smoothing it (~0.2 s) lets the plan see the bend's real steering and allows a narrower, later window (0.61–0.78 instead of 0.5–0.75); the medium bends (~500 m, ~2,600–2,800 m) gain most. The mid-corner brake/throttle sawtooth itself is not gone (32 brake applications either way). The throttle ramp of +0.05 per step (v0.5) is still optimal: faster ramps, flat or scaled by the deficit, run wide at the flick (1–3 of 30 off from 0.10). The flick takes the margin again (unperturbed slide 18.6 km/h, max 0.80 at the steering flip); `corner_speed` 77 on top still leaves the track in the `brake_margin` −2 run. |

## v0.58 — Corner set-up from the beam asymmetry

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.58 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.58`). One mechanism, the **corner set-up** (co-tuned): before a bend is detected (`side` 0), with less than `setup_dist` (new, **160** m, line 555) of road visible along the track direction and \|previous steer\| below `setup_steer` (new, **0.02**, line 559), the beams `setup_beam` (new, **2**°, line 556) either side of the track direction are compared; if they differ by more than `setup_min` (new, **3** m, line 557) the longer side is the coming bend's direction, and while more than 80 m of road is visible the car is pulled (`line_gain`) to `setup_offset` (new, **0.7**, line 558) on the outside of it. The `road` beam is now computed before the bend check (line 624; was inside it). Comment 625–631, code 632–639. No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.57 first** (`runs/run_20261002_141255.csv`, `tools/width.py`): entries at −80 m used −0.13 to +0.15 of the width (mean +0.02). Cause found in the telemetry: the bend is only detected when the weighted bearing passes 2°, which on every approach happens **~35 m before the corner** (448 m: `aim` −0.1 to −0.5° from 330 to 375 m, −3.3° at 396 m with 33 m of road left; 1,926 m and the hairpin the same), when the road along the track direction is already under 60 m, so the phase is −1 at once and the line target jumps straight to the **inside**: there has never been an outside approach phase. The d²-weighted bearing is blind to an approaching bend because the long straight-ahead beams dominate. But the end of the road ahead is a slanted edge: the beams ±1–2° differ by 5–20 m from ~150 m out, longer on the side the road turns (448 m: 90 → 72 m left to right at 345 m). **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`; v0.57 base suite 1 76.672, all-30 76.706, max 0.918, 0 off).** (1) **Inherited conclusion challenged: "the line pull is cancelled by the heading/centring terms"** (batch-4 hypothesis). Own test: drop a share `appr_aim` of the look-ahead bearing term while the line is outside (phase > 0): 0.3/0.6/0.9 → suite 1 76.784/76.891/77.332; with `line_offset` 0.6 77.752; earlier bend start `line_on` 1.5° 76.907, both 77.021 (all 0 off). Width unchanged (entries −0.05 to +0.17): before the corner the pull is not too weak, it never acts there. (2) **Chosen, own, new: corner set-up from the beam asymmetry.** Ungated: `setup_dist` 120/160/200 at offset 0.47 → **5–6 of 10 off** (flick: the set-up fires on the kink → flick approach and puts the car on the wrong side of the S); offset 0.7/0.3 → 77.077 (max 0.981)/76.723. Gated by \|steer\| (`setup_steer`): 0.05/0.1 at offset 0.5 → 76.701 / 1 off; 0.05 at 0.7 → 76.763; **0.02 at 0.7 → 76.565** (max 0.847); `setup_min` 6 → 76.615; 200 m/0.4 → 76.666. Optuna study `v58setup` (32 trials on suite 1: `setup_dist` 110–220, `setup_offset` 0.4–0.9, `setup_steer` 0.01–0.08, `setup_min` 2–10; plateau 76.57–76.72, all 0 off), top 4 confirmed on all 30: (160, 0.7, 0.02, 3) **76.638**, (145, 0.57, 0.044, 5) 76.646, (167, 0.61, 0.029, 8) 76.649, (165, 0.52, 0.035, 6) 76.694. Trial 0 (round values) kept: best all-30 mean and lowest max (0.847). (3) Pure-pursuit path following (the batch-4 hypothesis) not built in this version: the measurement above shows the first missing piece is knowing the bend early, which any path follower needs too. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): suites ~76.57/76.74/76.61 (all-30 −0.07 s), max \|trackPos\| ~0.85, unperturbed lap ~76.68 (+0.10 s, within the ±0.2 s chaos). Entries ~0.1 further outside. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.67 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 271 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 59 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_144324.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.676 s** → on screen 1:16:67: **0.10 s slower** than v0.57 on the unperturbed lap, but suites **76.565/76.737/76.614** (v0.57 76.672/76.776/76.671: **−0.11/−0.04/−0.06 s**; all-30 76.706 → **76.638**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.847/0.844/0.805** (v0.57 0.918/0.832/0.811). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.703** at ~2,476 m, the flick; v0.57 0.803). **Width use (entry −80 m, + = outside):** 448 m −0.08 → +0.07, 786 m +0.15 → +0.15, 1,041 m +0.04 → +0.18, 1,523 m −0.13 → +0.04, 1,926 m +0.06 → +0.19, flick +0.32 → +0.28, 2,989 m −0.07 → +0.07, hairpin −0.10 → +0.02 (mean +0.02 → +0.13); slowest points and exits about unchanged except the 1,041 m bend (slowest point −0.54 → −0.06, now at ~1,018 m, 146 → 137 km/h). Section times: ~1,000 m +0.11 s, ~1,300 m +0.04, ~1,900 m +0.04, ~500 m +0.04, ~2,900 m −0.06, ~3,000 m −0.07. Top speed 271.0 km/h (275 before). **Watch points:** flick 0.803 → **0.703**, slide 18.6 → 14.9 km/h, exit 14.4 → 9.3; kink 10.6 → 10.4 km/h; hairpin minimum 59.5 → 59.4 km/h, exit 0.62 → 0.59; largest slide elsewhere 13.6 (~227 m) → 18.0 km/h (~1,016 m). Slowest corner 59 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — enabling change: the first line mechanism that acts before the corner (entries +0.02 → +0.13 of the width, the bend direction known ~150 m out); faster over all three perturbation suites (−0.11/−0.04/−0.06 s, all-30 −0.07 s, max \|trackPos\| 0.918 → 0.847, 0 of 30 off), though the unperturbed lap is 0.10 s slower (within the ±0.2 s lap-to-lap chaos) |
| **Learned** | Until v0.57 the racing line never had an outside approach: the d²-weighted bearing only passes 2° ~35 m before a bend, when the road along the track is already under 60 m (phase −1), so the target went straight to the inside. Cutting the base terms that "fight" the line changes nothing there. The bend's side is readable from ~150 m: the beams ±2° of the track direction differ by metres, longer on the turning side. Ungated, that signal fires in the kink before the flick (an S-bend: it reads the second part) and runs the car off (5–6 of 10); gating it to near-straight steering (\|steer\| < 0.02) fixes that but also self-limits the set-up (once the car steers out, it pauses), so entries reach only +0.07 to +0.19, not the 0.7 aimed for. Corner speeds are unchanged and the 1,041 m bend got slower (its apex moved early, −0.54 → −0.06): the braking/sharpness plan does not yet reward the outside entry, and the apex/exit still use the old phase logic. Follow-ups this unlocks: (a) a set-up that holds its side (latched until the bend is detected) instead of the steering gate, with an S-bend rule for the kink → flick; (b) hand the side from the set-up to the line so the inside phase starts at a late apex instead of at 60 m of road; (c) an exit phase that stays outside to the straight. If those don't recover the unperturbed lap, reconsider. |

## v0.59 — Corner set-up re-tuned: wider, ends earlier

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.59 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.59`). One mechanism, v0.58's **corner set-up**, re-tuned (its knobs co-tuned): `setup_offset` 0.7 → **0.85** (line 558); the road length above which the set-up pulls is now a knob, new `setup_road` **95** m (line 559; was a hard-coded 80, code line 641); `setup_steer` 0.02 → **0.045** (line 560). Comment 633–635. No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.58 first** (`runs/run_20261002_144324.csv`). In the steady medium bends the car rides exactly on the plan's allowed speed (1,520 m: 136–141 km/h = allowed; 2,977 m: 141 = allowed) at only \|steer\| 0.2–0.35 and 5–9 km/h of slide, with the line target −0.81 but the car at −0.42: the corner speed there is set by the braking/sharpness plan, not by grip or by the line, so a wider line only pays where the plan credits it. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10 suites; v0.58 base all-30 76.638, max 0.847, 0 off).** (1) **v0.58's follow-up (a), latched set-up** (side held until the bend is detected instead of the \|steer\| gate): 77.273 (max 0.988; `setup_road` 60/40: 76.940/77.206). It does what was asked (entries mean +0.13 → +0.22, 1,041 m bend back to 146 km/h, −0.13 s there) but the held set-up carries through the kink and the flick apex moves to the wrong side (+0.86, 2,400 m section +0.28 s). Release rules: on opposite beam asymmetry 77.287–77.330 (1 off); on \|steer\| > 0.1 76.683 (unperturbed 76.462), > 0.15 76.962; no set-up within 30/60/100 m after a bend (S-bend rule) 76.840/76.706/76.769 (1 off). Optuna study `v59latch` (36 trials on suite 1: hold 0.02–0.3, gap 0–120 m, offset 0.4–0.9, road 50–110, gate 0.01–0.06; top 4 on all 30): best (hold 0.05, gap 1, offset 0.86, road 93, gate 0.044) **76.613**, others 76.677–76.684. Rounded (0.05, 0, 0.85, 95, 0.045): all-30 76.619, unperturbed 76.390. **Control without the latch at the same values: all-30 76.613, unperturbed 76.460**, so the latch itself adds nothing on the suites, and a fully held latch at offset 0.8–0.9 runs off at the flick in **25 of 30**: not kept. (2) **Own: later inside phase** (phase centre `road` 60 → 50/70 m, width 20 → 10/35 m): 77.033/77.435/77.431/76.911 (5 off): the 60/20 phase is a sharp optimum. (3) **Own, new: exit phase** (after the bend's shortest road + 10 m, outside target 0.65/0.8, or the pull ×0.5): 77.150 / 77.600 (2 off) / 77.200 (4 off): the hairpin exit runs to 0.81–0.92. (4) **Own, new: speed-scaled line pull** (linear pure pursuit: gain × (`pp_ref`/v)^k, equal at `pp_ref`): k 1 at 150/200/110 km/h → 76.854 / 77.215 (1 off) / 77.134; k 0.5 → 76.741. Path following that weakens the pull at speed does not help: the entries are not over-pulled. (5) **Chosen: the set-up re-tuned without the latch** (found as the latch's control). Neighbourhood (all 0 of 30 off): offset 0.8/0.9 → 76.637/76.778, gate 0.04/0.05 → 76.684/76.663, road 90/100 → 76.708/76.649; offset 0.85 alone 76.743, road 95 alone 76.680. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** v0.58 kept 0.02 as the gate because looser gates read the kink (0.05 at offset 0.5: 76.701; 0.1: 1 off); with the set-up ending at 95 m instead of 80 m, 0.045 is safe again. |

The set-up's value is in where it ends: stopping the outside pull at 95 m of road instead of 80 m lets the 1,041 m bend turn in from the outside to a late inside point again (146 km/h), and that alone pays the v0.58 cost back. The line's inside phase (60 m centre, 20 m wide) is a sharp optimum; wider exits and speed-scaled pulls are slower. In the steady medium bends the car rides the plan at \|steer\| 0.2–0.35 with little slide and short of the inside target: the corner speed there is set by the braking/sharpness plan (`corner_speed` 75 at the end of ~35 m of visible road, sharpness grip 8–9 m/s²), so more line width only pays once the plan credits it. Next: make the plan see the line (e.g. the sharpness credit from the arc the car will actually take), rather than more line geometry. |

## v0.60 — Grip to spare: more planned grip at light steering

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.60 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.60`). One mechanism, **grip to spare** in the sharpness plan (co-tuned): the sideways grip the plan assumes, `turn_grip`, is raised by new `grip_boost` **0.3** (line 572) while the smoothed \|steer\| (`c.steer_f`, v0.57) is below new `boost_steer` **0.2** (573), fading out over new `boost_fade` **0.1** (574), none from \|steer\| 0.3; `tg` (line 746) replaces `turn_grip` in the plan's grip speed (754). Comment 739–745. No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.59 first** (`runs/run_20261002_151026.csv`): in the steady medium bends (1,500–1,530 m, 2,940–2,990 m) the car coasts on the allowed speed (throttle 0–0.15, no brake) at \|steer\| 0.15–0.35 and 5–8 km/h of slide, 0.35–0.45 short of the inside target; at 448 m it is at \|steer\| 0.5–0.68 and drifts to the outside (+0.13 at the slowest point) as the inside integral fades. So light steering = grip to spare, heavy steering = at the limit. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, all 3×10; v0.59 base all-30 76.613, max 0.929, 0 off).** (1) **Own, new: end-of-sight speed from the curvature** (the plan's speed at the end of each beam = a share of that beam's grip speed instead of `corner_speed` 75, i.e. "the bend continues"): share 0.5–0.8 uncapped → **30 of 30 off** (448 m: near-straight beams have huge radii); capped at 85/95/105 km/h → 13 / 30 / 30 of 30 off (flick and hairpin: what lies past the sight line is sharper). Dead: the unseen part of the track is exactly where it fails. (2) **Inherited conclusions re-checked on v0.59:** `corner_speed` 77 ("flick cliff") → 76.587 but **1 off** (`brake_margin` −2 run, max 1.018): still true; `turn_grip` 7.3 → 76.592, 0 off (v0.52's "7.5: 1 off" does not apply at 7.3, but only −0.02 s). (3) **Line knobs** (the theme, 448 m inside drift): `line_isteer` 0.5 → 76.867; `apex_steer` 0.4 → 76.794: slower. (4) **Chosen, own, new: grip boost at light steering.** `grip_boost` 0.05/0.1/0.15/0.2/0.3 below \|steer\| 0.25 (fade 0.15) → 76.571/76.592/76.570/76.590/76.598; 0.2 below 0.35 → 76.611; **0.3 below 0.2 (fade 0.1) → 76.525, max 0.849** (all 0 off). Optuna study `v60gb` (30 trials on suite 1: boost 0.1–0.5, steer 0.1–0.35, fade 0.05–0.3; plateau 76.42–76.50, 0 off), top 4 on all 30: 76.534 / 76.557 / 76.559 / 76.572; trial 0 (0.3, 0.2, 0.1) kept: best all-30 and lowest max. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): suites ~76.44/76.58/76.56 (all-30 −0.09 s), max \|trackPos\| ~0.85, unperturbed lap ~76.40 (−0.06 s). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.40 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 274 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |

| **Learned** | The sharpness plan's grip is conservative where the car steers lightly: +30 % planned grip below smoothed \|steer\| 0.2, gone by 0.3, is faster and safer over the suites (the extra speed asks for more steering, which removes the extra: self-limiting). The gain is on approaches and bend exits (~800, ~1,000, ~2,700 m), not in the steady medium bends, where the smoothed \|steer\| is already 0.2–0.35: the speed there is still set by the plan's end-of-sight assumption (`corner_speed` 75 within the visible road). Replacing that assumption with "the bend continues at the curvature seen" runs off at every unseen sharpening (448 m, flick, hairpin: 13–30 of 30 off even capped at 85 km/h), so without track memory the end-of-sight speed must stay a floor. The line's width use is unchanged by this version: the line and the plan are still separate; next, a plan credit from the line the car will take (outside position at turn-in) or the flick's S-bend set-up. `corner_speed` 77 still goes off once (flick, `brake_margin` −2). |

## v0.61 — Exit release: the inside target lets go once the road opens

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.61 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.61`). One line mechanism, the **exit release** (co-tuned): in a bend the shortest road seen along the track direction is tracked (`c.road_min`, reset when a new bend side starts); on the inside half (phase < 0), once the road has grown new `rel_start` **7** m past that minimum, the inside target is released by new `rel_share` **0.8** over new `rel_width` **2** m (phase × (1 − 0.8·ramp)), so the target goes from fully inside to 20 % of it and the car unwinds toward the outside; the inside integral is left as it was. Knobs at lines 562–564; comment 661–668, code 669–673. No other knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.60 first** (`runs/run_20261002_152420.csv`). (a) Through a bend the road along the track direction stays short, so the phase stays −1 (inside) until it is back over 60 m: at 448 m the target stays +0.5 inside up to 529 m and at 1,522 m up to 1,590 m, while the car is already past the apex and running wide (448 m: +0.18 → −0.37 at \|steer\| 0.5–0.6, 8–10 km/h slide): the line asks for steering the exit could spend on throttle. (b) The v0.58/v0.59 set-up is a chattering pulse: it adds (0.85 − trackPos)·0.5 ≈ 0.4 steer, which shuts its own \|steer\| < 0.045 gate the next step, so on every approach the car weaves ±0.2–0.3 steer at 220–270 km/h (300–340 m, 1,390–1,424 m, 2,834–2,851 m) and entries stay at +0.05 to +0.35. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, suite 1 base 76.441, all-30 76.525).** (1) **Own, new: smooth set-up** (pull capped at `setup_cap` and/or gate on a low-passed signed steer, or on steer minus its own pull, or on \|`aim`\|): cap 0.05/0.1/0.2 → 76.684/76.522/76.667; low-pass 0.5–0.9 → 76.497–76.694 (gate 0.1: 1 off); minus own pull = unchanged; \|aim\| < 1.5° / 1° → 2 / 4 off, capped 0.1 → 76.437/76.640. The chatter is not what costs the time. (2) **Own, new: set-up integral** (`setup_ki` 1–6, cap 0.05–0.2, decay 0.9–0.97) → 76.476–76.667. (3) **Own: late apex** (inside target ramped in over 10–60 m after the bend is detected) → 76.66–77.45, slower the later: **inherited "60/20 phase is a sharp optimum" confirmed** from the other side. (4) **Exit phase** (separate exit offset 0/0.25/0.6 or pull share 0/0.5/1.5 once the road has grown) → 76.617–76.886, 0.25: 4 off, 0.6: 1 off: re-checks v0.59's finding, still true. (5) **Own, new: line-error speed feedback** (allowed speed −2/5/10 % per unit the car is outside the inside target) → 76.466/76.631/77.018; it calms the flick (max 0.77 → 0.55) but **spending that on `corner_speed` 77/79 (rank 2) does not pay**: 76.559 (0 off) / 76.396 (2 off), `corner_speed` 77 alone 76.554, 1 off: still the flick cliff. (6) **Chosen, own, new: exit release.** First screen 0.3–1.0 share: 76.369–76.481 on suite 1; Optuna study `v61rel` (36 trials on suite 1: share 0.2–1, start 0–10 m, width 2–20 m, integral released or not; the integral kept in all top trials), top 5 on all 30: 76.410/76.428/76.457/76.461/76.466. Rounded on all 30: (0.8, 7, 2) **76.416, max 0.850**; (0.75, 6, 2) 76.377, max 0.907; (0.7, 5, 2) 76.407; (0.8, 6, 3) 76.424: plateau 76.38–76.42, all 0 of 30 off. (0.8, 7, 2) kept: equal mean within noise, lowest suite max. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, see Why): suites 76.320/76.445/76.482 (all-30 −0.11 s), max \|trackPos\| 0.850, unperturbed lap 76.160 (−0.24 s). Exits a little wider. Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.16 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_154418.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **76.160 s** → on screen 1:16:16: **0.24 s faster** than v0.60 (76.402), the fastest lap so far. Suites **76.320/76.445/76.482** (v0.60 76.441/76.578/76.557: **−0.12/−0.13/−0.08 s**; all-30 76.525 → **76.416**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.842/0.850/0.804** (v0.60 0.845/0.849/0.793). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.791** at ~2,476 m, the flick steering flip; v0.60 0.770). Gains spread over the lap, ~0.02–0.04 s in most sections: ~1,900 m −0.04, ~2,600 m −0.04, ~3,000 m −0.04, ~200/500/700/1,000/1,300/1,600/1,700/2,400/2,500/3,200/3,300 m −0.02 each; ~2,700 m +0.04, ~1,400 m +0.03, ~400/1,800 m +0.02. Full throttle 34.1 → 35.1 s, brake 15.1 → 14.8 s. **Width use (entry −80 m / slowest point / exit +80 m, + = outside):** 448 m +0.09/+0.13/+0.22 (was +0.23 exit), 788 m +0.15/−0.06/+0.10, 1,040 m +0.21/−0.51/+0.13, 1,520 m +0.05/−0.43/**−0.14** (was −0.17), 1,930 m +0.24/−0.20/**−0.04** (−0.08), flick +0.38/−0.79/**−0.03** (−0.12), 2,976 m +0.11/−0.43/+0.14 (+0.12), hairpin +0.05/+0.64/+0.42 (+0.41): exits +0.02 to +0.09 wider, entries and slowest points unchanged. Slowest speeds 1,520 m 136, 2,976 m 139 (136), hairpin 60 km/h. Top speed 275.4 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.770 → **0.791**, slide 15.9 → 16.0 km/h, exit 9.5 → 7.9; kink 9.5 → 8.8 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.2 → 60.3 km/h, exit 0.64 → 0.64; largest slide elsewhere 13.8 (~788 m) → 13.4 km/h (~769 m). Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (1:16.16, −0.24 s vs v0.60) and faster over all three perturbation suites (−0.12/−0.13/−0.08 s, all-30 76.525 → 76.416), 0 of 30 off, suite max \|trackPos\| 0.849 → 0.850 |
| **Learned** | The line's inside phase lasted past the apex: the phase is set by the road along the track direction, which stays short through the whole bend, so the target held the car inside until the bend ended. Letting the target go once the road starts to open (7 m past its shortest) is worth ~0.1 s over the suites and 0.24 s on the unperturbed lap, a little everywhere (full throttle +1.0 s per lap) rather than in one corner; the inside integral must stay on (releasing it too was never in the top trials). Entry-side line mechanisms did not pay this time: smoothing the chattering set-up, a set-up integral and a later apex were all slower, and the late apex was monotonically slower the later it came, so on this car the turn-in point the 60/20 phase gives is right and the time is on the exit side. A line-error speed feedback buys flick margin (0.77 → 0.55) but `corner_speed` 77 still leaves the track once with it. Exit width grew only +0.02 to +0.09: the release frees steering, it does not yet run the car to the outside edge; next, an exit that uses the full width where a straight follows (hairpin, 448 m, 1,926 m) now that the target lets go at the right moment. |

## v0.62 — Corner speed 76: the end-of-sight speed one step up

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.62 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.62`). `corner_speed` **75 → 76** km/h (line 537, comment updated): the speed the braking plan must be able to reach by the end of the visible road. No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.61 first** (`runs/run_20261002_154418.csv`). (a) On the approaches to the 1,520 m and 1,930 m bends the car brakes 60–70 m (275 → 200 km/h, 260 → 150 km/h) with the bearing at −0.3 to −1.9° (under the 2° bend threshold) and the target at 0: the set-up fires only for a few steps on the full-throttle part (1,372 m), so the bend is entered from the centre (+0.05/+0.24). (b) The flick's first (left) part is entered from the **inside** (+0.46 at 2,398–2,432 m, side 0, road 23–58 m), so it needs full lock at 74 km/h and slides to −0.78 at the steering flip. (c) Exits: target −0.47 outside but the bend ends at \|aim\| < 1° within ~40 m (1,648 m, 1,956 m), the car reaches only −0.17; at 1,530–1,580 m the exit is traction-limited (TC cuts 1.0 → 0.7, 9–10 km/h slide). **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, suite 1 base 76.320).** (1) **Own: exit release beyond 100 %** (`rel_share` 1.0/1.2/1.5 = target past the centre to the outside after the apex) → 76.378/76.351/76.454. (2) **Inherited follow-up: full-width exit where a straight follows** (exit offset + 0.3/0.5 × clip((road − 100)/50) after the apex) → 77.110/77.177, **3 of 10 off** (hairpin exit 0.93–0.94, +0.9 s on p0). (3) **Own: exit hold** (keep the bend side 10/25 steps after \|aim\| < 1° once the apex has passed) → 76.546/77.143. (4) **Own, new: braking-zone set-up hold** (keep the set-up side at 0.3/0.5/0.8 × 0.85 through the braking zone until the bend is detected) → 76.571/76.596/**6 of 10 off** (re-checks v0.59's "latched set-up" result: still true). (5) **Own, new: short-sight set-up** (with < 95/60 m of road and no bend yet, the ±3–8° beam asymmetry sets an outside target 0.3–0.8, \|steer\| gate 0.15–0.3; aimed at the flick entry) → 76.661–77.114, all slower. (6) **Own, new: brake-scaled line pull** (set-up and bend pull × (1 − k·brake), k 0.3/0.6/1.0) → 76.484/76.440/76.553. So moving the car while braking costs time in both directions, more pull and less: the current line in braking zones is a local optimum. (7) Knob nudges (suite 1): `rel_start` 5/9 76.313/76.340, `line_offset` 0.52 76.610, `line_gain` 0.55/0.45 76.495/76.303, `lookahead_gain` 1.8 76.390, `setup_road` 105 76.345, **`corner_speed` 76 76.285**. On all 30: `corner_speed` 76 **76.335** (0 off, max 0.923), + `rel_start` 5 76.339, `rel_start` 5 alone 76.393, base 76.416. **Challenged:** "`corner_speed` is held by the flick cliff" (v0.55–v0.61: 77 → 1 of 30 off): true for 77, but 76 is on the safe side of it and all three suites are faster. Chosen as the only measured gain this iteration; it is a knob nudge, not a line mechanism. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 76.285/76.383/76.337 (all-30 −0.08 s), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.923; unperturbed lap 76.132 (−0.03 s). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.13 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 274 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |

| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — fastest lap so far (1:16.13, −0.03 s vs v0.61) and faster on all three perturbation suites (−0.04/−0.06/−0.15 s, all-30 76.416 → 76.335), 0 of 30 off; cost: suite max \|trackPos\| 0.850 → 0.923 (suite 2) |
| **Learned** | Six line mechanisms aimed at the batch theme were all slower on this car: a wider or longer exit (1 of them 3 of 10 off at the hairpin exit), and every change to the line in the braking zones, whether more outside pull (set-up hold, short-sight set-up) or less pull (brake-scaled), so lateral moves while braking are tuned to a local optimum and the line's remaining time is not reachable by adding targets on top of the present controller. The approach/entry problem is real (1,520 m and 1,930 m entered from the centre after 60–70 m of straight braking below the 2° bend threshold; the flick's first part entered from the inside), but fixing it needs the planned-line / path-following redesign, not another pull. `corner_speed` 76 is safe on all 30 runs (77 is not): the flick cliff sits between 76 and 77. Suite max rose to 0.923, so the next speed step needs flick margin first. |

## v0.63 — Traction limit 4.5 m/s: exits were traction-limited

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.63 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.63`). `tc_slip` **2.5 → 4.5** m/s (line 578, comment updated): the rear over-speed traction control allows before it cuts the throttle, in corners; on straights the `tc_slip_straight` extra (5.0) still comes on top (7.5 → 9.5 m/s). No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.62 first** (`runs/run_20261002_155252.csv`). Of the lap's time below the plan's allowed speed without full throttle or brake (~22 s), **14.3 s is traction-control cuts**, 4.6 s the +0.05/step throttle ramp after braking, 2.5 s the full-lock limit: every exit (1,540–1,590 m at \|steer\| 0.4: sent throttle 0.75–0.88 against stored 1.0, rear spin 14–16; hairpin exit 3,288–3,330 m at steer 0: 0.64–0.97, 67 → 140 km/h) is traction-limited, not line-limited. **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, suite 1 base 76.285).** (1) **Inherited rank 1: pure pursuit** (in a bend the heading and line terms replaced by the bearing to a point on the line target L = v·T ahead, gain 1.75 per unit of lock): T 1.5 s 76.631, T 0.7 s 78.485, T 1.5 s gain 1.2 **7 of 10 off** (flick). **Challenged:** the present controller already *is* a pure pursuit in linearised form: heading 4.77/rad and line 0.5 per unit trackPos (6 m) equal aiming at a line point ~57 m ahead; a speed-scaled look-ahead is the speed-scaled line pull batch 4 found slower (76.74–77.22), and that is what these trials show again. The line's limit is where the target is, not how it is followed. (2) **Own, new: plan credits the line position** (sharpness radius + k·6 m × the car's offset toward the bend's outside, so outside at turn-in = larger radius, inside = smaller): k 0.25/0.5/1.0 → 76.286/76.357/76.389 (k 1: 1 off). (3) **Own: traction knobs** (re-check of v0.45/v0.53 "`tc_slip` 2–3.5 no gain, more slip is not faster" and v0.43 "flat `tc_slip` 3.5–6 slides 25–36 km/h at the flick"): suite 1 `tc_slip_straight` 6/4 76.347/76.355, `tc_hold` 0.7 76.266 (max 0.987), `tc_slip` 3 76.196. On all 30 (base 76.335, max 0.923): `tc_slip` 2.75 76.348, 3 76.283 (max 0.977), 3.5 76.243 (0.967), 4 76.204 (0.928), **4.5 76.184 (0.926)**, 5 76.227 (0.924), 3.5 + `tc_slip_straight` 4 76.197 (0.946): a plateau 4–5, all 0 of 30 off. The old conclusions no longer hold: since v0.43–v0.55 the flick has the full-lock throttle limit (v0.50) and the slide guard, so the corner limit could rise; the flick slide is 17 km/h (was 16), not 25–36. Unperturbed laps on the plateau 76.11–76.21 (4: 76.214, 4.5: 76.168, 5: 76.112): chaotic, so chosen on the suite mean. Not a line mechanism: the only measured gain this iteration. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 76.130/76.265/76.157 (all-30 −0.15 s), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.926; unperturbed lap 76.168 (+0.04 s, within the ±0.2 s chaos). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.16 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 61 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_192848.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **76.168 s** → on screen 1:16:16: **0.04 s slower** than v0.62 (76.132) on the unperturbed lap. Suites **76.130/76.265/76.157** (v0.62 76.285/76.383/76.337: **−0.16/−0.12/−0.18 s**; all-30 76.335 → **76.184**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.926/0.922/0.885** (v0.62 0.872/0.923/0.865). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.802** at ~2,477 m, the flick steering flip; v0.62 0.793). Traction-control cut time 16.9 → 13.6 s, brake time 14.8 → 15.0 s. Section changes vs v0.62: ~700 m −0.04, ~2,800 m −0.04; ~800 m +0.09, ~100 m +0.07, ~1,500 m +0.06, ~2,400 m +0.06. **Width use (entry −80 m / slowest point / exit +80 m, + = outside):** 448 m +0.10/+0.19/+0.28, 800 m +0.09/+0.18/+0.26, 1,040 m +0.27/−0.51/+0.14, 1,520 m +0.12/−0.39/−0.20, 1,930 m +0.28/−0.19/−0.05, flick +0.36/−0.80/−0.03, 2,986 m +0.11/−0.46/−0.04, hairpin +0.19/+0.71/+0.48. | **Learned** | Exits are traction-limited, not line-limited: 14 s of each lap had the traction control cutting below the plan's speed. The 2.5 m/s corner limit was measured on the v0.28 car; with the full-lock limit, slide guard and smoothed plan since then, 4–5 m/s is a plateau (−0.11 to −0.15 s over 30 laps) and the flick slide grows only 1 km/h. Pure pursuit as such is not a new line: linearised, the present heading + line terms already aim at a line point ~57 m ahead, and a speed-scaled look-ahead (T 0.7–1.5 s) was slower or left the track at the flick; the line's remaining time is in *where* the target is (entry from the centre at 1,520/1,930 m, flick entry from the inside), not in how it is followed. Crediting the plan with the car's offset toward the outside gives nothing (76.29–76.39). |

## v0.64 — Brake touch keeps the throttle: no restart from zero after a light touch

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.64 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.64`). New knobs `touch_brake` **0.15** (line 592) and `touch_keep` **0.7** (line 593). When the car brakes with a pedal lighter than `touch_brake`, the stored throttle (`c.throttle`) is set to `touch_keep` × its value before the touch (lines 804, 862–863) instead of 0; nothing is sent while braking. Heavier braking still zeroes it as before. No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.63 first** (`runs/run_20261002_192848.csv`). (a) **448 m bend** (415–525 m, 100–112 km/h, \|steer\| 0.5–0.6): the car rides the plan and every 1–3 km/h excess gives a 0.05–0.15 brake touch (428, 471, 485 m) that zeroes the stored throttle, which climbs back at +0.05/step: a ~0.3 s brake/throttle sawtooth; the v0.51 lift band is only ~0.35 km/h wide at 105 km/h. 166 brake steps per lap are touches under 0.15 pedal. (b) **Hairpin**: the car moves inside (+0.15) while braking, then slides to the outside (−0.72) at full lock: its slowest point is on the outside. (c) **Flick**: both parts entered from their inside (+0.44 before the left part, −0.80 at the flip into the right part). **Candidates (`tools/suite.py`, suite 1 base 76.130).** (1) **Own: inside target held off while braking** (aimed at (b); the phase is pulled toward 0 or +0.5 by last step's brake): k 0.5 76.708, k 1 78.407 (2 off), hold +0.5 79.849 (3 off), full only at brake 0.6 77.677: moving to the inside while braking pays, as v0.62 found for pulls in general (confirmed, also at the hairpin: its exit got wider, 0.90). (2) **Re-check of the 60/20 m phase**: start 50/70/80 m 76.215/76.328/76.377 (80: 1 off), width 30 76.136, 70/30 76.078 (1 off): a local optimum. (3) **Own, new: short-sight end-of-sight floor** (`corner_speed` falls to 66–70 km/h as the road ahead shrinks below ~10–40 m, so the flick and hairpin plan lower than medium bends): at `corner_speed` 76 the suite max \|trackPos\| fell 0.926 → 0.74–0.78 (flick 0.54–0.64) for +0.05–0.18 s; spent on `corner_speed` 78–80 (Optuna `hp64`, 40 trials, top 5 on all 30) the best were 76.068/76.069/76.086, **1 of 30 off each** (hairpin exit, `brake_margin` −2); safer shapes 76.186–76.435 (0–1 off); spent instead on `brake_aero` 0.0065 / `brake_max` 30 / `brake_margin` 12 / `turn_grip` 7.3 / `grip_boost` 0.4 / `corner_speed` 77: 76.07–76.34 with 0–9 of 10 off. The margin is real but the hairpin exit takes the next speed step. (4) **Own, re-check of v0.45 "throttle memory over brake touches: no gain"**: since then the lift band (v0.51) and the smoothed plan (v0.57) removed most touches, leaving only light ones, so the old reason may no longer hold. Suite 1: keep 0.7 below 0.1/0.2 pedal 75.993/76.025, 0.9 below 0.1 76.289, 0.5 below 0.2 76.200, 0.8 below 0.3 76.090. On all 30 (base 76.184): 0.1/0.7 **76.079** (max 0.955), 0.15/0.7 **76.099** (0.947), 0.2/0.7 76.101 (0.944), 0.1/0.6 76.164; Optuna `bk64` (30 trials, top 4 on all 30): 0.05/0.64 76.157, 0.23/0.53 76.164, 0.08/0.63 76.115: a plateau; chosen 0.15/0.7 (best harness score, max under 0.95). (5) Knob nudges, suite 1: `tc_gain` 0.4/0.7 76.096/76.299, `tc_slip_steer` 0.5/0.9 76.232/76.181, `tc_hold` 0.7 76.104 (max 0.99), `lift_v0` 60 76.084, `lift_pct` 2 76.090 (1 off): noise level, so v0.63's suggestion to re-tune the traction knobs around `tc_slip` 4.5 gives nothing. Not a line mechanism: the line trials (1)–(3) were all slower or unsafe. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 76.049/76.180/76.066 (all-30 −0.09 s), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.947; unperturbed lap 76.162 (−0.01 s). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.16 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_194948.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **76.162 s** → on screen 1:16:16: 0.01 s faster than v0.63 (76.168) on the unperturbed lap (v0.62's 76.132 is still the best single lap). Suites **76.049/76.180/76.066** (v0.63 76.130/76.265/76.157: **−0.08/−0.09/−0.09 s**; all-30 76.184 → **76.099**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.924/0.947/0.940** (v0.63 0.926/0.922/0.885). Brake time 15.2 → 14.9 s (35 → 36 applications; steps with a touch under 0.15 pedal 166 → 155); traction-control cut time 14.7 → 16.5 s (more throttle after the touches meets the traction limit). Section changes vs v0.63: ~800 m −0.11, ~1,500 m −0.09; ~2,900 m +0.08, ~3,000 m +0.07, ~3,300 m +0.04. **Width use (entry −80 m / slowest point / exit +80 m, + = outside):** 449 m +0.12/+0.17/+0.32, 786 m +0.16/−0.02/+0.27, 1,040 m +0.27/−0.50/+0.14, 1,525 m +0.12/−0.46/−0.05, 1,929 m +0.21/−0.18/+0.01, flick +0.39/−0.87/−0.05, 2,980 m +0.12/−0.16/−0.01, hairpin +0.16/+0.71/+0.48. Top speed 275.1 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.802 → **0.874**, slide 17.1 → 19.7 km/h, exit 7.6 → **17.7**; kink 9.0 → 8.7 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.7 → 60.2 km/h, \|tp\| 0.72 → 0.71; largest slide elsewhere 17.9 km/h (~509 m). Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — faster on all three perturbation suites (−0.08/−0.09/−0.09 s, all-30 76.184 → 76.099), 0 of 30 off, unperturbed lap −0.01 s; cost: suite max 0.926 → 0.947 and the unperturbed flick 0.80 → 0.87 (exit slide 7.6 → 17.7 km/h) |
| **Learned** | The light brake touches the lift band leaves still cost time: zeroing the stored throttle for a 0.05–0.15 pedal touch made the car climb back from 0 at +0.05/step. Keeping ~70 % of it is worth ~0.09 s over 30 laps (plateau 0.05–0.3 pedal × 0.5–0.7 kept; 0.9 kept is slower: the touch must still take some throttle off). v0.45's "no gain" was measured before the lift band and the smoothed plan. Line findings: moving inside while braking is worth time (holding the inside target off while braking: +0.6 to +2.3 s on suite 1, off track at 1.0); the 60/20 m phase is a local optimum; a lower end-of-sight floor for very short sight (flick, hairpin) buys a large flick margin (suite max 0.93 → 0.74–0.78) but the next `corner_speed` step then fails at the hairpin exit (1 of 30 off), so the hairpin exit, not the flick, is the next limit for a short-sight-aware plan. The flick exit slide grew (7.6 → 17.7 km/h on the unperturbed lap): watch it. |

## v0.65 — Upshift hold: no downshift in the clutch dip right after an upshift

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.65 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.65`). New knob `upshift_hold` **15** steps (~0.3 s, line 568). For that many steps after an upshift no downshift is made unless the car is braking (lines 877–880; `c.up_t` counts the steps since the last upshift). No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.64 first** (`runs/run_20261002_194948.csv`). (a) **Gear hunts (new):** two 2→3→2→3 hunts per lap, at 2,543 m (flick exit) and 3,323 m (hairpin exit, start of the finish straight). After the 2→3 upshift at 18,600 rpm the engine reads ~14,700, then dips to 11,669/11,745 for 1–2 steps while the clutch engages; the downshift rule predicts 2nd at 11,745 × 2.9/2.3 = 14,800 < `downshift_rpm` 15,000 and shifts back down, then up again 8–10 m later: two extra shifts at full throttle per hunt. (b) **Hairpin:** full lock (steer 1.0) for ~20 m (3,260–3,283 m) while speed bleeds 76 → 60 km/h with the throttle held at 0.1 and the car sliding from +0.17 to −0.71 (outside); the exit is then traction-limited (sent throttle 0.26–0.97 in 2nd). (c) The flick and hairpin both need full lock. **Candidates** (`tools/suite.py`, base v0.64 all-30 76.099). (1) **Own, new: upshift hold** (chosen): hold 8/15/30 steps 76.081/76.081/76.079, 0 of 30 off, max 0.947 (unchanged); with downshifts still allowed while braking: identical (76.081). Per run: unperturbed −0.302 s, `corner_speed` −2 −0.11, `upshift_rpm` −200 −0.08; `downshift_rpm` +500 +0.12, `tc_slip` +0.2 +0.05 (downstream chaos: none of these hunts are braking downshifts). (2) **Own, new: steering cap at low speed** (front tyres at 21° may be past their peak slip angle at the hairpin): cap 0.6–0.9 below 120 km/h or 0.8–0.9 below 80 km/h: **10 of 10 off** at the flick (~2,483 m); cap 0.8 below 72 km/h: 10 of 10 off (hairpin exit \|tp\| 1.09). Both corners are steering-limited: full lock is needed. (3) **Shift points re-checked with the hold** (the up-200 run gained): `upshift_rpm` 18,400 76.171 (max 0.955), 18,200 76.288; `downshift_rpm` 14,500 76.132 (1 off, hairpin exit): v0.54's points stay. (4) Hairpin line (orchestrator lead): not trialled this iteration beyond (2); v0.64 already measured holding the inside target off while braking (slower) and full-width exits (off at the hairpin exit, v0.62). Challenged: v0.54's shift-point rule assumed the rpm reading is the gear's rpm; it is not during the shift. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 76.015/76.161/76.066 (all-30 −0.02 s), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.947 unchanged; unperturbed lap 75.860 (−0.30 s). Reject on damage or leaving the track. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.86 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 60 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_195816.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **75.860 s** → on screen 1:15:86: **0.30 s faster** than v0.64 (76.162) and the best single lap so far (v0.62 76.132). Suites **76.015/76.161/76.066** (v0.64 76.049/76.180/76.066: **−0.03/−0.02/−0.00 s**; all-30 76.099 → **76.081**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.924/0.947/0.940** (unchanged). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.874** at ~2,477 m, the flick steering flip; unchanged). Shifts per lap 45 → 41 (both hunts gone: single 2→3 at 2,543 and 3,324 m). Section changes vs v0.64: ~2,900 m −0.12, ~3,000 m −0.06, ~3,300 m −0.04, ~3,100 m −0.02, ~3,400 m −0.02, ~2,600 m −0.02. Top speed 275.1 km/h. **Watch points:** flick 0.874 (slide 19.7, exit 17.7 km/h), kink 8.7 km/h, hairpin minimum 60.2 km/h, \|tp\| 0.71, largest slide elsewhere 17.9 km/h (~509 m): all unchanged up to the first hunt at 2,543 m. Slowest corner 60 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — removes a deterministic defect (two gear hunts per lap), unperturbed lap −0.30 s, suites −0.03/−0.02/−0.00 s (all-30 76.099 → 76.081: inside the noise, but not slower), 0 of 30 off, suite max unchanged |
| **Learned** | The engine rpm reading dips ~3,000 rpm for 1–2 steps after an upshift (clutch), so an rpm-predicted downshift rule hunts 2-3-2-3 whenever the post-shift dip lands under `downshift_rpm` × 2.3/2.9 ≈ 11,900: worth 0.30 s on a lap where it happens twice, but whether it happens depends on the exact rpm at the shift, so over 30 perturbed laps the gain averages only −0.02 s. A low-speed steering cap is dead: the flick and the hairpin are steering-limited (full lock needed; every cap 0.6–0.9 left the track). `upshift_rpm` 18,400/18,200 and `downshift_rpm` 14,500 are still worse with the hold. |

## v0.66 — Sharp-bend phase: a later inside point for bends first seen with little road (rejected)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.66 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.66`; **rejected, code restored to v0.65**). Tried: new knobs `sharp_det` **30** m and `sharp_k` **0.5**. A bend whose bend side is first detected with less than `sharp_det` m of road along the track direction (`c.road_det`, the hairpin: ~29 m) gets its inside phase scaled by `sharp_k`: `phase = clip((road − 60k)/(20k), −1, 1)`, centre 60 → 30 m, width 20 → 10 m. No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.65 first** (`runs/run_20261002_195816.csv`). (a) **Hairpin** (left): the car arrives at the centre (+0.05 at −80 m); the 2° beam asymmetry reads the correct side (left) clearly from ~3,130 m (−4 to −9 m), but the set-up only pulls while > 95 m of road is visible and is gated off by its own steering after 1–2 steps, so it fires for a few steps at 3,163–3,169 m only. The bend is detected at 29 m of road (3,235 m, 137 km/h), already at phase −1, so the car moves inside (+0.20) while braking, reaches full lock at +0.08 and 85 km/h, and slides out to −0.71 while scrubbing 76 → 60 km/h at throttle 0.1–0.2. (b) **448 m bend:** the inside target is +0.5 but the car runs out from +0.17 to −0.47 at \|steer\| 0.55 while the throttle climbs (slide 10–14 km/h): the throttle, not the steering, sets the line there. (c) Every candidate that changed the approach to the flick left the track there (the flick is the cliff). **Candidates, all line mechanisms except (6)–(7)** (`tools/suite.py`, suite 1 base 76.015, all-30 76.081). (1) **Own: set-up gated on the steering minus its own pull** (so it stays on instead of chattering), gain 0.5/0.2/0.1: 76.054 (2 off) / 76.171 / 76.187: re-confirms v0.61 (the chatter is not the cost). (2) **Own: set-up held through braking** (pull continues while the last brake > 0.2 and > 30 m of road): 76.476 (1 off); with the own-pull gate and gain 0.2: 76.555. Its p0 lap was +0.75 s, mostly *not* at the hairpin (hairpin entry at −0.32 instead of 0: ±0.02 s): the outside entry is worth nothing there; the loss is in other braking zones (~600, ~1,100, ~3,600 m). (3) **Own, new: line-error throttle cap** (on the inside half of a bend, throttle ≤ 1 − k·(error − e0) while the car is wider than the target, aimed at (b)): k 1/2/3, e0 0.1–0.5 → 76.060–76.529: the throttle is worth more than the line. (4) **Own, new: sharp-bend phase (chosen to record)**: `sharp_det` 31–40 × `sharp_k` 0.3–0.7 on suite 1: 35/0.3 **5 of 10 off** (hairpin exit, \|tp\| 1.76), 35/0.5 76.557, 40/0.7 76.062, 31/0.4–0.7 75.977–76.034, 30/0.5 **75.969**, 32–33/0.5 75.972. On all 30: 30/0.5 **76.103** (0 off, max 0.944), 31/0.5 76.158, 32/0.5 76.184, 32/0.5 + `corner_speed` 77 76.192 (1 off, `brake_margin` −2). (5) **Own, new: look-ahead weight exponent** (the `aim` bearing weights beams by d^p, p = 2): p 1.5 77.284; p 2.5/3 on-track laps 75.91/76.32 but **5–9 of 10 off at the flick**; p 2.5–3 only above 80–150 km/h, or only while \|steer\| < 0.05–0.3: 2–8 of 10 off, always at the flick (~2,470–2,495 m); with the v0.64 short-sight floor (`corner_speed` −8 km/h below 40 m of road) still 6 of 10 off. A strong lever (−0.1 s on the laps that stay on) that moves the flick approach line off the track. (6) **Short-sight floor + sharp phase + `corner_speed` 78–80** (the orchestrator's hairpin lead): 76.255–76.490 on suite 1 (floor alone 76.195, max 0.739; + 78/80 without the phase 76.300/76.483): the flick margin bought by the floor does not pay for itself. (7) **Fallback, non-line: faster throttle ramp after braking** (+0.05–0.2 per step extra, scaled by the deficit to the allowed speed, faded with \|steer\|): 76.241–76.422, **2–6 of 10 off** at the flick. **Challenged:** the inherited lead that the hairpin entry from the outside is worth time (orchestrator note, v0.64): with the car at −0.32 instead of 0 at turn-in the hairpin section changed by ±0.02 s; the hairpin is steering-limited at full lock wherever it starts. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 75.969/76.202/76.139 (all-30 +0.02 s vs v0.65), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.944; unperturbed lap 75.776 (−0.08 s). Reject if the suites are slower (the suite mean decides, v0.63). |

Code restored to v0.65 (`git checkout v0.65 -- snakeoil3_v1.py`). |
| **Learned** | No line mechanism beat v0.65 this iteration (seven tried). At the hairpin the line is not the limit: turning in from −0.32 or with a later inside point changes the section by ±0.02 s; the car is steering-limited at full lock (v0.65) and the hairpin is seen only ~29 m out. The look-ahead bearing's d² weighting is a strong, untried lever: d^2.5 makes the laps that stay on ~0.1 s faster, but every form tried (global, speed-gated, steering-gated, with a short-sight floor) leaves the track at the flick in 2–9 of 10 runs, so it needs the flick handled first. Holding the set-up through braking zones costs time in other braking zones (v0.62's finding holds), and a throttle cap for running wide of the inside target is slower (the throttle is worth more than the line; v0.63's "exits are traction-limited" holds). A faster post-braking throttle ramp leaves the track at the flick (re-confirms v0.57). |

## v0.67 — No drive at full lock near the outside edge: flick margin (enabling change)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.67 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.67`). `lock_throttle_edge` **0.1 → 0** (line 587, comment updated): at full lock with the outside edge `lock_room_near` (0.3) or closer the throttle limit is now 0 (was 0.1), rising linearly to `lock_throttle` (0.2) at `lock_room_far` (0.8) of room, as before (formula lines 828–833 unchanged). One mechanism (v0.50's full-lock edge limit). No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.65 first** (`runs/run_20261002_195816.csv`, plus all 30 suite runs kept and split into flick zones: left arc 2,430–2,472 m, steering flip 2,472–2,492, right part 2,492–2,540, hairpin 3,250–3,400). (a) The flick's left part is a full-lock arc (2,448–2,472 m): the car enters from the inside (+0.45) at 86 km/h and **drifts across the whole width to −0.87** while scrubbing to 67 km/h at the 0.1 edge throttle; the steering then flips (+1 → −1 in ~6 steps, slide 19.7 km/h) with the car already at the edge. Suite maxima per zone: arc 0.88, flip 0.95, hairpin 0.92. The flip-zone maximum is the arc's end point (\|tp\| barely moves during the flip), so **v0.56's conclusion "the flick maximum is at the steering flip, not on the full-lock arc" is challenged**: the arc sets it. (b) Every faster change of batch 5 failed in these zones: `corner_speed` 77 → 1 of 30 off (`brake_margin` −2: −1.085 at 2,480 m, arc drift, then throttle 0.5–0.8 during the flip); `aim` exponent d^2.5 → **15 of 30 off** (turns in ~7 m earlier and reaches full lock while still braking at 0.5, 105 km/h: the fronts saturate and the arc drifts off by ~2,463 m). **Candidates** (`tools/suite.py`, all 30 each; base v0.65 76.081, max 0.947, 0 off). (1) **Chosen: `lock_throttle_edge` 0** (only 0.05 had been tried, on v0.49 as a knob nudge, 79.549): all-30 76.189 (+0.11 s), max **0.845** (arc 0.82, flip 0.84, hairpin 0.84); 0.05: 76.147, max 0.905. (2) **Own, new: lock hold** (the full-lock throttle cap decays from its last value instead of releasing as the steering passes through 0 at the flip): hold 0.8/0.9/0.95 per step → 76.064/76.071/76.165, max 0.934/0.991/0.963; 0.9 + `corner_speed` 77 → 2 off. (3) **Own, new: brake limit at full lock** (pedal × (1 − k·lock), friction circle for the fronts): k 0.5/0.8 → 76.109/76.044, **1 off** each (flick); with d^2.5: 10/9 of 30 off. (4) **Own, new: edge-drift throttle cap** (throttle falls to 0.1 from \|tp\| 0.8 to 0.95 while \|tp\| grows, whatever the steering): 76.091/76.083 (from 0.8/0.7), max 0.947 unchanged; + `corner_speed` 77: 1 off. (5) **Own, new: sight-gated `aim` exponent** (d^2 → d^2.5 only with > 60/100/140 m of road along the track): 15 off / 76.092 1 off / 76.103 max 0.950; exponent +1 from 100 m: 76.141 1 off: the exponent's gain is in the short-sight corners, where it fails. (6) Co-tuning the full-lock profile with edge 0 (same mechanism): `lock_throttle` 0.25/0.3 → 76.211/76.127 (max 0.967/0.915), `lock_room_near` 0.2 → 76.155 (0.888): none recovers the time, so the edge value alone is recorded. **Unlock measured (follow-up, not part of v0.67):** on top of edge 0, `corner_speed` 77/78/79/80 → 76.157 (max 0.902) / 76.059 (0.967) / **75.970** (75.959/75.960/75.991, **0 of 30 off**, max 0.994 at the hairpin exit, flick 0.96) / 75.965 (5 off). Without it `corner_speed` 79 is **14 of 30 off** (all at the flick but 2), and edge 0.05 + 79 is 5 off; d^2.5 with edge 0: 5 of 30 off (76.129 on-track; was 15). |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): suites 76.102/76.284/76.181 (all-30 +0.11 s vs v0.65), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.845 (was 0.947). Kept only as an enabling change: `corner_speed` 79 on top measured all-30 75.970 (−0.11 s vs v0.65) with 0 of 30 off, which is 14 of 30 off without it. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.20 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 274 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_202707.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.202 s** → on screen 1:16:20: **0.34 s slower** than v0.65 (75.860). Suites **76.102/76.284/76.181** (v0.65 76.015/76.161/76.066: **+0.09/+0.12/+0.12 s**; all-30 76.081 → **76.189**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.836/0.845/0.796** (v0.65 0.924/0.947/0.940). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.794** at ~2,476 m, the flick steering flip; v0.65 0.874). Section changes vs v0.65: ~1,000 m +0.22 (largest slide 21.5 km/h at ~1,017 m), ~2,500 m +0.08, ~3,200 m +0.04, ~2,400 m −0.04, ~3,000 m −0.03; the rest within ±0.02. **Watch points:** flick 0.874 → 0.794, slide 19.7 → 14.2 km/h, exit slide 17.7 → 6.4 km/h; kink 8.7 → 8.6 km/h; hairpin minimum 60.2 → 57.3 km/h, \|tp\| 0.71 → 0.68. Top speed 274.1 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — enabling change: +0.11 s on the suites (all-30 76.081 → 76.189) and +0.34 s unperturbed, but the suite maximum falls 0.947 → 0.845 (flick 0.95 → 0.84, hairpin 0.92 → 0.84), which is what makes `corner_speed` 79 safe: measured on top, all-30 **75.970** (−0.11 s vs v0.65, −0.22 s vs v0.67), 0 of 30 off (14 of 30 off without this change). If the follow-up does not hold on its own version, reconsider both (rule 8). |
| **Learned** | At the flick the car's \|trackPos\| maximum is set on the left full-lock arc (inside +0.45 → outside −0.87 over 24 m), not by the steering flip, so throttle on the arc near the edge is what costs the margin: 0.1 of throttle there is worth ~0.10 of track width over 30 perturbed laps (and ~0.11 s). The margin pays only when spent on the plan's end-of-sight speed: `corner_speed` 76 → 79 is 0 of 30 off with it, 14 of 30 without; 80 is the next cliff (5 off). With `corner_speed` 79 the limit moves from the flick to the hairpin exit (suite max 0.994 at ~3,281 m), so the hairpin exit is the next margin to buy. The `aim` exponent d^2.5 fails differently (full lock while still braking hard at the flick turn-in: the fronts saturate), so edge 0 only cuts its off count 15 → 5; a friction-circle pedal limit at full lock does not fix it (9–10 off). |

## v0.68 — End-of-sight speed 79 km/h: v0.67's follow-up

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.68 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.68`). `corner_speed` **76 → 79** km/h (line 537, comment updated): the speed the braking plan (road ahead and every sharpness beam) must be able to slow to by the end of the visible road. No other line or knob changed. One mechanism (the plan's end-of-sight speed); the v0.67 enabling change (`lock_throttle_edge` 0) is what makes it safe. |
| **Why** | **Follow-up of v0.67 (rule 8), value chosen on evidence. Own analysis of v0.67 first** (`runs/run_20261002_202707.csv`, plus the worst suite run of `corner_speed` 79, `brake_margin` −2, kept and traced). (a) At both short-sight corners the plan ends at exactly `corner_speed`: once the road ahead is below `brake_margin` (15 m) the allowed speed *is* `corner_speed`, so the car brakes until it is just under it and then sits at full lock with the throttle near 0. **Hairpin** (worst run): turn-in at 101 km/h still braking, full lock from ~3,258 m at +0.09, then a 12 m drift across the whole width to −0.99 at ~3,281 m while scrubbing 77 → 57 km/h. **Flick:** the same pattern (full lock at 97 km/h, +0.21 → −0.94 by ~2,474 m). So `corner_speed` buys later braking on the approaches; the in-corner speed is set by the steering, not by the plan. (b) The section gains of 79 vs v0.65 are spread over the braking zones (100–1,900 m, 2,300–2,400 m: −0.02 to −0.04 each); the flick (+0.08) and hairpin (+0.04/+0.02) lose a little. **Candidates** (`tools/suite.py`, all 30 each; v0.65 76.081, v0.67 76.189; a variant with three new trial knobs). (1) **Follow-up `corner_speed` 78/79** (re-measured, deterministic): 78 → 76.059 (max 0.967), **79 → 75.970** (75.959/75.960/75.991, 0 of 30 off, max 0.994 in suite 2, `brake_margin` −2, hairpin exit). (2) **Short-sight floor** (v0.64's idea: `corner_speed` lowered by up to Δ as the road ahead falls below a threshold; aimed at the hairpin exit so 80+ is safe): 8 km/h below 40 m (v0.64's shape) + 79/80/81 → 76.165/76.212/76.178 (81: 1 off); 4 km/h below 20 m + 79 → 75.947 (max 0.993), 5 below 20 m + 80 → 76.008, 2 off; very short (6–10 km/h below 12 m) + 79 → 75.978/75.979 (max 0.993/0.982), + 80 → 4 of 30 off each; 10 below 15 m + 80 → 76.013 (max 0.977); 12 below 15 m + 81 → 3 off. The floor does not buy hairpin-exit margin for free: either it costs the approach speed it allows, or 80 still leaves the track (the full-lock drift starts before the floor acts). (3) **Own, new: separate end-of-sight speed for the sharpness beams** (beams plan to `corner_speed` + Δ): road 76/beams 79 → 76.005 (0 off, max 0.977), beams 81 → 76.014, 2 off; road 78/beams 80 → 76.039, 1 off; road 79/beams 80 → 76.028, 2 off; road 79/beams 76 → 76.168; road 80/beams 77–78 → 76.186/76.112 (1 off): the gain needs both plans raised together. (4) **Own robustness check beyond the standard suites** (suites 1–2 on shifted bases `brake_margin` 14 and `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs each): v0.65 4 of 40 off, v0.67 (76) 0, `corner_speed` 77 0 (max 0.992), 78 2, 79 5, floors with 79 1–5. **Challenged:** the inherited reading that 78 is the safer choice by a useful margin: on the 40 shifted runs 78 also leaves the track (2 of 40, at `brake_margin` 12, like 79), 79 is about as robust as the accepted v0.65 (5 vs 4 of 40), and 78's suite gain over v0.65 (−0.02 s) is inside the noise. 80 is the cliff (5 of 30 off). Chosen: 79 alone (the floors and the beam split add knobs without a measured gain). |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`): suites 75.959/75.960/75.991 (all-30 **75.970**, −0.11 s vs v0.65, −0.22 s vs v0.67), 0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.933/0.994/0.908; unperturbed lap 75.710 (−0.15 s vs v0.65). |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.71 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_210408.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **75.710 s** → on screen 1:15:71: **new best**, 0.15 s faster than v0.65 (75.860) and 0.49 s faster than v0.67 (76.202). Suites **75.959/75.960/75.991** (v0.65 76.015/76.161/76.066: **−0.06/−0.20/−0.08 s**; all-30 76.081 → **75.970**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.933/0.994/0.908** (v0.67 0.836/0.845/0.796; the 0.994 is suite 2's `brake_margin` −2 run at the hairpin exit, ~3,281 m). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.815** at ~3,281 m, the hairpin exit). Section changes vs v0.65: 200, 2,300 and 2,400 m −0.04 each; 100, 700, 800, 900, 1,100, 1,600, 1,900 and 3,100 m −0.02 each; 2,500 m +0.08, 3,200 m +0.04, 2,600/2,700/3,300 m +0.02. vs v0.67: ~1,000 m −0.22 (v0.67's 21.5 km/h slide at ~1,017 m is gone), the rest within ±0.05. **Watch points:** flick 0.794 → 0.784, slide 14.2 → 12.4 km/h, exit slide 6.4 → 5.8 km/h; kink 8.6 → 8.9 km/h; hairpin minimum 57.3 → 57.0 km/h, \|tp\| 0.68 → 0.81; largest slide elsewhere 18.3 km/h (~510 m). Top speed 275.3 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — all-30 75.970 vs v0.65's 76.081 (−0.11 s) and v0.67's 76.189 (−0.22 s), 0 of 30 off; the v0.67 enabling change is recovered and beaten, so both stay (rule 8). The margin is thin at the hairpin exit (suite max 0.994). |
| **Learned** | The end-of-sight speed buys time only through later braking on the approaches; at the flick and the hairpin the car is steering-limited at full lock and drifts across the full width whatever the plan says (allowed = `corner_speed` once the road ahead is under `brake_margin`). With `lock_throttle_edge` 0 the cliff is between 79 and 80 (80: 5 of 30 off) and the limit is now the hairpin exit, as v0.67 found. A short-sight floor does not move that cliff (80 + floor: 2–4 of 30 off, or slower), so the next margin must come from how the car turns at full lock (turn-in speed, or the drift at the hairpin), not from the end-of-sight speed. Splitting the end-of-sight speed between the road-ahead plan and the sharpness beams gains nothing: both have to rise together. Beyond the standard suites (shifted `brake_margin`/`line_offset` bases, 40 runs) 79 is as robust as v0.65 (5 vs 4 of 40 off); 76–77 are the most robust (0 of 40) but slower. |

## v0.69 — Launch traction: no traction-control cut on the standing start (rejected)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.69 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.69`). New knobs `launch_v` **130** km/h and `launch_slip` **25** m/s (lines 597–598): from the standing start until the car first reaches `launch_v`, the traction-control over-speed limit is raised by `launch_slip` (in practice no cut); lines 862–868 (`c.launch`, latched off for the rest of the run). One mechanism (launch traction). No other line or knob changed. **Rejected: code restored to v0.68.** |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.68 first** (`runs/run_20261002_210408.csv`). (a) The timed lap starts from standstill (curLapTime 0 at ~3,598 m) and the first 100 m take 4.36 s. In 1st–2nd gear the engine sits at 5,200–12,000 rpm, far below its 16–18k torque peak, and traction control cycles the throttle 1.0 → 0.2 every ~7 steps (rear over-speed 1 ↔ 10 m/s); the same cycle runs at the hairpin exit (57 → 137 km/h in 2nd, ~3,285–3,330 m) and in medium exits (448 m, 1,500 m, 2,700 m): 4.1 throttle-seconds cut per lap. (b) Hairpin: the line pulls the car inside (−0.17 → +0.17) under braking at 146 km/h (3,233–3,250 m) before it turns in at full lock at 97 km/h, still braking (0.2), then drifts to −0.81; flick: full lock at 91 km/h, +0.13 → −0.75. Not trialled here (v0.66 measured an outside hairpin entry at ±0.02 s). **Candidates** (single runs on v0.68 with section times, then `tools/suite.py` all 30; base 75.970, 0 off, max 0.994). (1) **TC dynamics** (single runs): `tc_gain` 0 (no TC at all): first 200 m **5.900 s vs 6.098**, hairpin exit to 3,500 m −0.07 s, but the 448 m exit +0.43 s (lap 76.886); `tc_gain` 0.25/1.0 76.296/76.194; `tc_hold` 0.5/0.9 75.920/76.068; `tc_slip` 3 **off** (1.012 at the flick), 6 75.876. (2) **Straight-line TC relief** (`tc_slip_straight` 5 → 10/15/20/40, all 30): 75.977 (1 off) / 76.072 (3 off) / 76.097 (0 off) / 76.081 (2 off): the launch and hairpin-exit gain is lost at the flick exit and in medium exits. (3) **Own, new: launch traction (chosen)**: no-TC launch until 90/130/180 km/h → all-30 75.903 (1 off) / 75.837 (2) / 75.858 (1); extra slip `launch_slip` 5/10/15/20/25/30/40 until 130 km/h → 76.000 (0) / 75.928 (2) / 75.979 (2) / 75.967 (1) / **75.815 (1)** / 75.837 (2) / 75.837 (2); 25 until 80/100 km/h → 75.870 (2) / 75.957 (0, max 0.995); 100 until 60 → 75.932 (1). Deterministic part (single runs, no-TC launch to 130 vs v0.68): −0.04 s at 5 m, **−0.18 s at 100 m**, −0.12 s at 400 m (it arrives faster at the ~200 m bend and lifts), −0.07 s carried from 700 to 2,000 m. Without the cut the wheels spin up to ~40 % and the rpm-based gearbox shifts 1→2 at 32 km/h and 2→3 at 47 km/h, with up to 10 km/h of sideways speed in 3rd at ~120 km/h on the start straight. **Inherited conclusions challenged:** v0.46's "launch slip: no gain" (measured on v0.45) does not hold: the launch is 0.18 s faster to 100 m with no cut. v0.53's "less slip at low speed is not faster" holds and extends: at the start more slip is faster (the wheelspin works as a slipping clutch that revs the engine into its torque peak). v0.68's "79 is as robust as v0.65": its 0 of 30 sits on an edge (see Learned). |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly: all-30 75.815 (−0.155 s vs v0.68), but 1 of 30 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.57 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_211743.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`; rerun byte-identical); TORCS time **75.570 s** → on screen 1:15:57, 0.14 s faster than v0.68 (75.710): section 0 m −0.156, 2,500 m −0.060, 2,400 m +0.060, the rest within ±0.03. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.857** at ~2,474 m, the flick; hairpin 0.85, 57.0 km/h). Watch points: flick 0.784 → 0.857, slide 12.4 → 16.3 km/h, exit slide 5.8 → 10.0 km/h; kink 8.9 km/h; largest slide elsewhere 18.5 km/h (~508 m). Top speed 275.2 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). Suites **75.799/75.782/75.861** (v0.68 75.959/75.960/75.991; all-30 75.970 → **75.815**), max \|trackPos\| 0.971/**1.051**/0.986, **1 of 30 off**: suite 2's `line_gain` +0.1 run leaves the track at the flick (1.051 at ~2,479 m; the same perturbation on v0.68: 0.959 at ~2,475 m). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — 1 of 30 off (flick, `line_gain` +0.1), despite all-30 −0.155 s and a 0.14 s faster unperturbed lap. Code restored to v0.68 (rule 8). The only launch variant with 0 of 30 off (`launch_slip` 25 until 100 km/h) gains only 0.013 s on the suites (75.957, max 0.995) and is slower unperturbed (75.866), so it was not kept either. |
| **Learned** | The standing start is worth ~0.1 s per lap: with no traction-control cut until ~130 km/h the car reaches 100 m 0.18 s sooner and keeps ~0.07 s to the end of the lap; spin there helps (the engine revs into its torque peak). But the v0.68 margin is too thin to bank it: across 13 launch variants 17 of 390 perturbed runs left the track, always in the same four edge runs (`corner_speed` +2, `brake_margin` −2, `line_gain` +0.1, `lock_throttle` +0.05; flick and hairpin exit), although the launch changes nothing in those corners. On shifted bases (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs) v0.68 itself is 5 of 40 off, the launch 7–8 of 40. So v0.68's 0 of 30 is luck at an edge: any upstream change of the trajectory moves 1–2 of those runs over it. The launch gain is ready to take once flick/hairpin-exit margin is bought (a full-lock margin mechanism first, or `corner_speed` 78 with the launch). |
