## v0.70 — End-of-sight speed 78 km/h: margin for the launch (enabling change)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.70 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.70`). `corner_speed` **79 → 78** km/h (line 537, comment updated). No other line or knob changed. One mechanism (the plan's end-of-sight speed). **Enabling change (rule 8):** it buys the hairpin-exit/flick margin that v0.69's launch traction needs (measured on top, below); the launch itself is the next version. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.68 first** (`runs/run_20261002_210408.csv`, plus the edge runs `line_gain` +0.1, `brake_margin` −2, `corner_speed` +2 kept and traced). (a) At the hairpin and the flick's left arc the car reaches full lock and then drifts across the width while scrubbing speed: hairpin full lock at 3,258 m, 91 km/h, +0.10 → −0.81; flick 2,445 m, 91 km/h, +0.13 → −0.78. In the edge runs full lock comes at 94–98 km/h (hairpin) and the drift ends at −0.90/−0.93/−0.99; at the flick the onset position and pre-rotation (angle 0.04–0.15) matter as well (−0.86/−0.94/−0.95). Once at full lock the outcome is set: throttle is already 0 near the edge (v0.67) and the grip is used up. (b) Through the turn-in the car is ~10 km/h over the plan (brake 0.2–0.5, halved by ABS 3233–3262 m). **Candidates** (`tools/suite.py`, all 30; base 75.970, 0 off, max 0.994). (1) **Own, new: edge scrub brake** (brake `scrub_k`·(room deficit) at \|steer\| ≥ 0.95 while drifting toward the outside edge with room < `scrub_room`): k 0.2/room 0.5 76.251 (0 off, max 0.963), k 0.3/0.7 76.639, k 0.4/0.5 4 of 30 off, k 0.6/0.7 7 off (hairpin minimum 35–48 km/h, the car nearly stops): braking at full lock takes lateral grip one for one, the drift does not shrink. (2) **Own, new: steering-compensated ABS** (threshold `abs_ratio` − k·\|steer\|, the inner/slip-angled wheels read slow at turn-in): k 0.05/0.1/0.2/0.3 → 75.974/75.897/75.948 (1 off)/76.009, max 0.98–1.23: noise, no margin. (3) **Own, new: turn-in brake boost** (brake gain ×(1 + k·\|steer\|)): k 1/2/4 → 76.184/76.394/76.828, each 1 off (`brake_margin` −2, max 1.24–1.41). (4) **Full-lock throttle re-check** with edge 0: `lock_throttle` 0.1/0.15 → 76.004 (1 off)/75.988 (max 0.978); `lock_room_far` 1.0/1.2 → 75.992/75.986 (1 off): no margin. (5) **`corner_speed` 78 (chosen)**: alone 76.059 (0 off, max 0.967; shifted bases `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, suites 1–2, 40 runs: 2 off, v0.68 5). **With the v0.69 launch on top** (`launch_slip` 25 until `launch_v` 130): all-30 **75.882** (0 of 30 off, max 0.959; p0 75.650), shifted bases **1 of 40 off** (75.780/76.028); launch + 79: 1 of 30 and 8 of 40 off (v0.69). **Challenged:** v0.68's conclusion that 79 is about as robust as v0.65 and that 78 gains nothing in safety: with any upstream change (the launch) 79 tips 1–8 runs over the edge, 78 does not; and v0.65–v0.67's reading that the full-lock drift is a throttle/brake problem: neither less throttle nor any braking at or into full lock moved the suite maximum. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`): suites 76.026/76.092/76.059 (all-30 76.059, +0.09 s vs v0.68), 0 of 30 off, max 0.900/0.967/0.903; with the launch next: all-30 75.882 (−0.09 s vs v0.68), 0 of 30 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:16.07 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_213037.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.070 s** → on screen 1:16:07, 0.36 s slower than v0.68 (75.710; v0.68's lap was a fast draw of the chaos: its all-30 mean is 0.26 s above it, 78's only 0.01 s). Suites **76.026/76.092/76.059** (v0.68 75.959/75.960/75.991; all-30 75.970 → **76.059**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.900/0.967/0.903** (v0.68 0.933/0.994/0.908). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.790** at ~2,473 m, the flick). **Watch points:** flick 0.784 → 0.790, slide 12.4 → 6.2 km/h, exit slide 5.8 → 5.4 km/h; kink 8.9 → 8.8 km/h; hairpin minimum 57.0 → 57.1 km/h, \|tp\| 0.81 → 0.75; largest slide elsewhere 19.5 km/h (~507 m). Top speed 275.3 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — enabling change: all-30 +0.09 s vs v0.68, but 79 sits at an edge (v0.69: any upstream change tips 1–2 of 30 runs off), and 78 makes the measured launch gain bankable: 78 + launch all-30 75.882 (−0.09 s vs v0.68), 0 of 30 off, 1 of 40 on shifted bases (v0.68 5, launch + 79 8). If the launch version does not recover the time, reconsider both (rule 8). |
| **Learned** | At the hairpin and flick the full-lock drift is fixed by the state at full-lock onset (speed, position, pre-rotation); after it, nothing helps: braking at full lock removes lateral grip one for one (scrub brake: same max \|tp\|, hairpin 35–48 km/h), less full-lock throttle and steer-compensated ABS are noise, and more brake into the turn-in costs 0.2–0.9 s and still leaves at `brake_margin` −2. The cheapest margin is the end-of-sight speed itself: 78 vs 79 costs 0.09 s all-30 but moves the edge enough that the launch (worth ~0.18 s) fits with 0 of 30 off. Lowering the full-lock onset speed by braking harder at turn-in costs more than lowering `corner_speed`. |

## v0.71 — Launch traction on corner_speed 78: v0.70's follow-up

| Field | Detail |

New knobs `launch_v` **130** km/h and `launch_slip` **25** m/s (lines 597–598): from the standing start until the car first reaches `launch_v`, the traction-control over-speed limit is raised by `launch_slip` (in practice no cut); lines 862–869 (`c.launch`, latched off for the rest of the run). Same code as the rejected v0.69, now on v0.70's `corner_speed` 78. One mechanism (launch traction). No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.70 first** (`runs/run_20261002_213037.csv`, start to 160 m): the engine sits at 5,236 rpm (clutch) to ~18 km/h, then traction control chops the sent throttle 1.0 → 0.02–0.6 every few steps through 1st and 2nd (rear spin 6 ↔ 66 rad/s); 1st is held to 87 km/h (rpm read 19,642 under spin), 2nd to 128 km/h; 100 m at 4.36 s. **Candidates** (variant file with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.70 76.059, 0 off, max 0.967). (1) **Follow-up: launch traction** 25 m/s until 130 km/h: **75.882** (0 off, max 0.959; p0 75.650) — chosen. (2) **Own, new: faded launch** (extra limit `launch_slip`·(1 − v/`launch_v`), latched): 25/130 **75.847** (0 off, max 0.957, p0 75.498 but flick 0.929), 25/150 75.921, 25/180 75.987 (max 1.000), 40/150–180 75.932; on shifted bases (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs) **2 of 40 off** (plain launch 1 of 40, 130 and 150 alike: the known `brake_margin` −2 edge run): −0.035 s is noise, less margin. (3) **Own, new: 2nd-gear start** (gear 2 below 10 km/h, with the launch): 76.306 (0 off): the engine bogs at the clutch, +0.42 s. (4) **Own, new: launch upshift on ground speed** (during the launch the upshift rpm is scaled by front/rear wheel speed, so spin does not trigger early shifts): 75.952 (0 off, max 0.945), p0 75.806: slower. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** v0.69 read the spin-induced early upshifts (1→2 at 32 km/h, 2→3 at 47 km/h) as a side effect to watch; measured, they are part of the gain (shifting on ground rpm costs +0.07 s all-30, +0.16 s p0): with the spin the engine stays in its torque band in the higher gear. v0.70's "78 + launch = 75.882, 0 of 30" reproduces exactly. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`): all-30 75.882 (−0.088 s vs v0.68, −0.177 vs v0.70), 0 of 30 off, max 0.932/0.959/0.911. |

Suites **75.921/75.876/75.849** (v0.70 76.026/76.092/76.059; all-30 76.059 → **75.882**; v0.68 75.970), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.932/0.959/0.911**. ## v0.72 — Straight-exit traction: the launch allowance out of the hairpin and the Corkscrew

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.72 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.72`). New knobs `exit_steer` **0.3** and `exit_vy` **6** km/h (lines 599–600): after the launch, below `launch_v` (130 km/h) the launch allowance `launch_slip` (25 m/s of extra rear over-speed) applies again while the car runs straight, scaled by clip(1 − \|steer\|/`exit_steer`, 0, 1)·clip(1 − \|speedY\|/`exit_vy`, 0, 1) (lines 872–880, after the launch latch). One mechanism (traction control on slow straight exits). No other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.71 first** (`runs/run_20261002_213831.csv`, time per 100 m split into full throttle / part / full lock / lift / brake, and every step where traction control cut the throttle). Part throttle is the largest non-braking loss (400 m 2.04 s, 1,500 m 1.83 s, 2,500 m 1.36 s, 3,300 m 1.03 s). Most of it is in corners (vy 12–17 km/h, the cut is doing its job), but out of the hairpin (3,285–3,330 m) the car is dead straight (steer 0.00, vy 0.1 km/h) in 2nd at 57–134 km/h and ~6,800–15,000 rpm, and traction control cut the throttle by 0.15–0.6 for ~100 m (rear over-speed beyond the 9.5 m/s straight limit), the same state v0.71 fixed at the standing start; the Corkscrew exit (2,500 m, 78–131 km/h, \|steer\| ≤ 0.15) the same, cut 0.19. **Candidates** (variant with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.71 75.882, 0 off, max 0.959). (1) **Own, new: straight-exit traction (chosen)**, gate width `exit_steer`/`exit_vy`: 0.1/3 → 75.834, 0.2/6 → 75.790, **0.3/6 → 75.782**, 0.2/10 → 75.825, 0.3/10 → 75.797, 0.5/15 → 75.785 but **1 of 30 off** (s1 `corner_speed` +2, max 1.064; flick slide 19.5 km/h); allowance 10/15/25/40 m/s at 0.1–0.2 gate → 75.882/75.850/75.790/75.793; speed limit 130 vs 180 identical (no straight exit above 130 is cut). Shifted bases (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): **0.3/6: 1 of 40 off** (75.709/75.974; v0.71 1 of 40, 75.780/76.028, the same `brake_margin` −2 run), 0.2/6: 2 of 40 off. (2) **Own, new: 1st gear on slow straight exits** (below 80 km/h, \|steer\| < 0.1, rpm < 12,000: the hairpin exit sits at 6,800 rpm in 2nd): all-30 **76.152** (+0.27 s, 0 off): more spin, not more drive; the v0.23 rule (`lowest_running_gear` 2) holds again. (3) Orchestrator lead, the state at full-lock onset (hairpin/flick): not re-tried as-is, v0.70 measured every brake-into-lock variant slower. **Inherited conclusion challenged:** batch 5 found raising the straight traction limit (`tc_slip_straight` 4/6) gives nothing; that holds above ~130 km/h, but at low speed in 2nd far below the torque peak wheelspin is drive, as v0.71 showed for the start: the extra limit needs a speed bound, not a bigger global value. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`): all-30 75.782 (−0.10 s vs v0.71), 0 of 30 off, max 0.932/0.960/0.911. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.60 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_214610.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **75.600 s** → on screen 1:15:60, **new best** (v0.71 75.650, −0.050 s): sections 3,400 m −0.044, 3,300 m −0.022, 3,600 m −0.022, 2,600 m −0.020, 3,500 m +0.022, 2,500 m +0.020. Suites **75.826/75.780/75.740** (v0.71 75.921/75.876/75.849; all-30 75.882 → **75.782**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.932/0.960/0.911**. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.843** at ~2,475 m, the flick). **Watch points:** flick 0.843, slide 16.8 km/h, exit slide 8.4 → 8.0 km/h; kink 9.0 km/h; hairpin minimum 57.3 km/h, \|tp\| 0.77; largest slide elsewhere 17.1 km/h (~508 m). Top speed 275.2 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). Shifted bases (40 runs): 1 of 40 off (`brake_margin` 14 with −2, as v0.71). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (75.600) and all-30 75.882 → 75.782 (−0.10 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum unchanged (0.960 vs 0.959), shifted bases unchanged (1 of 40). |
| **Learned** | The standing-start finding generalises: below ~130 km/h on a straight exit the engine is far below its torque peak and wheelspin is drive, so traction control should not cut there (−0.10 s all-30, mostly the hairpin exit onto the finish straight). The gate must stay narrow: up to \|steer\| 0.3 and 6 km/h of slide is safe; 0.5/15 lets the flick exit slide (19.5 km/h) and 1 of 30 leaves. A lower gear is not the same thing (1st gear: +0.27 s). Remaining part-throttle time is in corners where the car slides 12–17 km/h, where the cut is needed. |

## v0.73 — Brake touch threshold 0.26 (outside analysis "Bob"): no gain

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.73 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.73`). Line 593: `touch_brake` **0.15 → 0.26** (a brake pedal lighter than this keeps `touch_keep` 0.7 of the stored throttle instead of zeroing it). Single value, no other line or knob changed. **Rejected: the code is restored to v0.72.** |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.72 first** (`runs/run_20261002_214610.csv`, every brake application: start, length, peak pedal, stored throttle before/after, steps back to full throttle). 43 applications, 700 brake steps. **The outside claim** (brake touches of 0.15–0.35 pedal zero the stored throttle, esp. at 400–520 m, cost 0.30–0.45 s; 400–520 m brake % 31 → < 20) **does not hold**: only 7 of 43 applications peak at 0.15–0.35 (159 m 2 steps, 480 m 11 steps, 998 m 8 steps, 2,282/2,289/2,300 m 1–2 steps each, 2,884 m 8 steps), one of them in 400–520 m; 89 steps in total have a pedal of 0.15–0.26. Three of the seven start from a stored throttle of 0.10–0.15 (nothing to keep), and `touch_keep` is applied every step of a touch, so after the 8–11-step touches 0.7^n leaves ~0 whatever the threshold (also at 428 m: peak 0.14, 11 steps, throttle after 0.00). Measured on the version's run: 400–520 m brake % **25 → 25**, section time **4.020 → 4.020 s**, allowed speed 102–157 km/h in both. **Candidates** (variant file with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.72 **75.782**, 0 off, max 0.960). (1) **Outside idea, `touch_brake`** 0.12 / 0.18 / 0.22 / 0.26 / 0.30 → **75.776 / 75.809 / 75.777 / 75.790 / 75.829** (max 0.950 / 0.951 / 1.000 / 0.991 / 1.763; 0 off except 0.30: **1 of 30 off**, `brake_margin` −2); 0.22 with `touch_keep` 0.6 75.776 (max 0.958), 0.26 with keep 0.9 75.922 (max 0.995): a plateau within ±0.03 s (chaos), as v0.64's Optuna study found, and the suite maximum rises above 0.15. (2) **Own: keep more through a touch**, `touch_keep` 0.85 / 1.0 → 75.921 (1 off) / 75.950. (3) **Own, new: latched touch** (the kept throttle is `touch_keep` × the throttle at the start of the application, not compounded per step; cleared once the pedal passes `touch_brake` (L1) or not (L2)): L1 75.951, L1 + 0.26 75.928 (1 off), L2 76.047 (max 0.996), L2 + 0.26 76.027 (**5 off**). (4) **Own, new: maintenance throttle in the lift band** (send 0.3 / 0.6 of the stored throttle instead of 0): 75.856 (max 0.974) / 75.941 (1 off, `corner_speed` +2). (5) **Own, new: de-noising the allowed speed** (at 2,280–2,365 m it jumps 235 ↔ 300 km/h step to step as the sharpness plan flickers, and the car brakes in short 1–5-step pulses 268 → 231 km/h with the throttle re-opening in between): peak hold over 2 / 3 / 5 steps → 75.891 (1 off) / 24 off / 30 off; median of 3 / 5 / 7 → 2 / 13 / 28 off; hold of 3 only above 240 / 200 km/h → 12 / 22 off, 5 above 240 → 24 off (all at the flick or hairpin exit). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** v0.64's "the +0.05/step climb after a touch costs ~0.3 s"; re-checked, every way of keeping more throttle through or after a touch (higher share, latch, wider threshold with a high share) is slower by 0.14–0.27 s all-30 or leaves the track: at those points the car is at the plan's speed limit, not short of throttle. The chosen version is the outside value 0.26, recorded as the test of that claim. |
| **Prediction** | Outside estimate: −0.30 to −0.45 s, 400–520 m brake % < 20. Measured before the run (`tools/suite.py`): all-30 75.790 (+0.008 s), 0 of 30 off, max 0.991. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.59 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_224942.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **75.598 s** → on screen 1:15:59 (v0.72 75.600, −0.002 s: sections 200 m +0.044, 2,400 m +0.040, 100 m −0.022, the rest within ±0.022). Suites **75.740/75.889/75.741** (v0.72 75.826/75.780/75.740; all-30 75.782 → **75.790**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.991/0.949/0.865** (v0.72 0.932/0.960/0.911). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.841** at ~2,474 m, the flick). **Watch points:** flick 0.841, slide 15.8 km/h, exit slide 8.9 km/h; kink 8.9 km/h; hairpin minimum 57.1 km/h, \|tp\| 0.77, exit slide 1.5 km/h; largest slide elsewhere 18.4 km/h (~507 m); 448 m bend (400–520 m): brake % 25 (v0.72 25), allowed speed 102–157 km/h, section 4.020 s, all unchanged. Top speed 275.2 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). Shifted bases not run (the version already fails on the suite mean and margin). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — all-30 75.790 does not beat v0.72's 75.782 (the −0.002 s single lap is chaos) and the suite maximum rises 0.960 → 0.991; no other candidate gained either. `snakeoil3_v1.py` restored to v0.72. |
| **Learned** | The brake-touch mechanism is exhausted: `touch_brake` 0.12–0.26 is a ±0.03 s plateau, and keeping more throttle through a touch (higher share, latched share, wider threshold with a high share) costs 0.14–0.27 s or margin. Only 7 of 43 brake applications peak in 0.15–0.35, and the long ones lose the kept throttle anyway (0.7 per step). The 448 m bend's braking is the plan's swinging allowed speed, not the stored throttle. The allowed speed is very noisy on the flick approach (2,280–2,365 m, 235 ↔ 300 km/h), but the pulsed early braking it causes is what brings the car to the kink at 231 km/h: any filter that delays the plan (hold or median, even only above 240 km/h) puts 1–30 of 30 off at the flick or hairpin. A fix there has to lower the noise without raising the arrival speed (e.g. a steadier plan with the same lower envelope). |

## v0.74 — Low-pass on the look-ahead bearing (outside analysis "Bob"): slower

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.74 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.74`). New knob `aim_lp` **0.8** (line 545): the d²-weighted look-ahead bearing is low-passed for the steering term, `c.aim_f = aim_lp·c.aim_f + (1 − aim_lp)·aim` (lines 615–619); the bend detection, the line side and the telemetry keep the raw bearing. One mechanism, no other knob changed. **Rejected: the code is restored to v0.72.** |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.72 first** (`runs/run_20261002_214610.csv`, per 100 m: time, share at the plan, full throttle, traction-control cut, mean step-to-step change of steer and of the bearing). (a) **New: the corner set-up is a 2-step limit cycle.** On every fast straight before a bend (2,268–2,300 m at 267 km/h, 3,120–3,160 m at 222–237 km/h, also 100–300, 900, 1,300, 1,800, 2,800 m) the set-up target flips 0.85 ↔ 0 every step: the pull moves the wheel by the rate limit 0.2, which is over the gate `setup_steer` 0.045, so the next step has no set-up; steer +0.24/+0.04/+0.24…, mean \|Δsteer\| 0.07–0.11 per step, yaw swinging ±0.05 rad with ±5 km/h of slide, and in 40 m the car moves only 0.1 of the half-width outward. When the yaw passes 3° the plan loses the track-direction beams, which is the pulsed braking on the flick approach (2,282–2,300 m, allowed 245–291 km/h) the v0.73 agent found. (b) The flick (2,300–2,600 m, 8.6 s) is not braking-limited: 256 → 64 km/h over 170 m is 13.9 m/s² on average; the car brakes straight for 35 m at trackPos +0.45, the *inside* of the left arc, reaches full lock only when the −45° beam opens (2,447 m, 86 km/h) and scrubs to 64 km/h at full lock. (c) The bearing jumps > 2° in one step 33 times a lap, but only once in 400–520 m (510 m, −8.5° → −13.9°, the −19° beam opening 84 → 167 m at the exit, steer 0.50 → 0.68); that section has 3 steering reversals and a mean \|Δallowed\| of 0.6 km/h per step. **The outside claim** (the −19° beam flipping drives a steering wiggle and the 448 m allowed-speed sawtooth, 0.10–0.25 s) is **partly true as an observation, false as a gain**: one jump, at the exit; with the filter 400–520 m is 4.00 → 3.98 s, brake % 25 → 26, allowed 102–157 → 102–150, and the lap loses elsewhere. **Candidates** (variant file with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.72 **75.782**, 0 off, max 0.960). (1) **Outside idea, `aim_lp`** (steering term only) 0.5 / 0.7 / 0.8 / 0.9 → **75.913 (1 off, `brake_margin` −2) / 75.953 (1 off, `line_offset` −0.1, 1.643) / 76.092 (0 off, max 0.937) / 76.156 (1 off, `turn_grip_aero` +)**; 0.8 also in the bend detection: 76.023, max 0.994. Same result as v0.46 (smoothed aim slower) and v0.51 (steering low-pass slower): the steering needs the bearing without lag. (2) **Own, new: latched set-up** (once pulling, the set-up stays on until the road is under `setup_road` or the bend is detected; own gain instead of the 50 % duty chatter) gain 0.1 / 0.2 / 0.35 → 76.039 (1 off) / 75.917 (1 off, 1.002) / 76.056 (0 off, max 0.953): the chatter's short yaw swings and early brake pulses are part of what the lap is tuned on (as batch 4's cap/low-pass trials). (3) **Own: faster throttle rise** (+0.05 per step → 0.08 / 0.12): 75.980 (max 0.974) / 76.034 (3 off). (4) **Own, new, and a challenge to "the hairpin and flick are steering-limited"**: a front slip-angle cap on the steering (steer·21° within ± `slip_max` of the body slip angle atan(speedY/speedX); at the flick the fronts run ~30° of slip at full lock), suite 1: 10°–22° → **10 of 10 off at the flick**, 26° → 9 of 10 off, 30° → 75.831 (never binds). The inherited conclusion holds: full lock turns the car more than any smaller angle, the scrub is the price. (5) **Own: grip to spare / sharpness plan re-tune**: `grip_boost` 0.4 / 0.5 → 75.923 / 75.948; `boost_steer` 0.25 / 0.3 → 75.802 / 75.862; `boost_fade` 0.2 → 75.915; `turn_grip` 7.3 → 75.935; `turn_grip_aero` 1.7e-4 → 75.887 (1 off); `tc_slip_steer` 0.9 → 75.778 (max 0.966, noise); `lift_pct` 1.5 → 75.768 (1 off, `corner_speed` +2). (6) **Orchestrator lead, a steadier plan with the same lower envelope**: allowed speed = minimum of the last 2 / 4 steps → 75.850 (1 off) / 75.961 (max 0.993); only above 200 km/h → 75.827 (max 0.999) / 75.836 (1 off); with `corner_speed` 79 → 75.828 / 75.925 / 75.753 / 75.934, 1–3 of 30 off each (`brake_margin` −2, `corner_speed` +2): no margin gained. Nothing beat v0.72; the outside idea is recorded as the version, at the one setting with 0 of 30 off. |
| **Prediction** | Outside estimate: −0.10 to −0.25 s. Measured before the run (`tools/suite.py`): all-30 76.092 (+0.31 s), 0 of 30 off, max 0.937. |

| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 274 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 56 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_230356.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **76.160 s** → on screen 1:16:16 (v0.72 75.600, **+0.560 s**; vs the v0.73 run: sections 1,000 m +0.242, 2,500 m +0.120, 1,500 m +0.108, 2,400 m −0.060). Suites **76.107/76.076/76.092** (v0.72 75.826/75.780/75.740; all-30 75.782 → **76.092**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.898/0.937/0.911** (v0.72 0.932/0.960/0.911). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.763** at ~2,474 m, the flick). **Watch points:** flick 0.763 (v0.72 0.843), slide 14.4 km/h, exit slide 6.7 km/h; kink 9.3 km/h; hairpin minimum 56.3 km/h, \|tp\| 0.75, exit slide 1.4 km/h; largest slide elsewhere 17.5 km/h (~3,006 m); 448 m bend (400–520 m): brake % 26 (v0.72 25), allowed speed 102–150 km/h (102–157), section 3.98 s (4.00), 1 steering reversal (3). Bearing jumps > 2° per lap: 33 → 45 (raw bearing). Top speed 274.8 km/h. Slowest corner 56 km/h (~3,284 m, the hairpin). Shifted bases not run (the version already fails on the suite mean). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — all-30 76.092 vs v0.72's 75.782 (+0.31 s) and the lap +0.56 s; the small flick margin gain (suite max 0.960 → 0.937) unlocked nothing tested. `snakeoil3_v1.py` restored to v0.72. |
| **Learned** | A lag on the look-ahead bearing costs 0.13–0.37 s at any strength (third time: v0.46, v0.51, v0.74): the bearing's jumps are real information (a beam opening = the road opening), not noise. The 448 m bend has one bearing jump at its exit and no bearing-driven sawtooth. The corner set-up is a 2-step on/off limit cycle (gate on its own steering), yet a smooth latched pull is slower or leaves the track: the lap is tuned on that chatter and its early brake pulses. A front slip-angle steering cap leaves the track at the flick at every setting that binds: full lock is needed. v0.72 is a tight local optimum on the 30-run suites: 30 one-knob or one-switch changes all landed between −0.01 and +0.37 s, none clearly better; the next gain needs a change worth > 0.1 s, most likely at the flick entry (the car brakes on the inside, +0.45, for the left arc) rather than in filters or re-tunes. |

## v0.75 — No outside pull while braking at speed: faster on the suites, fails the shifted bases

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.75 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.75`). New knobs `out_brake` **2**, `out_v` **180** km/h, `out_lp` **0.8** (lines 601–603): in a detected bend's outside phase (phase > 0, more than 60 m of road visible), at or above `out_v`, the phase is multiplied by 1 − `out_brake`·clip(2·`c.brk_f`, 0, 1) (lines 662–670), where `c.brk_f` is the braking flag (brake > 0) low-passed by `out_lp` per step (line 835, after the ABS). With `out_brake` 2 the target becomes the inside target instead of the outside one while the car brakes at speed. One mechanism (racing-line target while braking), no other knob changed. **Rejected: the code is restored to v0.72.** |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.72 first** (`runs/run_20261002_214610.csv`, per-100 m sections, `tools/width.py`, step traces of 2,250–2,560 m and 3,150–3,350 m). (a) The flick (2,400–2,600 m, 7.04 s) and hairpin (3,200–3,300 m, 3.81 s) are the slowest sections. At the flick the car reaches full lock at 2,448 m at 85.9 km/h, trackPos +0.02, slide 14 km/h, then scrubs to 63.6 km/h (below `corner_speed` 78) and drifts to −0.84; through turn-in it runs 10–15 km/h over the plan with the ABS cutting most steps (2,400–2,432 m: 179 → 110 km/h, ~23 m/s²: the brakes are at the limit, the plan is late). (b) **New: the kink before the flick is taken on the *outside* target.** At 2,364–2,395 m the bend is detected (bearing > 2°) with 85–93 m of road along the track direction, so the phase is +1 and the target is +0.47 (left, the outside of the right kink) while the car brakes at 231 → 190 km/h on the crest: the car moves −0.30 → +0.45, which is the inside of the flick's left arc, and brakes straight there for 35 m. The same outside-target-while-braking state occurs at 700, 960, 1,400, 2,320 and 2,900 m (80 steps ≥ 180 km/h per lap). **Candidates** (variant file with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.72 **75.782**, 0 off, max 0.960; shifted bases 1 of 40). (1) **Outside idea ("Bob"), `setup_steer`** 0.050 / 0.055 / 0.060 / 0.070 → **75.904 / 75.882 / 75.876 (1 off, `brake_margin` −2, 1.084) / 75.849 (3 off, max 1.745)**; plain laps 76.180 / 75.994 / 75.682 / 75.536. The claim (re-opening the set-up before 1,520/1,930 m is worth 0.10–0.25 s) is **false as a gain**: every wider gate is 0.07–0.12 s slower on the suites and from 0.060 leaves the track; the gate is part of the set-up's 2-step on/off cycle (v0.74), so widening it changes the chatter everywhere, not just at those two bends. (2) **Own, new: no outside pull while braking (chosen)**, at every speed: `out_brake` 0.5 / 1 / 1.5 / 2 / 2.5 / 3 → 75.755 / 75.748 (1 off) / 75.762 (2 off) / **75.734 (0 off, max 0.949)** / 75.994 (2 off) / 76.167 (6 off; spin at 1,466 m, 54–60 km/h of slide); flag low-pass 0 / 0.5 / 0.9 at 2 → 75.771 (2 off) / 75.771 (0 off) / 75.717 (1 off); with `corner_speed` 79 → 75.760, 2 off. Split by speed, `out_brake` 2: **only ≥ 150 / 180 / 200 / 220 km/h → 75.716 / 75.712 / 75.713 / 75.786 (1 off)**, max 0.957 / 0.953 / 0.950; only < 150 km/h → 75.833 (1 off): the gain is on the fast approaches, the slow ones (448 m, flick, hairpin) need the outside target. At ≥ 200: `out_brake` 1 / 1.5 / 2.5 → 75.748 (max 0.989) / 75.763 (1 off) / 75.992 (4 off). **Shifted bases (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): all speeds 3 of 40 off (0.5: 3 of 40); ≥ 150: 4 of 40; ≥ 180: 5 of 40; ≥ 200: 5 of 40** (`corner_speed` +2, `brake_margin` −2, `line_offset` +0.1, `line_gain` +0.1, `turn_grip_aero` −): the acceptance limit is 1. (3) **Own, new: the same only while steering** (smoothed \|steer\| > 0.3: the kink alone): `out_brake` 1 / 1.5 / 2 → 75.777 / 75.816 / 75.790, 0 off, max 0.952 / 0.946 / 0.991: nothing; the kink's side is not where the time is. (4) **Margin pairing** (enabling-change test): ≥ 180 with `corner_speed` 77 → 75.810, 1 of 30 off, shifted 3 of 40: the margin is not bought back and the time is gone. (5) **Orchestrator lead, the car's position before the flick's left arc:** measured through (2)–(3): with the outside pull removed in the kink the car arrives at +0.36 to +0.41 instead of +0.41 to +0.46 (the kink at 190–230 km/h under braking on the crest understeers to the left whatever the target, steer −0.6 to −0.75), full lock comes at 2,451 m at 88.9 km/h (v0.72 2,448 m, 85.9) and the flick is wider, not narrower (0.928 vs 0.843 on the plain lap). An earlier turn-in from the bend's side cannot help either: the inside target (+0.47) is where the car already is. (6) **Own: split end-of-sight speed for road and beams**: not run, already measured in batch 6 (76.005 or off). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "every lateral move under braking costs time" (batch 4–5): taking the outside pull *away* while braking at speed is 0.05–0.07 s faster on all three suites (the earlier trials added pulls); but it costs margin on the shifted bases. |

| **Lap time** | 1:15.56 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261002_231925.csv`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`); TORCS time **75.560 s** → on screen 1:15:56 (v0.72 75.600, −0.040 s: sections 1,500 m +0.124, 2,500 m −0.080, 3,600 m +0.062, 3,000 m −0.044, 2,400 m −0.040, 1,900 m +0.040, 2,700 m +0.040). Suites **75.752/75.682/75.700** (v0.72 75.826/75.780/75.740; all-30 75.782 → **75.712**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.946/0.953/0.930** (v0.72 0.932/0.960/0.911). **Shifted bases: 5 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14: `corner_speed` +2, `brake_margin` −2; `line_offset` 0.5: `turn_grip_aero` −, `line_offset` +0.1, `line_gain` +0.1; v0.72: 1 of 40). Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.928** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.72 0.843). **Watch points:** flick 0.928, slide 20.6 km/h, exit slide 19.6 km/h (v0.72 16.8 / 8.0); kink slide 13.1 km/h (9.0); full lock first reached at the flick at 2,451 m, 88.9 km/h, trackPos −0.01 (v0.72 2,448 m, 85.9 km/h, +0.02), at the hairpin at 3,259 m, 88.6 km/h, +0.08 (3,258 m, 89.7 km/h, +0.09); hairpin minimum 57.1 km/h, \|tp\| 0.76, exit slide 1.4 km/h; largest slide elsewhere 20.8 km/h (~1,419 m). Entry positions (`tools/width.py`, + = outside, at −80 m): 1,525 m +0.07 (v0.72 +0.11; minimum 126 km/h vs 137), 1,931 m +0.28 (+0.20), flick +0.34 (+0.36), hairpin +0.15 (+0.17). Top speed 275.2 km/h. Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,283 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ❌ Rejected — faster on the three suites (all-30 75.782 → 75.712, 0 of 30 off) but **5 of 40 off on the shifted bases** (limit 1; v0.72 1 of 40), and the plain lap's flick is wider (0.843 → 0.928, exit slide 8 → 19.6 km/h): it spends margin at the flick instead of buying it. No setting passed both checks. `snakeoil3_v1.py` restored to v0.72. |
| **Learned** | The outside target while braking at ≥ 180 km/h (bend detected with > 80 m of road visible) costs ~0.07 s over 30 laps: without it the car arrives faster, which is exactly why it has less margin at the flick (5 of 40 shifted-base runs off). It is a candidate once the flick/hairpin margin exists, not a way to get it. Below 150 km/h the outside target is needed (75.833, 1 off). The kink's side does not set the flick: the car understeers to the left of the kink under braking on the crest whatever the line target, so its position before the left arc moves only 0.05. Widening the set-up gate (`setup_steer` 0.050–0.070) is slower and leaves the track from 0.060. The 3×10 suites alone would have accepted this version; the shifted bases caught it: keep both checks. |

## v0.76 — Full-lock throttle from the time to the outside edge: faster and more margin

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.76 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.76`). The full-lock throttle limit (step 6, v0.29/v0.50/v0.67) is no longer set by the room to the outside edge alone but by the **time to the outside edge**: `tte` = room / outward drift rate of `trackPos` (per-step change /0.021 s, smoothed 0.7/0.3, floor 0.05 units/s; lines 844–849), and `lt = clip(lock_throttle·(tte − lock_tte_near)/(lock_tte_far − lock_tte_near), 0, lock_throttle_max)` (line 850). New knobs `lock_tte_near` **0.9** s, `lock_tte_far` **1.7** s, `lock_throttle_max` **0.5** (lines 587–589); `lock_throttle` 0.2 (line 586) is now the throttle at `lock_tte_far`. Removed knobs: `lock_throttle_edge` (0), `lock_room_near` (0.3), `lock_room_far` (0.8). One mechanism (throttle near full lock); no other line or knob changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.72 first** (`runs/run_20261002_214610.csv`, step traces 2,300–2,540 m and 3,130–3,350 m). (a) Flick: after the kink the car brakes straight at trackPos +0.45, turns in at 2,432 m (110 km/h) with \|steer\| only 0.6–0.77 for 15 m, reaches full left lock at 2,448 m (85.9 km/h, trackPos +0.02, heading +0.044 rad, slide 14 km/h) when the −45° beam opens, drifts out at ~0.7–1.0 trackPos units per second to −0.84 while scrubbing to 63.5 km/h, **with 0.1–0.2 of throttle sent at full lock for most of the drift** (2,450–2,468 m: the room rule allows 0.2 with ≥ 0.8 of room); the right arc (2,486–2,507 m) then runs at a constant 72 km/h on 0.2 throttle with 1.8 → 1.0 of room and exits at trackPos +0.10 (unused width). (b) Hairpin: full lock at 3,258 m (89.7 km/h, +0.09), throttle 0.2 → 0 during the drift to −0.77, and **no throttle for the ~5 steps after the drift has stopped** (room < 0.3) until the wheel unwinds; minimum 57.3 km/h. So the room rule gives drive while the car is drifting toward the edge and withholds it once it is not. **Candidates** (variant file with switches, `tools/suite.py` all 30; base v0.72 **75.782**, 0 off, max 0.960; shifted bases 1 of 40). (1) **Outside idea ("Bob"), look-ahead weight exponent** d² → d^2.2 / d^2.3 / d^2.5 on v0.72: **75.705 (6 of 30 off) / 75.884 (5 off) / 75.807 (7 off)**; plain laps 75.460 / 75.724 / 75.788: at 2.3–2.5 not even faster on the laps that stay on, and runs off in the suites at every value. On top of v0.76: 2.1 → 75.677 (3 off), 2.2 → 75.732 (1 off, `line_offset` −0.1). (2) **Own, new: short-sight steering boost** (steer × (1 + g·clip((24 − road ahead)/6, 0, 1)): full lock ~15 m earlier at the flick): g 0.3 / 0.6 / 1.0 → 75.921 (max 0.946) / 75.976 (0.970) / 75.962 (1 off); from 30 m, g 0.6 → 76.126 (0.992): earlier lock is slower and no safer. (3) **Own, new: more full-lock throttle with a lot of room** (+0.2 / +0.4 from room 1.2, full at 1.6: the flick's right arc): 75.824 (max 0.952) / 75.829 (0.956): nothing. (4) **Orchestrator lead, turn-in speed for short sight** (extra margin on the road-ahead plan below 40 m of sight: 3 / 6 m; 4 m below 28 m): 75.812 (max 0.971) / 75.847 (0.973) / 75.827 (0.957): no margin gained. (5) **Own, new: time to the edge (chosen)**, near / far / max (throttle 0.2 at far): 0.6/1.4/0.2 → 75.742 (max 0.947); 0.6/1.3/0.4 → 75.704 (0.926); 0.6/1.4/0.3 → 75.740 (0.886); 0.75/1.3/0.4 → 75.693 (0.871); 0.9/1.3/0.3 → 75.768 (0.873); 0.9/1.7/0.2 → 75.759 (0.890); 0.9/1.7/0.3 → 75.746 (0.867); 0.9/1.7/0.4 → 75.779 (0.872); **0.9/1.7/0.5 → 75.649 (0.889)**; 0.9/1.7/0.6 → 75.718 (0.869); 0.9/1.7/0.8 → 75.719 (0.887); 0.9/1.5/0.5 → 75.718 (0.887); 1.0/1.8/0.5 → 75.670 (0.890); 1.2/1.6/0.4 → 75.715 (0.888); 1.2/2.0/0.3 → 75.723 (0.883); 1.2/2.0/0.4 → 75.724 (0.870); 1.5/2.3/0.4 → 75.751 (0.888); too permissive 0.4/1.2/0.2 → 75.687 (1 off, 1.015) and 0.6/1.0/0.2 → 75.757 (1 off, 1.002). A plateau: every setting with near ≥ 0.6 and far ≥ 1.3 is 0 of 30 off, 0.00–0.13 s faster than v0.72, with the suite maximum at 0.87–0.95 instead of 0.960. (In these trials the suite's `lock_throttle` +0.05 run did not act on the new rule; in the version it scales the slope: 75.647.) **Shifted bases** (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 / `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): 0.9/1.7 with max 0.2 / 0.3 / 0.5 and 0.75/1.3/0.4 → **0 of 40 off** each (max 0.996 / 1.000 / 0.986 / 0.983). **Stacks on the chosen setting** (the three banked gains): + `corner_speed` 79 → 75.664, 0 off, max 0.929, shifted 1 of 40 (`brake_margin` 14 with −2: 1.265); `corner_speed` 80 → 75.710, max 0.974; + v0.75's mechanism (`out_brake` 2, `out_v` 180, `out_lp` 0.8) → 75.684, 0 off, max 0.881, shifted 0 of 40 (max 0.994); + both → 75.643, 1 of 30 off (`brake_decel` +0.5). None beats the mechanism alone (75.649; within chaos), so they are not added. **Inherited conclusions challenged:** "the full-lock drift is set when full lock is reached" (batch 6) is only half true: the entry state did not change at the hairpin (89.4 km/h, +0.09) yet the drift ends at −0.65 instead of −0.77, because the 0.2 of drive during the drift was widening it. "Raising the full-lock throttle with room leaves the track" (v0.50, 5–10 of 10 off): true for room, not for time to the edge, where a limit of 0.5–0.8 is safe once the drift has stopped. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`, `tools/finalize.py --suite`): all-30 75.647 (−0.135 s vs v0.72), 0 of 30 off, max 0.889/0.886/0.768; shifted bases 0 of 40 off. |

Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.693** at ~2,477 m, the flick; v0.72 0.843). **Watch points:** flick: full lock first reached at 2,442 m at 92.0 km/h, trackPos +0.20, heading +0.130 rad, slide 8.4 km/h (v0.72: 2,448 m, 85.9 km/h, +0.02, +0.044 rad, 14.1 km/h); max 0.693 (0.843), slide 12.4 km/h (16.8), exit slide 11.2 km/h (8.0), kink slide 8.7 km/h (9.0), minimum 65.7 km/h (63.5), 81.6 km/h at 2,515 m (80.7). Hairpin: full lock at 3,258 m at 89.4 km/h, trackPos +0.09, heading +0.079 rad, slide 6.8 km/h (v0.72: 3,258 m, 89.7 km/h, +0.09, +0.076 rad, 6.7 km/h); no throttle during the drift, 0.2–0.8 from 3,275 m as it stops; minimum 57.6 km/h at ~3,276 m (57.3 at ~3,283 m), max \|tp\| 0.650 (0.766), exit slide 13.4 km/h (1.5; the car leaves with ~0.5 throttle on part lock), 95.4 km/h at 3,300 m (92.0). Largest slide elsewhere 17.1 km/h (~508 m). Positions (`tools/width.py`, −80 m / slowest point / +80 m, + = outside): flick −0.32 / +0.68 / −0.02, hairpin +0.15 / +0.62 / +0.47. Top speed 275.3 km/h. Slowest corner 58 km/h (~3,276 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (75.482) and all-30 75.782 → 75.647 (−0.135 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum 0.960 → 0.889, shifted bases 1 → 0 of 40 off. |
| **Learned** | The full-lock throttle should follow what the car is doing, not where it is: no drive while it drifts toward the edge (time to the edge under ~0.9 s), drive as soon as the drift stops. That shrinks the flick drift (0.843 → 0.693) and the hairpin drift (0.77 → 0.65) at the same hairpin entry speed, and starts the hairpin exit ~7 m earlier. The setting is a wide plateau (near 0.6–1.5 s, far 1.3–2.3 s, max 0.2–0.8: 75.65–75.78, all 0 of 30 off); the chosen 75.649 is its best point, so part of the −0.135 s may be chaos (the plateau is typically −0.03 to −0.09 s); the margin gain is in every setting. The new margin is not yet turned into time by the banked gains: `corner_speed` 79 and v0.75's mechanism stack safely (0 of 30; shifted 1 and 0 of 40) but are not faster on top (75.664, 75.684). d^2.1–2.5 still leaves the track. New cost: the hairpin exit slide rises to 13 km/h (drive on part lock as the wheel unwinds); a guard on that slide is a candidate. Earlier full lock at the flick (steering boost) and a slower turn-in (short-sight margin) buy nothing. |

## v0.77 — Braking refit on the v0.76 margin: ABS back at 20 % slip, later braking plan

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.77 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.77`). One mechanism, braking to the limit, two knobs changed together: `abs_ratio` 0.85 → **0.8** (line 595; the brake is halved once the slowest wheel turns below 80 % of the car speed, as before v0.55) and `brake_aero` 0.006 → **0.0065** (line 539; the plan's speed-dependent deceleration, so the plan brakes later at speed). Comment added at the ABS block (lines 819–822). No other knob or code changed. |
| **Why** | v0.76 gave the lap margin at the flick and hairpin for the first time since batch 5 (suite max 0.960 → 0.889, shifted bases 0 of 40 off), but the gains that had been waiting behind that margin were flat on it (`corner_speed` 79: 75.664; v0.75's mechanism: 75.684; d^2.1–2.2: 1–3 off). This version looks for where the margin does convert. **Measured on v0.76 (all 30; base 75.647, max 0.889):** ABS at 0.8 alone costs no time and narrows the drift further (75.654, max **0.807**: fewer ABS cuts on the approach, so the car is nearer the plan speed when full lock is reached); the later plan alone is as fast as the base but leaves the track (`brake_aero` 0.0065 at `abs_ratio` 0.85: 75.651, **1 of 30 off**, `brake_margin` −2, max 1.154). Together the later plan spends the ABS margin: **75.527** (−0.12 s), 0 of 30 off, max 0.854. **Neighbours (all 30, all 0 off):** `abs_ratio` 0.78 / 0.82 at `brake_aero` 0.0065 → 75.582 (max 0.838) / 75.556 (max 0.901); `brake_aero` 0.00625 / 0.00675 / 0.007 at `abs_ratio` 0.8 → 75.595 (max 0.824) / 75.625 (max 0.904) / 75.672 (max 0.994). Every neighbour is faster than v0.76, so the direction is real; the chosen point is the best of the plateau and part of its lead over the neighbours (0.03–0.10 s) is probably chaos. **Challenged:** v0.55's "ABS at 15 % slip is the plateau (0.83–0.87)", measured before the full-lock throttle rule existed, and the outside analysis's advice to stop touching the braking plan and ABS threshold. **Record note:** the iteration agent that chose this change was cut off (session limit) after recording the run and before writing its notes, so its other trials are not on record; every number here was re-measured by the orchestrator on the final code. Alternatives measured earlier in the batch are in the v0.73–v0.76 entries. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py`): all-30 75.527 (−0.120 s vs v0.76), 0 of 30 off, max 0.776/0.854/0.780; shifted bases 1 of 40 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.27 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 275 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_000058.csv`; `tools/finalize.py --verify` rerun byte-identical); TORCS time **75.278 s** → on screen 1:15:27, **new best** (v0.76 75.482, −0.204 s; spread over the lap in steps of ~0.02 s per 100 m section, largest at 200 m −0.04). Suites **75.455/75.606/75.519** (v0.76 75.625/75.651/75.665; all-30 75.647 → **75.527**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.776/0.854/0.780** (v0.76 0.889/0.886/0.768). **Shifted bases** (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 and `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): **1 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14 with `turn_grip` +0.5, max 1.673; the other 39: means 75.594/75.594, max 0.997 and 0.948); v0.76 0 of 40, v0.72 1 of 40. Watch points (v0.76 in brackets): max \|trackPos\| 0.709 at ~2,473 m, flick (0.693); flick slide 14.5 km/h (12.4), exit slide 12.2 (11.2), kink slide 9.0 (8.7); hairpin min 57.2 km/h (57.6), \|trackPos\| 0.65 (0.65), exit slide 13.6 km/h (13.4); largest slide elsewhere 17.8 km/h at ~510 m (17.1 at ~508 m). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (75.278) and all-30 75.647 → 75.527 (−0.120 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum 0.889 → 0.854; shifted bases 1 of 40 off (within the batch's limit of 1; v0.76 had 0). |
| **Learned** | The flick/hairpin margin converts into time through the braking plan, not through `corner_speed` or the line: an ABS that cuts later (20 % slip) puts the car nearer the plan speed at full-lock onset, and a later plan at speed (`brake_aero` 0.0065) spends that. Neither knob works alone (0.8 alone: no time; 0.0065 alone: 1 of 30 off). Margin is thinner again on the shifted bases (1 of 40, `turn_grip` +0.5 at `brake_margin` 14), so the next speed gain needs new margin first. Inherited "plateau" results for braking knobs do not survive a change in how the car behaves at full lock: re-scan them after each margin gain. |

## v0.78 — Clutch slip: the engine at its torque peak out of slow corners and after upshifts

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.78 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.78`). One new mechanism, **clutch slip**: while accelerating (no brake, stored throttle > 0) the clutch pedal is pressed whenever the rpm the gear gives at the car's speed (ground rpm = speed / rear radius 0.315 m × gear ratio × final drive 4.5; line 920) is below `clutch_rpm`: `R['clutch'] = clutch_slip·clip((clutch_rpm − ground rpm)/clutch_fade, 0, 1)` (lines 909–923). New knobs `clutch_slip` **0.7**, `clutch_rpm` **14,000**, `clutch_fade` **2,000** (lines 601–603). No upshift while the clutch slips (line 933: the engine rpm is then not the gear's rpm). Until now the driver never used the clutch (always 0). No other knob or code changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.77 first** (`runs/run_20261003_000058.csv`, per-100 m table and step traces of the start, the 1,400 m braking zone and the hairpin exit). (a) Every exit is traction-control- or rpm-limited: share of steps with a TC cut 49 % at 500–600 m, 45 % at 1,500 m, 30 % at 2,500 m, 20–28 % at 400, 700, 800, 1,900, 2,700 m. (b) Out of the hairpin the car is in 2nd at 6,800–12,000 rpm up to ~92 km/h, far below the 16–18k torque peak, and only gets there by spinning the rears (rear over-speed 20–94 m/s; v0.71/v0.72); the spin rpm then triggers the upshift at 3,302 m into 3rd at 10,254 rpm, a 3→2→3 hunt follows (3,313–3,320 m), and 3rd runs at 12,900–15,000 rpm up to 155 km/h. The same after every upshift at the start. (c) The 1,400 m braking zone is plan-limited, not pedal-limited (274 → 165 km/h in 1.23 s ≈ 24.6 m/s², pedal 0.06–0.92 in a sawtooth from the set-up chatter). **Candidates.** (1) **Own, new (chosen): clutch slip.** In TORCS the torque passed to the wheels saturates once the clutch is about 1/3 engaged, while the engine speed follows the wheels only weakly at part engagement (from memory of the simulator's transmission model, not re-read; the 0.8 result below fits it), so a partly pressed clutch lets the engine rev to its torque peak and still drive with all of it; the driver had never sent a clutch value. Variant trials, all 30 (`tools/suite.py`; base v0.77 **75.527**, max 0.854): only below 130 km/h, pedal 0.5 → **75.426** (0 off, max 0.874), pedal 0.7 → 75.386, pedal 0.6 → 75.409, `clutch_rpm` 16,000 → 75.558; up to 160 / 180 km/h at 0.7 → 75.379 / 75.371; up to 200 km/h or no speed limit at 0.7 (identical: the rpm rule alone decides) → **75.243** (0 off, max 0.860); pedal 0.5 up to 200 km/h 75.282 but **2 of 30 off** (`turn_grip_aero` +, `turn_grip` +0.5); 0.65 → 75.267 (0 off; shifted bases 2 of 40 off); **0.8 → 76.163** (torque is lost); `clutch_rpm` 13,000 / 15,000 → 75.317 / 75.299; `clutch_fade` 1,000 → 75.285 (1 off) / 3,000 → 75.293; faded out with \|steer\| 0.5 → 75.306; faded out with slide 10 km/h → 75.272 (shifted 2 of 40 off); with the launch/exit TC allowance off while slipping → 75.546 (below 130 km/h; with `clutch_rpm` 16,000: 75.520): the wheelspin allowance is still worth keeping. (2) **Plan rank 1, braking re-scan on v0.77** (all 30): `brake_max` 30 → 75.602, **1 off**; `abs_cut` 0.6 → 75.664, **2 off**. (3) **Plan rank 2, full-lock rule**: `lock_tte_near` 0.7 → 75.547 (0 off, max 0.857): flat. (4) **Plan rank 4**: `corner_speed` 79 on v0.77 → 75.571 (0 off, max 0.924): slower. (5) Own, not run for time: a proportional ABS on the plan-limited braking zones (v0.55 measured 76.80–76.89 against 76.856) and a slide guard on the hairpin exit (13 km/h since v0.76). **Challenged:** v0.71/v0.72 "wheelspin far below the torque peak is drive" and v0.71's "the spin-triggered early upshifts are part of the gain": true only as long as the clutch is closed; spinning the tyres was a substitute for a slipping clutch. Also re-checked plan rank 1 ("re-scan the braking knobs"): on v0.77 the two braking knobs tried only spend margin (1–2 of 30 off). |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py` on the final code): all-30 75.259 (−0.268 s vs v0.77), 0 of 30 off, max 0.828/0.856/0.777; shifted bases 1 of 40 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:15.19 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 276 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_003711.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **75.192 s** → on screen 1:15:19, **new best** (v0.77 75.278, −0.086 s; the single lap is within the ±0.2 s chaos, largest section changes 2,500 m +0.080, 200 m +0.066, 3,600 m −0.062, 2,400 m +0.060, 700 m −0.044, 3,300 m −0.044). Suites **75.224/75.278/75.276** (v0.77 75.455/75.606/75.519; all-30 75.527 → **75.259**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.828/0.856/0.777** (v0.77 0.776/0.854/0.780). **Shifted bases** (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 and `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): **1 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14 with `brake_margin` −2, max 1.263; `brake_margin` 14: mean of the other 19 75.311, suite 1 max 0.885; `line_offset` 0.5: mean 75.222, max 0.958, 0 off); v0.77 1 of 40. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.770** at ~2,476 m, the flick; v0.77 0.709). Watch points (v0.77 in brackets): flick slide 16.1 km/h (14.5), exit slide 9.7 (12.2), kink slide 8.7 (9.0); hairpin min 57.1 km/h (57.2), \|trackPos\| 0.64 (0.65), exit slide 13.0 km/h (13.6); largest slide elsewhere 17.4 km/h at ~1,946 m (17.8 at ~510 m). Start: 50 km/h reached ~1.08 s after moving off (1.12), 200 km/h after ~4.41 s (4.45). Hairpin exit: 3,400 m passed at 201 km/h (198), 3,500 m at 244 km/h (242), 0.03 s gained from the hairpin minimum to 3,500 m (68.47 → 73.67 s against 68.52 → 73.75 s). Top speed 276.7 km/h logged (275.3). Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,278 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (75.192) and all-30 75.527 → 75.259 (−0.268 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum 0.854 → 0.856; shifted bases 1 of 40 off (within the limit of 1, as v0.77). |
| **Learned** | The clutch is a driver output that had never been used: a partly pressed clutch (0.7) keeps the engine at its torque peak whenever the gear would hold it below 14,000 rpm, at the start, out of the hairpin and the flick, and for a moment after every upshift on the medium-corner exits (most of the gain is above 130 km/h: 75.386 below 130 only, 75.243 without a speed limit in the variant, 75.259 in the final code). The pedal must stay at or below ~0.7: at 0.8 the torque is lost (76.163). The traction allowance of v0.71/v0.72 still helps on top. Margin is unchanged on the suites (max 0.856) but the setting sits near an edge like every faster car here: pedal 0.5, 0.65, `clutch_fade` 1,000 and the slide-gated form each put 1–2 runs off somewhere (suites or shifted bases), always the `turn_grip`/`turn_grip_aero` + or `brake_margin` −2 runs. Open after this version: the gear shift points and `upshift_hold` were tuned for a closed clutch (with slip the engine no longer needs 15,000 rpm after a downshift, so `downshift_rpm`, `upshift_rpm` and `clutch_rpm` should be co-tuned), the TC knobs (`tc_slip`, `launch_slip`, `exit_steer`) on the new exits, and slip under part throttle in the medium bends. The plan's braking re-scan (`brake_max` 30, `abs_cut` 0.6), `lock_tte_near` 0.7 and `corner_speed` 79 gave nothing on v0.77. |

## v0.79 — Clutch slip in every gear, held under the rev limiter (from the simulator's source)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.79 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.79`). One mechanism, the **clutch slip law** of v0.78, rewritten: while accelerating (no brake, stored throttle > 0) the clutch is now slipped **in every gear** whenever the rpm of the driven wheels (`axle_rpm` = mean rear wheel speed × gear ratio × final drive 4.5, line 933) is below `clutch_top`, with the pedal `min(clutch_slip, 1 − (clutch_k/(clutch_top − axle_rpm + clutch_k))^¼)` (lines 934–936): full slip far below `clutch_top`, closing as the wheels near it. Knobs: `clutch_slip` 0.7 → **0.667** (line 601); new `clutch_top` **17,000** rpm (line 602) and `clutch_k` **60** rpm (line 603); removed `clutch_rpm` 14,000 and `clutch_fade` 2,000. The upshift now reads the driven wheels' rpm instead of the engine rpm (line 946: `axle_rpm > upshift_rpm`, was `S['rpm'] > upshift_rpm and R['clutch'] == 0`), because with the clutch slipping the engine rpm is no longer the gear's rpm. No other knob or code changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.78 first** (`runs/run_20261003_003711.csv`: every gear change with the acceleration before and after it, the throttle state per 100 m, traces of the hairpin and 500 m exits). (a) Of the lap, 40.2 s are full throttle, 14.6 s braking, 11.8 s under a traction-control cut and 7.9 s part throttle. (b) Every 2→3 upshift is triggered by wheelspin at 127–140 km/h (ground rpm 14,000–15,400), and the acceleration falls from 12.5–15.3 to 10.1–12.5 m/s² across it; every 3→4 upshift (196 km/h) drops it from ~11.4 to ~7.9 m/s². (c) The orchestrator's open point (c) was checked against the **simulator source** (`C:\torcs\gym_torcs\vtorcs-RL-color\src\modules\simu\simuv2`, `engine.cpp`, `transmission.cpp`, `simu.cpp`) and the car file: torque passed to the wheels = engine torque × min(3·(1 − pedal), 1) (so **all of it up to pedal 2/3, only 0.9 of it at v0.78's 0.7**, 0.6 at 0.8: v0.78's 76.163); the engine speed is pulled to the wheels' only by (1 − pedal)⁴ per simulation step; the engine torque is **0 above the 18,700 limiter**; and the torque curve is nearly flat (340–360 N·m from 9,000 to 18,000 rpm). So v0.78's reading ("the engine at its torque peak") was not the reason it gained, and its rule (slip only below 14,000 ground rpm) left the slip unused over most of every gear. **Candidates.** (1) **Own, new (chosen): slip in every gear, closed toward the limiter.** First lap of the variant 74.934 (v0.78 75.192). All 30 (`tools/suite.py`; base v0.78 **75.259**, max 0.856), `clutch_slip` / `clutch_top` / `clutch_k`: 0.667 / 18,300 / 28 → 74.982; 0.7 / 18,300 / 28 → 75.224 (torque lost); 0.667 / 18,300 / 60 → 74.953; / 120 → 74.905; / 300 → 74.986; 0.667 / 17,500 / 28 → 74.919; **0.667 / 19,000 / 28 → 75.972** (the engine sits on the limiter); 0.6 / 18,300 / 28 → 74.943; 0.63 / 18,300 / 120 → 74.929; 0.667 / 17,500 / 120 → 74.934; / 17,000 / 120 → 74.937; / 16,500 / 120 → 74.931; 0.667 / 17,500 / 200 → 74.941; **0.667 / 17,000 / 60 → 74.909** (max 0.852; chosen: the middle of a flat plateau 74.90–74.99, away from the cliff at 19,000); 0.6 / 17,500 / 120 → 74.918; with `upshift_rpm` 18,200 → 75.055, 19,000 → **86.970** (the car never shifts up); all 0 of 30 off. (2) **Own, new: upshift held until the ground rpm is high enough** (new `upshift_ground`, so wheelspin cannot trigger the 2→3 shift early), all 30: 14,000 (= v0.78) 75.258; 15,000 → 75.282; 15,500 → 75.308; 16,000 → 75.304; 16,500 → 75.236; 17,000 → 75.234 (0 off, max 0.871): flat within the noise, lost. (3) **Orchestrator's point (a), shift and traction knobs re-tuned on the clutch car** (all 30, v0.78 code): `downshift_rpm` 14,000 → 75.511 with **1 of 30 off** (`turn_grip` +0.5, max 1.662), 16,000 → 75.231 (max 0.869), 17,000 → 75.291 (max 0.968); `upshift_hold` 8 and 25 → 75.259 (no effect); `tc_slip` 4.0 → 75.300, 5.0 → 75.288; `tc_slip_straight` 6.5 → 75.266; `launch_v` 150 → 75.278; `exit_steer` 0.4 → 75.264; `tc_gain` 0.4 → 75.272; `tc_hold` 0.7 → 75.244 (max 0.937): all flat, v0.78 is a local optimum for these knobs. (4) Own, not run for time: a lower gear through the traction-limited corner exits now that the clutch decouples the engine. **Challenged:** v0.78's "Learned" (the gain is the torque peak; the pedal must stay ≤ ~0.7) and v0.71's "spin-triggered early upshifts are part of the gain": the source and the 0.667 against 0.7 result (74.982 against 75.224) say the limit is 2/3, not 0.7, and the torque peak is not the lever. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py` on the final code): all-30 74.909 (−0.350 s vs v0.78), 0 of 30 off, max 0.782/0.852/0.779; shifted bases 0 of 40 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:14.86 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 282 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 57 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_010051.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **74.860 s** → on screen 1:14:86, **new best** (v0.78 75.192, −0.332 s; largest section changes against the v0.78 run: 2,500 m −0.080, 3,300 m −0.044, 3,500 m −0.044, 1,700 m −0.040, 2,200 m −0.040; 500 m **+0.100**, 1,500 m +0.040). Suites **74.925/74.919/74.884** (v0.78 75.224/75.278/75.276; all-30 75.259 → **74.909**), **0 of 30 off, max \|trackPos\| 0.782/0.852/0.779** (v0.78 0.828/0.856/0.777). **Shifted bases** (suites 1–2 at `brake_margin` 14 and `line_offset` 0.5, 40 runs): **0 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14: mean 74.969, max 0.858 / **0.991**; `line_offset` 0.5: mean 74.910, max 0.935 / 0.851); v0.78 1 of 40. Zero damage, on track (max `\|trackPos\|` **0.719** at ~2,476 m, the flick; v0.78 0.770). Watch points (v0.78 in brackets): flick slide 14.4 km/h (16.1), exit slide 12.2 (9.7), kink slide 8.8 (8.7); hairpin min 57.3 km/h (57.1), \|trackPos\| 0.65 (0.64), exit slide **14.2 km/h** (13.0); largest slide elsewhere 16.5 km/h at ~1,945 m (17.4 at ~1,946 m). Upshifts now at 204–206 km/h in 3rd (196) and 257 km/h in 4th (245); 2→3 at 141–151 km/h on the medium exits (137–140), but at 94–105 km/h out of the hairpin and the flick (128–137) and at 47 km/h at the start (wheelspin carries the wheels' rpm past `upshift_rpm`). Speeds: 300 m 253.5 km/h (248.3), 2,300 m 254.9 (249.0), 3,400 m 206.5 (200.4), 3,500 m 247.6 (244.2); start 100 km/h after 2.05 s (2.14), 200 km/h after 4.38 s (4.45). Engine rpm at full throttle above 130 km/h: median 17,665, 17 % of the steps read above 18,700 (up to 19,575). Top speed 282.2 km/h logged (276.7). Slowest corner 57 km/h (~3,277 m, the hairpin). |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (74.860) and all-30 75.259 → 74.909 (−0.350 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum 0.856 → 0.852; shifted bases 0 of 40 off (v0.78: 1). The lap is 0.14 s from the 1:14.72 target. |
| **Learned** | Reading the simulator's source paid more than any knob sweep: the clutch passes the full engine torque up to pedal 2/3 while the engine is almost uncoupled from the wheels, so slipping it in every gear, closed only as the wheels near the limiter, is worth 0.35 s over 30 perturbed laps; pedal 0.7 gives most of it away (75.224) and letting the engine sit on the limiter loses a second (`clutch_top` 19,000: 75.972). The setting is a flat plateau (`clutch_top` 16,500–18,300, `clutch_k` 28–300, pedal 0.6–0.667: 74.90–74.99, all 0 of 30 off), unlike the edges of the last batches. Not separated: how much comes from the slip itself and how much from the later upshifts that came with reading the wheels' rpm (3rd to 206 km/h instead of 196, 4th to 257 instead of 245; with a closed clutch the engine reads ~4–8 % above the wheels' rpm, so `upshift_rpm` 18,600 now means a higher road speed; 18,200 was slower, 75.055). The engine reading is above the 18,700 limiter on 17 % of the full-throttle steps, so the rule that keeps it under the limiter is not exact yet (the constants of the installed simulator may differ from the source read, a fork of the same module). Open: the early 2→3 upshift at 94–105 km/h out of the hairpin and the flick and the 1→2→3 at the start (wheelspin; the ground-rpm upshift gate was flat on v0.78 but should be re-measured here), the 500 m exit (+0.10 s on the single lap), the hairpin exit slide (14.2 km/h), `upshift_rpm` between 18,600 and 19,000, and a lower gear on the traction-limited exits. The shift and traction knobs (`downshift_rpm`, `upshift_hold`, `tc_slip`, `tc_slip_straight`, `launch_v`, `exit_steer`, `tc_gain`, `tc_hold`) were flat on v0.78. |

## v0.80 — Held corner set-up with a capped pull (the on/off set-up wobble removed)

| Field | Detail |
|---|---|
| **Version** | v0.80 |
| **What changed** | `snakeoil3_v1.py` (Git tag `v0.80`). One mechanism, the **corner set-up** of v0.58, made continuous (lines 653–674). (1) **Held:** the steering gate (`setup_steer` 0.045, line 560) now applies only when the set-up starts; once a side is read from the beam asymmetry it is kept (`c.setup_side`, line 671) until the road along the track falls to `setup_road`, a bend is detected (bearing over 2°), or the beams show the other side by more than `setup_min` 3 m. Until v0.79 the gate was checked every step, and the set-up's own pull closed it. (2) **Capped pull:** the pull `(setup_offset·side − trackPos)·line_gain` is limited to ±`setup_pull` **0.15** steer (new knob, line 561; line 674). (3) `setup_road` 95 → **80** m (line 559): the held set-up runs closer to the bend. Unchanged: `setup_dist` 160, `setup_beam` 2, `setup_min` 3, `setup_offset` 0.85, `setup_steer` 0.045. Nothing else changed. |
| **Why** | **Own analysis of v0.79 first** (`runs/run_20261003_010051.csv`, step traces of the approaches; `tools/patterns.py`, `tools/width.py`). (a) On every straight approach the set-up cycles in 2–3 steps: pull on (target ±0.85, steer +0.20 at the rate limit), next step the gate `|prev steer| < 0.045` is closed, the heading term (4.77 steer per rad) and the look-ahead term (2.0 per rad) swing the wheel back to −0.23, then on again. 858–945 m (to the 1,042 m right-hander): 30 target flips, steer ±0.2, and `trackPos` moves only +0.28 → +0.33 against a target of +0.85. 283 steering reversals and 12.1 s of the lap are in such episodes; 12 of the 13 episodes contain set-up targets. (b) Why the car does not move: a steady pull p holds the car yawed by only p/6.8 rad, so the sideways rate is v·p/6.8: the pulsed pull averages ~0.03–0.05 steer. (c) Width unused: entries at −80 m sit at +0.05 to +0.27 (flick −0.32). **Candidates (own):** (1) **held set-up** (latch; gate only at the start) — chosen, with (2) a **cap on the pull** (new, not in the plan: the pull as a bounded steer bias, sized from the 6.8 steer/rad the controller pushes back with); (3) gate on the steering minus the set-up's own contribution (the orchestrator's suggestion) — not built: in steady state the heading and look-ahead terms answer the pull with an equal and opposite steer, so the remainder closes the gate just the same; (4) keep the 0.85 outside target after the bend is detected (bearing > 2°) instead of dropping to `line_offset` 0.47; (5) start earlier (`setup_dist` 200). **Inherited conclusion challenged:** "smoothing the set-up is slower" (v0.61/v0.74 trials) and "only the combination offset 0.85 / road 95 / gate 0.045 works" (v0.59): on the v0.79 car a continuous set-up is faster (below), and the v0.59 combination was a tuning of the limit cycle, not of the line. **Trials** (variant file, `tools/suite.py`; v0.79 reference suite 1 74.925, all-30 74.909; ~550 races): held, **uncapped** (pull up to ~0.28): the unperturbed lap leaves the track at the flick (1.897 at 2,467 m, slide 32.6 km/h at 1,426 m). Held, uncapped, half gain (0.25): 74.906/74.880/74.925 = all-30 74.904, 0 off (no gain). Held, uncapped, `setup_offset` 0.6: suite 1 74.922, max 0.976. Held + cap at `setup_road` 95: cap 0.08 74.930/74.974/75.005; cap 0.12 suite 1 **1 of 10 off** (flick, `turn_grip` +0.5); cap 0.15 74.887/74.856/74.926 = 74.890, 0 off; cap 0.20 suite 1 1 of 10 off; cap 0.30 74.994, max 0.971. Cap 0.15 with `setup_road`: 85 → 74.869/74.876/74.820 = 74.855 but **shifted bases 1 of 40 off** (`brake_margin` 14 + `line_offset` +0.1, 1.721); **80 → 74.841/74.807/74.850 = 74.833, shifted 0 of 40 (chosen)**; 70 → 74.910/74.991/74.909. At road 80: cap 0.12 suite 1 2 of 10 off, cap 0.18 75.146 (max 0.965), cap 0.25 75.124; `setup_steer` 0.1 → 74.849/74.860/74.841 = 74.850, shifted 0 of 40 (max 0.966; no gain); `setup_offset` 1.0 → 74.844 (the cap is saturated, the offset no longer matters). (4) outside target kept after detection: uncapped 6 of 10 off, with cap 0.15 / road 80 2 of 10 off (flick) — lost. (5) `setup_dist` 200 with cap 0.15: 74.970 (max 0.949), at road 80 75.098 (max 0.981) — lost. |
| **Prediction** | Measured directly (`tools/suite.py` on the final code): all-30 74.833 (−0.076 s vs v0.79), 0 of 30 off, max 0.776/0.840/0.778; shifted bases 0 of 40 off. |
| **Lap time** | 1:14.72 |
| **Damage** | 0 |
| **Top speed** | 283 km/h |
| **Min speed** | 58 km/h |
| **Observed** | Run automatically (`runs/run_20261003_011448.csv`, `tools/finalize.py`); TORCS time **74.728 s** → on screen 1:14:72, **new best** (v0.79 74.860, −0.132 s; largest section changes: 500 m −0.080, 2,500 m +0.060, 1,400 m −0.044, 600 m −0.028, 1,500 m −0.026, 3,100 m −0.022). Max \|trackPos\| 0.763 at ~2,475 m (flick; v0.79 0.719); hairpin 58.4 km/h, 0.63, exit slide 14.4 km/h; flick slide 15.5, kink 8.4, largest slide elsewhere 17.0 km/h at 508 m. **Suites** (3×10): 74.841 / 74.807 / 74.850, **all-30 74.833** (v0.79 74.909), max 0.776 / 0.840 / 0.778, **0 of 30 off**. **Shifted bases** (suites 1–2): `brake_margin` 14 → 74.891/74.834, max 0.963, 0 of 20 off; `line_offset` 0.5 → 74.945/74.881, max 0.944, 0 of 20 off: **0 of 40 off**. **Pattern line** (v0.79 → v0.80): steering reversals 283 → **81**, oscillation episodes 13 → **5**, time in them 12.1 → **2.0 s**; target flips 326 → **108**; allowed-speed jumps > 20 km/h 94 → 92; brake applications 38 → 34 (short touches 22 → 15, re-applied within 10 steps 13 → 9); shifts 39 → 39 (1 undone); TC cut 11.3 → 11.9 s. Remaining episodes: 536–547 m, 1,456–1,480 m, 1,521–1,540 m, 1,901–1,916 m, 2,566–2,583 m (0.28–0.48 s each). **Width** (`tools/width.py`, entry −80 m / apex / exit +80 m, + = outside; v0.79 → v0.80): 445 m +0.13/+0.15/+0.53 → +0.20/+0.26/+0.34; 783 m +0.17/−0.06/+0.27 → +0.17/−0.09/+0.30; 1,042 m +0.27/−0.49/+0.16 → +0.40/−0.44/+0.16; 1,514 m +0.05/−0.30/−0.26 → +0.10/−0.44/−0.11; 1,927 m +0.26/−0.13/+0.08 → +0.31/−0.20/−0.02; flick −0.32/+0.71/−0.04 → −0.09/+0.61/−0.14; 2,990 m +0.12/−0.43/+0.17 → +0.17/−0.43/+0.04; hairpin +0.06/+0.64/+0.47 → +0.23/+0.62/+0.47. Entries are further outside at 7 of 8 corners (by 0.05–0.23), still far from the +0.8 aimed for. |
| **Decision** | ✅ Kept — new best lap (74.728) and all-30 74.909 → 74.833 (−0.076 s), 0 of 30 off, suite maximum 0.852 → 0.840; shifted bases 0 of 40 off; the set-up oscillation is gone (12.1 → 2.0 s in steering-oscillation episodes). The lap is 0.008 s above the 74.72 target. |
| **Learned** | The set-up wobble was the gate, as diagnosed: holding the set-up removes it and is faster, so "smoothing the set-up is slower" no longer holds on this car. But the line itself moves little: the centre-following terms answer any outward pull with 6.8 steer per radian of yaw, so a pull of 0.15 gives only ~0.02 rad and 0.05–0.2 of `trackPos` per approach; a stronger held pull (uncapped, or the 0.85 target kept after the bend is detected) reaches the outside but leaves the track at the flick, and the results between caps are not monotonic (0.12 and 0.20 off at the flick, 0.15 and 0.30 on): the flick entry, set by the kink before it, decides every line trial. The wide entry therefore needs (a) the heading and look-ahead terms referenced to the planned line instead of the track centre (path-following), not a larger pull, and (b) the flick approach (set-up at ~2,265–2,295 m ahead of the kink) handled on its own. With the cap saturated, `setup_offset` no longer acts (0.85 and 1.0: suite 1 74.841 and 74.844). |
